## Global Minimum Tax Act {#global-minimum-tax-act .Title-of-Act}

[S.C.]{.abbr title="Statutes of Canada"} 2024, c. 17, s. 81
:::

Assented to 2024-06-20

An Act respecting a global minimum tax

::: ReaderNote
\[Enacted by section 81 of chapter 17 of the Statutes of Canada, 2024,
in force on assent June 20, 2024.\]
:::
:::::

:::::::: section
## [Short Title]{.HTitleText1} {#h-1474109 .Part}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Short title

**[[1]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-1 .sectionLabel}** This Act may be cited as
the [Global Minimum Tax Act](/eng/acts/G-3.3).

## [PART 1]{.HLabel1}[Interpretation and Application]{.HTitleText1} {#h-1474112 .Part}

### [Interpretation]{.HTitleText2} {#h-1474113 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Definitions

- **[[2]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-2 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} The
  following definitions apply in this Act.

  [[acceptable financial accounting standard]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[acceptable financial accounting
      standard]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means

      - [(a)]{.lawlabel} Canadian generally accepted accounting
        principles (GAAP);

      - [(b)]{.lawlabel} IFRS; or

      - [(c)]{.lawlabel} the generally accepted accounting principles of

        - [(i)]{.lawlabel} Australia,

        - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} Brazil,

        - [(iii)]{.lawlabel} European Economic Area member states,

        - [(iv)]{.lawlabel} European Union member states,

        - [(v)]{.lawlabel} Hong Kong (Special Administrative Region of
          the People's Republic of China),

        - [(vi)]{.lawlabel} Japan,

        - [(vii)]{.lawlabel} Mexico,

        - [(viii)]{.lawlabel} New Zealand,

        - [(ix)]{.lawlabel} People's Republic of China,

        - [(x)]{.lawlabel} Republic of India,

        - [(xi)]{.lawlabel} Republic of Korea,

        - [(xii)]{.lawlabel} Singapore,

        - [(xiii)]{.lawlabel} Switzerland,

        - [(xiv)]{.lawlabel} United Kingdom, or

        - [(xv)]{.lawlabel} United States. ([norme de comptabilité
          financière admissible]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[adjusted covered taxes]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[adjusted covered taxes]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}, of a constituent
      entity of an MNE group for a fiscal year, has the same meaning as
      in subsection 22(1). ([impôts concernés ajustés]{.DefinedTermLink
      lang="fr"})

  [[adjustment year]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[adjustment year]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a fiscal year in
      respect of which an adjustment is made to the GloBE income or loss
      or adjusted covered taxes, for the fiscal year, of a constituent
      entity of an MNE group or the jurisdictional adjusted covered
      taxes of an MNE group for the fiscal year because of the
      application of an ETR adjustment provision. ([année
      d'ajustement]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[aggregate asset gain]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[aggregate asset gain]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}, in respect of an
      election under subsection 18(23), means the net gain in a fiscal
      year from the disposition of local tangible assets by all of the
      constituent entities of an MNE group that are located in a
      particular jurisdiction, excluding any gain or loss on a transfer
      of assets between group entities. ([profit cumulé sur cession
      d'actifs]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[aggregate asset loss]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[aggregate asset loss]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}, in respect of an
      election under subsection 18(23), means the net loss in a fiscal
      year from the disposition of local tangible assets by all of the
      constituent entities of an MNE group that are located in a
      particular jurisdiction, excluding any gain or loss on a transfer
      of assets between group entities. ([perte cumulée sur cession
      d'actifs]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[allocable share]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[allocable share]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} has the same meaning as in
      subsection 15(2). ([part à répartir]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[allocated adjustment top-up amount]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[allocated adjustment top-up amount]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} has the
      same meaning as in subsection 30(5). ([montant complémentaire
      d'ajustement attribué]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[ancillary international shipping activity]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[ancillary international shipping
      activity]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} has the same meaning as in
      subsection 19(11). ([activité de transport maritime international
      accessoire]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[ancillary international shipping costs]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[ancillary international shipping costs]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} has
      the same meaning as in subsection 19(10). ([coûts relatifs au
      transport maritime international accessoires]{.DefinedTermLink
      lang="fr"})

  [[ancillary international shipping income]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[ancillary international shipping
      income]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} has the same meaning as in subsection
      19(8). ([revenu de transport maritime international
      accessoire]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[ancillary international shipping revenue]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[ancillary international shipping
      revenue]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} has the same meaning as in
      subsection 19(9). ([recettes de transport maritime international
      accessoire]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[authorized financial accounting standard]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[authorized financial accounting
      standard]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}, in respect of an entity, means a
      set of generally acceptable accounting principles permitted by the
      body responsible for prescribing, establishing or accepting
      accounting standards for financial reporting purposes in the
      jurisdiction where the entity is located. ([norme de comptabilité
      financière agréée]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[blended controlled foreign company tax regime]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[blended controlled foreign company tax
      regime]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}, of a particular jurisdiction, means
      a controlled foreign company tax regime under which

      - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the tax liability of an owner located in the
        particular jurisdiction is determined by reference to an
        aggregate of the income, losses and creditable taxes of other
        entities, located in one or more other jurisdictions, in which
        the owner holds an ownership interest;

      - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the lowest rate that, if it were the corporate
        tax rate applicable in the one or more jurisdictions in which
        the other entities are located, would result in the tax charge
        in those jurisdictions being sufficient to prevent a tax charge
        on the owner under the controlled foreign company tax regime in
        respect of the other entities for a fiscal year, is less than
        the minimum rate; and

      - [(c)]{.lawlabel} income of entities located in the particular
        jurisdiction is not taken into account. ([régime fiscal intégré
        des sociétés étrangères contrôlées]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[consolidated financial statements]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[consolidated financial statements]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}, of an
      entity, means

      - [(a)]{.lawlabel} if the entity is not the ultimate parent entity
        of a group described in paragraph 10(2)(b), the financial
        statements of the entity prepared in accordance with an
        acceptable financial accounting standard, in which the assets,
        liabilities, income, expenses and cash flows of that entity and
        the entities in which it has a controlling interest are
        presented as those of a single economic unit;

      - [(b)]{.lawlabel} if the entity is the ultimate parent entity of
        a group described in paragraph 10(2)(b), the financial
        statements of the entity that are prepared in accordance with an
        acceptable financial accounting standard;

      - [(c)]{.lawlabel} if the entity has financial statements that
        would be described in paragraph (a) or (b) except that they are
        not prepared in accordance with an acceptable financial
        accounting standard, those financial statements, adjusted to
        prevent any material competitive distortions; and

      - [(d)]{.lawlabel} if the entity does not prepare financial
        statements described in any of paragraphs (a) to (c), the
        financial statements --- in which the assets, liabilities,
        income, expenses and cash flows of that entity and other
        entities are presented as those of a single economic unit ---
        that would have been prepared in accordance with an authorized
        financial accounting standard that is

        - [(i)]{.lawlabel} an acceptable financial accounting standard,
          or

        - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} a financial accounting standard that is
          adjusted to prevent any material competitive
          distortions. ([états financiers consolidés]{.DefinedTermLink
          lang="fr"})

  [[constituent entity]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[constituent entity]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} has the same meaning as
      in subsection 11(1). ([entité constitutive]{.DefinedTermLink
      lang="fr"})

  [[constituent entity-owner]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[constituent entity-owner]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a
      constituent entity that directly or indirectly holds an ownership
      interest in another constituent entity of the same MNE
      group. ([entité détentrice de titres d'une entité
      constitutive]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[controlled foreign company tax regime]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[controlled foreign company tax regime]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}, of
      a particular jurisdiction, means a set of tax rules (other than an
      IIR) under which an entity (referred to in this definition as the
      "owner") --- that is located in the particular jurisdiction and
      that holds an ownership interest in another entity (referred to in
      this definition as the "controlled foreign company") that is
      located in another jurisdiction --- is subject to current taxation
      on its share of all or part of the income earned by the controlled
      foreign company, irrespective of whether that income is
      distributed on a current basis to the owner. ([régime fiscal des
      sociétés étrangères contrôlées]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[controlling interest]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[controlling interest]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means

      - [(a)]{.lawlabel} an ownership interest in an entity such that
        the interest holder

        - [(i)]{.lawlabel} is required to consolidate the assets,
          liabilities, income, expenses and cash flows of the entity on
          a line-by-line basis in accordance with an acceptable
          financial accounting standard, or

        - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} would have been required to consolidate the
          assets, liabilities, income, expenses and cash flows of the
          entity on a line-by-line basis if the interest holder had
          prepared consolidated financial statements; or

      - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the controlling interest that a main entity is
        deemed to hold in a permanent establishment under paragraph
        (2)(b). ([participation conférant le contrôle]{.DefinedTermLink
        lang="fr"})

  [[cooperative]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[cooperative]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means an entity that 

      - [(a)]{.lawlabel} collectively markets or acquires goods or
        services on behalf of its members; and

      - [(b)]{.lawlabel} is subject to a tax regime in the jurisdiction
        in which it is located that is designed to ensure the tax
        neutrality of the cooperative in respect of the property or
        services of its members that are sold through the cooperative
        and property or services that are acquired by members through
        the cooperative. ([coopérative]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[core international shipping activity]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[core international shipping activity]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} has
      the same meaning as in subsection 19(6). ([activité de transport
      maritime international principale]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[core international shipping costs]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[core international shipping costs]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} has the
      same meaning as in subsection 19(5). ([coûts relatifs au transport
      maritime international principal]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[core international shipping income]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[core international shipping income]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} has the
      same meaning as in subsection 19(3). ([revenu de transport
      maritime international principal]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[core international shipping revenue]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[core international shipping revenue]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} has
      the same meaning as in subsection 19(4). ([recettes de transport
      maritime international principal]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[corporation]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[corporation]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} includes an incorporated
      company, arrangement, association, organization or
      body. ([société]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[covered taxes]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[covered taxes]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} has the same meaning as in
      subsection 23(1). ([impôts concernés]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[deductible dividend]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[deductible dividend]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}, in respect of a
      constituent entity that is subject to a deductible dividend
      regime, means

      - [(a)]{.lawlabel} a distribution of profits, by the constituent
        entity to the holder of an ownership interest in the constituent
        entity, that is deductible from income for tax purposes of the
        constituent entity under the laws of the jurisdiction in which
        it is located; or

      - [(b)]{.lawlabel} a patronage dividend distributed to a member of
        a cooperative. ([dividende déductible]{.DefinedTermLink
        lang="fr"})

  [[deductible dividend regime]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[deductible dividend regime]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a tax
      regime that

      - [(a)]{.lawlabel} is designed to yield a single level of
        taxation, on the owners of an entity, through a deduction from
        the income of the entity for distributions of profits to the
        owners (which, for this purpose, are considered to include
        patronage dividends); or

      - [(b)]{.lawlabel} is applicable to cooperatives and exempts the
        cooperative from taxation. ([régime des dividendes
        déductibles]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[deemed distribution tax]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[deemed distribution tax]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} has the same
      meaning as in subsection 37(2). ([impôt sur les distributions
      présumées]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[deemed distribution tax recapture account]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[deemed distribution tax recapture
      account]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means an account established and
      maintained in accordance with subsection 37(1). ([compte de
      récupération de l'impôt sur les distributions
      présumées]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[departing constituent entity]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[departing constituent entity]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}, of an MNE
      group, means an entity that is a standard constituent entity of
      the MNE group located in a jurisdiction in a fiscal year in
      respect of which an election is made under subsection 37(1) and
      that has subsequently

      - [(a)]{.lawlabel} left the MNE group; or

      - [(b)]{.lawlabel} transferred substantially all of its assets to
        a recipient that is not, at the time of the transfer, a standard
        constituent entity of the MNE group located in the
        jurisdiction. ([entité constitutive sortante]{.DefinedTermLink
        lang="fr"})

  [[disallowed accrual]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[disallowed accrual]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}, of a constituent
      entity, means any movement in deferred tax expense recorded in the
      constituent entity's financial accounts that relates to

      - [(a)]{.lawlabel} an uncertain tax position; or

      - [(b)]{.lawlabel} a distribution from a group entity in respect
        of the constituent entity. ([accumulation non
        admise]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[disposition recapture ratio]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[disposition recapture ratio]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} has the same
      meaning as in subsection 37(6). ([ratio de récupération de
      disposition]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[disqualified refundable imputation tax]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[disqualified refundable imputation
      tax]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means an amount of tax paid or accrued
      by a constituent entity (other than an amount of tax paid or
      accrued that is a qualified imputation tax) that is

      - [(a)]{.lawlabel} refundable to the beneficial owner of a
        dividend or similar distribution by the constituent entity;

      - [(b)]{.lawlabel} creditable against a tax liability (other than
        a tax liability in respect of the distribution) of the
        beneficial owner; or

      - [(c)]{.lawlabel} refundable to, or creditable against a tax
        liability of, the constituent entity, in respect of the
        distribution. ([impôt d'imputation remboursable non
        admissible]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[domestic top-up amount]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[domestic top-up amount]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} has the same
      meaning as in subsection 52(1). ([montant complémentaire
      national]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[dual-listed arrangement]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[dual-listed arrangement]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means an
      arrangement entered into by the ultimate parent entities of two or
      more groups, under which

      - [(a)]{.lawlabel} all or substantially all the businesses of the
        groups are combined by contract alone;

      - [(b)]{.lawlabel} pursuant to contractual arrangements, the
        ultimate parent entities each make distributions (including
        dividends and distributions on a liquidation, if relevant) to
        their respective owners on the basis of a fixed ratio;

      - [(c)]{.lawlabel} the activities of the groups are managed as
        those of a single economic unit;

      - [(d)]{.lawlabel} the groups retain the separate legal identity
        they had before the arrangement was entered into;

      - [(e)]{.lawlabel} ownership interests of at least one of the
        ultimate parent entities are quoted, traded or transferred
        independently on a securities market that is not the same as the
        securities market on which the ownership interests of another of
        the ultimate parent entities are quoted, traded or transferred
        independently; and

      - [(f)]{.lawlabel} financial statements are prepared by one or
        more of the ultimate parent entities in which the assets,
        liabilities, income, expenses and cash flows of the groups'
        entities are presented together as those of a single economic
        unit and those financial statements

        - [(i)]{.lawlabel} would be consolidated financial statements if
          the groups' entities were included in a single group and the
          ultimate parent entity that prepared the financial statements
          were the ultimate parent entity of that group, and

        - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} are required by an applicable regulatory
          regime of a jurisdiction to be externally audited. ([accord de
          double cotation]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[effective tax rate]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[effective tax rate]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} has the same meaning as
      in subsection 29(1). ([taux effectif
      d'imposition]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[eligible distribution tax system]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[eligible distribution tax system]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}, of a
      jurisdiction, means a corporate income tax system that is
      applicable in the jurisdiction that

      - [(a)]{.lawlabel} generally imposes tax on the profits, or
        certain non-business expenses, of a corporation only when the
        corporation

        - [(i)]{.lawlabel} distributes those profits to its
          shareholders,

        - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} is deemed to distribute those profits to its
          shareholders, or

        - [(iii)]{.lawlabel} incurs those expenses;

      - [(b)]{.lawlabel} imposes tax at a rate greater than or equal to
        the minimum rate; and

      - [(c)]{.lawlabel} was in force on or before July 1,
        2021. ([régime admissible d'impôt sur les
        distributions]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[eligible employee]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[eligible employee]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} has the same meaning as
      in subsection 32(8). ([employé admissible]{.DefinedTermLink
      lang="fr"})

  [[eligible payroll costs]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[eligible payroll costs]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} has the same
      meaning as in subsection 32(2). ([frais de personnel
      admissibles]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[eligible tangible asset]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[eligible tangible asset]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} has the same
      meaning as in subsection 32(14). ([actif corporel
      admissible]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[eligible tangible asset amount]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[eligible tangible asset amount]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} has the
      same meaning as in subsection 32(9). ([montant de l'actif corporel
      admissible]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[entity]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[entity]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a corporation, a partnership,
      a trust or any other arrangement, association, organization or
      body whether registered or unregistered for which separate
      financial accounts are prepared, but does not include a central,
      state or local government or its administration or agencies that
      carries out government functions. ([entité]{.DefinedTermLink
      lang="fr"})

  [[ETR adjustment provision]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[ETR adjustment provision]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means paragraph
      18(23)(c), subsection 25(6), paragraph 27(1)(b), subsection 27(4)
      or paragraph 37(4)(b) or (5)(b). ([disposition d'ajustement du
      TEI]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[excess negative tax expense]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[excess negative tax expense]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} has the same
      meaning as in subsection 29(4). ([charge d'impôt négative
      excédentaire]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[excess negative tax expense top-up amount]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[excess negative tax expense top-up
      amount]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} has the same meaning as in subsection
      31(2). ([montant complémentaire de la charge d'impôt négative
      excédentaire]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[excess profit]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[excess profit]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} has the same meaning as in
      subsection 30(4). ([bénéfice excédentaire]{.DefinedTermLink
      lang="fr"})

  [[excluded costs]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[excluded costs]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} has the same meaning as in
      subsection 32(3). ([coûts exclus]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[excluded dividends]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[excluded dividends]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means dividends or
      other distributions received or accrued by a constituent entity in
      respect of an ownership interest in an entity, other than

      - [(a)]{.lawlabel} a dividend or other distribution received or
        accrued in respect of

        - [(i)]{.lawlabel} an interest that is a short-term portfolio
          holding of the constituent entity on the earlier of

          - [(A)]{.lawlabel} the date of the distribution, and

          - [(B)]{.lawlabel} the date on which the constituent entity
            becomes entitled to the distribution, or

        - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} an ownership interest in an investment
          entity that is subject to an election under subsection 42(2);
          or

      - [(b)]{.lawlabel} a dividend or other distribution received or
        accrued

        - [(i)]{.lawlabel} to the extent that it can reasonably be
          considered to be interest or another amount in respect of a
          debt component of a financial instrument, or

        - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} if it is

          - [(A)]{.lawlabel} made by another group entity, and

          - [(B)]{.lawlabel} treated as an expense for the purposes of
            determining the financial accounting income of the other
            group entity. ([dividendes exclus]{.DefinedTermLink
            lang="fr"})

  [[excluded entity]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[excluded entity]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} has the same meaning as in
      subsection 13(1). ([entité exclue]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[excluded equity gain or loss]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[excluded equity gain or loss]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}, of a
      constituent entity for a fiscal year, means the gain, profit or
      loss included in the financial accounting income of the
      constituent entity for the fiscal year arising from

      - [(a)]{.lawlabel} gains and losses from changes in the fair value
        of an ownership interest, or from the impairment of an ownership
        interest, except for an interest that is a portfolio holding at
        the end of the fiscal year;

      - [(b)]{.lawlabel} profit or loss in respect of an ownership
        interest included under the equity method of accounting; or

      - [(c)]{.lawlabel} gains and losses from a disposition of an
        ownership interest, except for a disposition of an interest that
        is a portfolio holding immediately before the
        disposition. ([profit ou perte sur capitaux propres
        exclu]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[excluded taxes]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[excluded taxes]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} has the same meaning as in
      subsection 23(2). ([impôts exclus]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[filing constituent entity]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[filing constituent entity]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means

      - [(a)]{.lawlabel} in respect of a qualifying MNE group for a
        fiscal year, a constituent entity of the MNE group that is

        - [(i)]{.lawlabel} located in Canada and required to file a GIR
          with the Minister, in respect of the MNE group for the fiscal
          year, in accordance with subsection 60(1) or 60(2),

        - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} a designated local entity (as appointed
          under paragraph 60(3)(a)) for the fiscal year, or

        - [(iii)]{.lawlabel} a [[qualifying foreign filing
          entity]{.dfn}]{.DefinitionRef}, as defined in subsection
          55(1), for the fiscal year; and

      - [(b)]{.lawlabel} in respect of an investment subgroup, joint
        venture group, minority-owned subgroup or minority-owned
        investment subgroup that is included in a qualifying MNE group
        for a fiscal year, the constituent entity that files the GIR in
        respect of the MNE group for the fiscal year. ([entité
        constitutive déclarante]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[financial accounting income]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[financial accounting income]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} has the same
      meaning as in subsection 17(1). ([résultat net
      comptable]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[financial accounting revenue]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[financial accounting revenue]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} has the same
      meaning as in subsection 33(3). ([chiffre d'affaires
      comptable]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[fiscal year]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[fiscal year]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}, in respect of an MNE group,
      means

      - [(a)]{.lawlabel} if paragraph (d) of the definition
        [[consolidated financial statements]{.dfn}]{.DefinitionRef} in
        this subsection applies in respect of the ultimate parent
        entity, the calendar year; and

      - [(b)]{.lawlabel} in any other case, an accounting period for
        which the ultimate parent entity of an MNE group prepares its
        consolidated financial statements. ([année
        financière]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[fiscally transparent]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[fiscally transparent]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}, in respect of an
      entity, means that under the laws of a jurisdiction, the income,
      expenditure, profit or loss of the entity is treated as if it were
      derived or incurred by the owner of the entity in proportion to
      the owner's interest in the entity. ([fiscalement
      transparent]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[five-year election]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[five-year election]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means an election, in
      respect of a particular fiscal year, to which the following rules
      apply:

      - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the election applies to the particular fiscal
        year and, unless the election is revoked, each subsequent fiscal
        year;

      - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the election cannot be revoked before the fifth
        fiscal year following the particular fiscal year;

      - [(c)]{.lawlabel} if the election is revoked, no such election
        can subsequently be made until the fifth fiscal year following
        the revocation year; and

      - [(d)]{.lawlabel} the election or any revocation of the election,
        as the case may be, must be made in the GIR for the first fiscal
        year in respect of which the election or revocation, as the case
        may be, is to be effective. ([choix pour cinq
        ans]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[flow-through entity]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[flow-through entity]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means an entity to the
      extent it

      - [(a)]{.lawlabel} is fiscally transparent under the laws of the
        jurisdiction where it was created; and

      - [(b)]{.lawlabel} is not subject to a covered tax on its income
        or profit in another jurisdiction as a result of being tax
        resident in that jurisdiction. ([entité
        intermédiaire]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[flow-through tax benefits]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[flow-through tax benefits]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} has the same
      meaning as in subsection 28(1). ([avantages fiscaux
      intermédiaires]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[general government]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[general government]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means the central
      administration, agencies whose operations are under its effective
      control, state and local governments and their respective
      administrations. ([administration publique]{.DefinedTermLink
      lang="fr"})

  [[GIR]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[GIR]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means an information return that
      contains the information, and is disseminated, in accordance with
      the Tax Challenges Arising from the Digitalisation of the Economy
      --- GloBE Information Return (Pillar Two) published by the OECD on
      July 13, 2023, as amended from time to time, and that is

      - [(a)]{.lawlabel} if required to be filed with the Minister by a
        constituent entity located in Canada under subsection 60(1) or
        60(2) or subparagraph 60(3)(b)(i), the information return in
        prescribed form; and

      - [(b)]{.lawlabel} in any other case, a substantially similar
        information return that is required to be filed in a
        jurisdiction other than Canada. ([DRG]{.DefinedTermLink
        lang="fr"})

  [[GloBE Commentary]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[GloBE Commentary]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means the commentary
      entitled Tax Challenges Arising from the Digitalisation of the
      Economy --- Commentary to the Global Anti-Base Erosion Model Rules
      (Pillar Two), published by the OECD on March 14, 2022, as amended
      from time to time. ([commentaires GloBE]{.DefinedTermLink
      lang="fr"})

  [[GloBE income]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[GloBE income]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}, of a constituent entity for
      a fiscal year, means the positive amount, if any, of the
      constituent entity's GloBE income or loss for the fiscal
      year. ([revenu GloBE]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[GloBE income or loss]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[GloBE income or loss]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} has the meaning
      assigned in section 16. ([résultat net GloBE]{.DefinedTermLink
      lang="fr"})

  [[GloBE loss]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[GloBE loss]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}, of a constituent entity for a
      fiscal year, means the absolute value of the negative amount, if
      any, of the constituent entity's GloBE income or loss for the
      fiscal year. ([perte GloBE]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[GloBE Model Rules]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[GloBE Model Rules]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means the model rules
      set out in the document entitled Tax Challenges Arising from the
      Digitalisation of the Economy --- Global Anti-Base Erosion Model
      Rules (Pillar Two) published by the OECD on December 20,
      2021. ([règles GloBE]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[GloBE reorganization]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[GloBE reorganization]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a
      transformation or transfer of assets and liabilities such as in a
      merger, demerger, liquidation or similar transaction, if

      - [(a)]{.lawlabel} it is the case that

        - [(i)]{.lawlabel} if no consideration is provided for the
          transfer, the issuance of an equity interest as consideration
          for the transfer would have no economic significance because
          the transaction does not result in a change in the beneficial
          ownership of an entity, and

        - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} in any other case, the consideration for the
          transfer consists, in whole or in significant part, of

          - [(A)]{.lawlabel} if the transaction is a liquidation, the
            cancellation of equity interests of the entity that is the
            subject of the liquidation, and

          - [(B)]{.lawlabel} in any other case, equity interests issued
            by the transferee or an entity that is connected with the
            transferee;

      - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the transferor's gain or loss on the transfer
        is not subject, in whole or in part, to an income or profits tax
        in any jurisdiction; and

      - [(c)]{.lawlabel} for the purpose of determining the transferee's
        income or profits that are subject to an income or profits tax
        of the jurisdiction in which it is located, the transferee is
        required under the laws of that jurisdiction to use the
        transferor's tax basis in respect of the transferred assets,
        adjusted to reflect any non-qualifying gain or loss in respect
        of the transfer. ([réorganisation GloBE]{.DefinedTermLink
        lang="fr"})

  [[GloBE transition year]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[GloBE transition year]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}, of an MNE group in
      respect of a jurisdiction, means the first fiscal year in which

      - [(a)]{.lawlabel} a constituent entity of, or joint venture
        entity in respect of, the MNE group that is located in the
        jurisdiction is subject to a qualified IIR or qualified UTPR;
        and

      - [(b)]{.lawlabel} subsection 33(1) or 47(2), section 45 or an
        equivalent provision under another qualified IIR or qualified
        UTPR does not apply for the jurisdiction. ([année de transition
        GloBE]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[governmental entity]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[governmental entity]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means an entity, if

      - [(a)]{.lawlabel} it is part of or wholly owned by a government
        (including any political subdivision or local authority of the
        government);

      - [(b)]{.lawlabel} its principal purpose is

        - [(i)]{.lawlabel} to fulfill a government function, or

        - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} to manage or invest the government's assets
          through the making and holding of investments, asset
          management and related investment activities for the
          government's assets;

      - [(c)]{.lawlabel} it does not carry on a trade or business;

      - [(d)]{.lawlabel} it is accountable to the government on its
        overall performance and provides annual information reporting to
        the government;

      - [(e)]{.lawlabel} its assets vest in the government upon
        dissolution; and

      - [(f)]{.lawlabel} if it distributes net earnings, the net
        earnings are distributed solely to the government with no
        portion of its net earnings inuring to the benefit of any
        private person. ([entité gouvernementale]{.DefinedTermLink
        lang="fr"})

  [[group]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[group]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} has the same meaning as in
      subsection 10(2). ([groupe]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[group entity]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[group entity]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}, in respect of another entity
      or a group, means an entity that is a member of the same
      group. ([entité du groupe]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[high-tax counterparty]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[high-tax counterparty]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a constituent
      entity of an MNE group that is located in a jurisdiction that

      - [(a)]{.lawlabel} is not a low-tax jurisdiction; or

      - [(b)]{.lawlabel} would not be a low-tax jurisdiction if the
        effective tax rate of the MNE group for the jurisdiction were
        determined without regard to any income or expense recorded by
        an entity in respect of an intragroup financing
        arrangement. ([contrepartie à imposition
        élevée]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[hybrid entity]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[hybrid entity]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}, in relation to an owner,
      means an entity that is

      - [(a)]{.lawlabel} not fiscally transparent in the jurisdiction
        where it is located; and

      - [(b)]{.lawlabel} fiscally transparent in the jurisdiction where
        the owner is located. ([entité hybride]{.DefinedTermLink
        lang="fr"})

  [[IFRS]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[IFRS]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means International Financial
      Reporting Standards. ([IFRS]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[IIR]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[IIR]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a law of a jurisdiction that may
      reasonably be considered to have been enacted with the intention
      of implementing, in whole or in part, Articles 2.1 to 2.3 of the
      GloBE Model Rules. ([RDIR]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[included revaluation method gain or loss]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[included revaluation method gain or loss]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm},
      of a constituent entity for a fiscal year, means the net gain or
      loss, before covered taxes, for the fiscal year in respect of all
      property, plant and equipment that arises under an accounting
      method or practice that

      - [(a)]{.lawlabel} periodically adjusts the carrying value of such
        property, plant and equipment to its fair value;

      - [(b)]{.lawlabel} records the changes in value in other
        comprehensive income; and

      - [(c)]{.lawlabel} does not subsequently report the gains or
        losses recorded in other comprehensive income through profit and
        loss. ([profit ou perte inclus au titre de la méthode de
        réévaluation]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[inclusion ratio]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[inclusion ratio]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} has the same meaning as in
      subsection 15(3). ([ratio d'inclusion]{.DefinedTermLink
      lang="fr"})

  [[Inclusive Framework]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[Inclusive Framework]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means the
      jurisdictions of the OECD and Group of 20 (G20) Inclusive
      Framework on Base Erosion and Profit Shifting. ([Cadre
      inclusif]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[insurance investment entity]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[insurance investment entity]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a
      particular entity, other than an investment fund or real estate
      investment vehicle,

      - [(a)]{.lawlabel} that would be

        - [(i)]{.lawlabel} an investment fund if the definition
          [[investment fund]{.dfn}]{.DefinitionRef} in this subsection
          were read without reference to subparagraph (a)(i) and
          paragraphs (c) and (d) of that definition, or

        - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} a real estate investment vehicle if the
          definition [[real estate investment
          vehicle]{.dfn}]{.DefinitionRef} in this subsection were read
          without reference to subparagraph (b)(ii) of that definition;

      - [(b)]{.lawlabel} that is primarily designed or established to
        generate investment income or gains to offset, or protect
        against an event or outcome that gives rise to, liabilities or
        losses associated with insurance or annuity contracts; and

      - [(c)]{.lawlabel} in which each ownership interest is held by one
        or more entities each of which

        - [(i)]{.lawlabel} is a group entity in respect of the
          particular entity, and

        - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} is subject to a regulatory regime, in the
          jurisdiction in which it is established or managed, that is
          specific to entities engaged in the business of negotiating
          and entering into contracts of insurance or annuity or in
          activities ancillary to that business. ([entité
          d'investissement d'assurance]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[intermediate parent entity]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[intermediate parent entity]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a
      constituent entity of an MNE group (other than an ultimate parent
      entity, partially-owned parent entity, permanent establishment,
      investment entity or insurance investment entity) that holds,
      directly or indirectly, an ownership interest in another
      constituent entity of the MNE group. ([entité mère
      intermédiaire]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[international organization]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[international organization]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means any
      intergovernmental or supranational organization, or an entity that
      acts for, is part of or is wholly owned by that organization, if

      - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the organization is comprised primarily of
        governments;

      - [(b)]{.lawlabel} it has in effect a headquarters agreement or
        substantially similar agreement, such as arrangements for
        privileges or immunities in respect of its offices or
        establishments, with the jurisdiction in which it is
        established; and

      - [(c)]{.lawlabel} law or its governing documents prevent its
        income inuring to the benefit of private persons. ([organisation
        internationale]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[international shipping]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[international shipping]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means the
      transportation of passengers or cargo by ship in international
      traffic. ([transport maritime international]{.DefinedTermLink
      lang="fr"})

  [[international traffic]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[international traffic]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means any transport
      by ship, except when the ship is operated solely between places
      within a single jurisdiction. ([trafic
      international]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[intragroup financing arrangement]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[intragroup financing arrangement]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means any
      arrangement between two or more group entities of an MNE group
      under which a high-tax counterparty directly or indirectly
      provides credit to or otherwise makes an investment in a low-tax
      entity. ([accord de financement intragroupe]{.DefinedTermLink
      lang="fr"})

  [[investment entity]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[investment entity]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}, for a fiscal year,
      means

      - [(a)]{.lawlabel} an entity that is an investment fund for all or
        substantially all of the fiscal year;

      - [(b)]{.lawlabel} an entity that is a real estate investment
        vehicle for all or substantially all of the fiscal year;

      - [(c)]{.lawlabel} a particular entity, if

        - [(i)]{.lawlabel} for all or substantially all of the fiscal
          year ownership interests in the particular entity having a
          fair market value equal to at least 95% of the fair market
          value of all ownership interests in the particular entity are
          held directly, or indirectly through one or more other
          entities that are investment funds or real estate investment
          vehicles, by one or more entities that are investment funds or
          real estate investment vehicles, and

        - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} all or substantially all the activities of
          the particular entity during the fiscal year consist of
          holding assets or investing funds for the benefit of any of
          the investment funds or real estate investment vehicles
          referred to in subparagraph (i); or

      - [(d)]{.lawlabel} a particular entity, if

        - [(i)]{.lawlabel} for all or substantially all of the fiscal
          year ownership interests in the particular entity having a
          fair market value equal to at least 85% of the fair market
          value of all ownership interests in the particular entity are
          held directly, or indirectly through one or more other
          entities that are investment funds or real estate investment
          vehicles, by one or more entities that are investment funds or
          real estate investment vehicles, and

        - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} all or substantially all the financial
          accounting income of the particular entity is comprised of
          excluded dividends or excluded equity gains or losses.
          ([entité d'investissement]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[investment fund]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[investment fund]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means an entity

      - [(a)]{.lawlabel} that is primarily designed or established to

        - [(i)]{.lawlabel} pool assets (which may be financial or
          non-financial) from a number of investors, at least some of
          which are not connected, and

        - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} generate investment income or gains, or
          protect against a specific or general event or outcome;

      - [(b)]{.lawlabel} that invests in accordance with a defined
        investment policy;

      - [(c)]{.lawlabel} that allows investors to reduce transaction,
        research and analytical costs or to spread risk collectively;

      - [(d)]{.lawlabel} the investors in which have a right to the
        assets of the entity, or income earned on those assets, based on
        the respective contributions made by those investors;

      - [(e)]{.lawlabel} that is, or whose management is, subject to a
        regulatory regime (which may include appropriate anti-money
        laundering and investor protection regulations) in the
        jurisdiction in which the entity is established or managed; and

      - [(f)]{.lawlabel} that is managed by investment management
        professionals on behalf of its investors. ([fonds de
        placement]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[joint venture]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[joint venture]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}, in respect of a particular
      MNE group (other than a group composed exclusively of excluded
      entities), means a particular entity

      - [(a)]{.lawlabel} whose financial results are reported under the
        equity method in the consolidated financial statements of the
        ultimate parent entity of the particular MNE group;

      - [(b)]{.lawlabel} of which at least 50% of the ownership
        interests are held, directly or indirectly, by the ultimate
        parent entity of the particular MNE group; and

      - [(c)]{.lawlabel} that is not

        - [(i)]{.lawlabel} the ultimate parent entity of an MNE group, a
          constituent entity of which, or joint venture in respect of
          which, is subject to a qualified IIR or qualified UTPR,

        - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} an [[excluded entity]{.dfn}]{.DefinitionRef}
          described in paragraph 13(1)(a),

        - [(iii)]{.lawlabel} an entity, if

          - [(A)]{.lawlabel} all ownership interests in that entity held
            by group entities in respect of the particular MNE group are
            held directly by [[excluded entities]{.dfn}]{.DefinitionRef}
            described in paragraph 13(1)(a), and

          - [(B)]{.lawlabel} one of the following conditions is met:

            - [(I)]{.lawlabel} the entity operates exclusively or almost
              exclusively to hold assets or invest funds for the benefit
              of its investors,

            - [(II)]{.lawlabel} the entity carries out activities that
              are ancillary to those carried out by any of those group
              entities, or

            - [(III)]{.lawlabel} all or substantially all of the
              financial accounting income of the entity is excluded
              dividends or excluded equity gains, or

        - [(iv)]{.lawlabel} a joint venture subsidiary of another joint
          venture. ([coentreprise]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[joint venture entity]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[joint venture entity]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}, of a joint venture
      group, means the joint venture or a joint venture subsidiary of
      the group. ([entité d'une coentreprise]{.DefinedTermLink
      lang="fr"})

  [[joint venture group]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[joint venture group]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a joint venture
      and its joint venture subsidiaries, if any. ([groupe d'une
      coentreprise]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[joint venture subsidiary]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[joint venture subsidiary]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}, of a joint
      venture, means an entity (other than the joint venture or an
      excluded entity) the assets, liabilities, income, expenses and
      cash flows of which would, because of ownership or control, be
      included in the consolidated financial statements of the joint
      venture if the references to "ultimate parent entity" in the
      definition [[consolidated financial
      statements]{.dfn}]{.DefinitionRef} in this subsection were read as
      references to the "joint venture". ([filiale d'une
      coentreprise]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[jurisdictional adjusted covered taxes]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[jurisdictional adjusted covered taxes]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} has
      the same meaning as in subsection 29(3). ([impôts concernés
      ajustés juridictionnels]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[jurisdictional excess negative tax expense top-up amount]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[jurisdictional excess negative tax expense top-up
      amount]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} has the same meaning as in subsection
      31(4). ([montant complémentaire de la charge d'impôt négative
      excédentaire juridictionnel]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[jurisdictional GloBE income or loss]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[jurisdictional GloBE income or loss]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} has
      the same meaning as in subsection 33(4). ([résultat net GloBE
      juridictionnel]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[jurisdictional GloBE revenue]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[jurisdictional GloBE revenue]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} has the same
      meaning as in subsection 33(2). ([chiffre d'affaires GloBE
      juridictionnel]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[jurisdictional top-up amount]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[jurisdictional top-up amount]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} has the same
      meaning as in subsection 30(2). ([montant complémentaire
      juridictionnel]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[local tangible asset]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[local tangible asset]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}, in respect of a
      constituent entity, means real or immovable property situated in
      the same jurisdiction as the jurisdiction where the constituent
      entity is located. ([actif corporel local]{.DefinedTermLink
      lang="fr"})

  [[local taxation year]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[local taxation year]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} of an entity means the
      period for which the accounts of the entity have been ordinarily
      made up for the purpose of computing the entity's income for tax
      purposes in the jurisdiction in which the entity is
      located. ([année d'imposition locale]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[look-back period]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[look-back period]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}, in respect of an
      election under subsection 18(23), means the election year and the
      four fiscal years immediately preceding the election
      year. ([période antérieure concernée]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[loss year]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[loss year]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}, in respect of a jurisdiction
      for which the filing constituent entity of an MNE group has made
      an election under subsection 18(23), means a fiscal year in the
      look-back period for which a constituent entity of the MNE group
      that is located in that jurisdiction has a net asset loss and the
      total amount of net asset losses of all constituent entities of
      the MNE group that are located in that jurisdiction exceeds the
      total amount of their net asset gains. ([année de
      perte]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[low-tax entity]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[low-tax entity]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a constituent entity
      of an MNE group that is located in

      - [(a)]{.lawlabel} a low-tax jurisdiction; or

      - [(b)]{.lawlabel} a jurisdiction that would be a low-tax
        jurisdiction if the effective tax rate of the MNE group for the
        jurisdiction were determined without regard to any income or
        expense recorded by an entity in respect of an intragroup
        financing arrangement. ([entité à faible
        imposition]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[low-tax jurisdiction]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[low-tax jurisdiction]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}, in respect of an MNE
      group for a fiscal year, means a jurisdiction where the MNE group
      has net GloBE income and the effective tax rate of the MNE group
      for the jurisdiction for that fiscal year is lower than the
      minimum rate. ([juridiction à faible imposition]{.DefinedTermLink
      lang="fr"})

  [[main entity]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[main entity]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}, in respect of a permanent
      establishment, means the entity that includes the financial
      accounting income of the permanent establishment in its financial
      statements. ([entité principale]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[marketable price floor]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[marketable price floor]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}, in respect of the
      transfer of a tax credit, means 80% of the net present value of
      the tax credit, where the net present value is determined based on
      the yield to maturity on a debt instrument with equal or similar
      maturity --- limited to a maximum of five-year maturity --- issued
      by the government that granted the tax credit in the same fiscal
      year as the tax credit is transferred or, if it is not
      transferred, the origination year. ([prix plancher
      commercialisable]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[marketable transferable tax credit]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[marketable transferable tax credit]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means
      all or a portion of a tax credit (other than a qualified
      refundable tax credit) if

      - [(a)]{.lawlabel} in respect of an originator,

        - [(i)]{.lawlabel} the tax credit, or portion of the tax credit,
          is transferable to an unrelated purchaser in the origination
          year, or within 15 months of the end of the origination year,
          under the laws of the jurisdiction granting the tax credit,
          and

        - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} one of the following conditions is met:

          - [(A)]{.lawlabel} the tax credit, or portion of the tax
            credit, is transferred to an unrelated purchaser within 15
            months of the end of the origination year at a price that
            equals or exceeds the marketable price floor, or

          - [(B)]{.lawlabel} if the tax credit, or portion of the tax
            credit, is not transferred, or is transferred to a person
            that is not an unrelated purchaser, similar tax credits are
            traded between persons and unrelated purchasers within 15
            months of the end of the origination year at a price that
            equals or exceeds the marketable price floor; and

      - [(b)]{.lawlabel} in respect of a particular unrelated purchaser,

        - [(i)]{.lawlabel} the tax credit, or portion of the tax credit,
          is transferable from the particular unrelated purchaser to
          another unrelated purchaser in the fiscal year in which the
          tax credit, or portion of the tax credit, is purchased by the
          particular unrelated purchaser under the laws of the
          jurisdiction granting the tax credit, and

        - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} the tax credit, or portion of the tax
          credit, is acquired by the particular unrelated purchaser at a
          price that equals or exceeds the marketable price floor.
          ([crédit d'impôt transférable
          commercialisable]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[material competitive distortion]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[material competitive distortion]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}, in
      respect of consolidated financial statements, means an application
      of a specific principle or procedure, under the set of generally
      accepted accounting principles used in preparing the consolidated
      financial statements, that results in an aggregate variation
      greater than €75 million in a fiscal year as compared to the
      amounts that would have been determined by applying the
      corresponding IFRS principle or procedure.  ([distorsion
      importante créant un avantage concurrentiel]{.DefinedTermLink
      lang="fr"})

  [[minimum rate]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[minimum rate]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means 15%. ([taux
      minimum]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[Minister]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[Minister]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means the Minister of National
      Revenue. ([ministre]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[minority-owned constituent entity]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[minority-owned constituent entity]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a
      constituent entity of an MNE group if the ultimate parent entity
      of the MNE group holds, directly or indirectly, 30% or less of the
      ownership interests in the constituent entity. ([entité
      constitutive à détention minoritaire]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[minority-owned parent entity]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[minority-owned parent entity]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a
      particular minority-owned constituent entity that holds, directly
      or indirectly, the controlling interest in another minority-owned
      constituent entity unless the controlling interest of the
      particular minority-owned constituent entity is held, directly or
      indirectly, by another minority-owned constituent entity. ([entité
      mère à détention minoritaire]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[minority-owned subgroup]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[minority-owned subgroup]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means 

      - [(a)]{.lawlabel} a minority-owned parent entity and its
        minority-owned subsidiaries; or

      - [(b)]{.lawlabel} a minority-owned constituent entity that is not
        a minority-owned parent entity or a minority-owned
        subsidiary. ([sous-groupe à détention
        minoritaire]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[minority-owned subsidiary]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[minority-owned subsidiary]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a
      minority-owned constituent entity the controlling interest in
      which is held, directly or indirectly, by a minority-owned parent
      entity. ([filiale à détention minoritaire]{.DefinedTermLink
      lang="fr"})

  [[MNE group]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[MNE group]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} has the same meaning as in
      subsection 10(1). ([groupe d'EMN]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[multi-parented MNE group]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[multi-parented MNE group]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means two or more
      groups, if

      - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the ultimate parent entities of the groups are
        party to a stapled structure or dual-listed arrangement; and

      - [(b)]{.lawlabel} at least one entity or permanent establishment
        included in any of the groups --- or at least one entity, or
        permanent establishment in respect of a main entity, that would
        be included in any of the groups if all the ownership interests
        in that entity or main entity held by group entities of the
        groups were held by a single group entity of one of the groups
        --- is not located in the jurisdiction in which any other entity
        or permanent establishment included in any of the groups is
        located. ([groupe d'EMN à entités mères
        multiples]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[mutual insurance company]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[mutual insurance company]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a regulated
      insurance company that does not have share capital and is owned
      exclusively by its policyholders. ([compagnie d'assurance
      mutuelle]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[negative tax expense constituent entity]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[negative tax expense constituent
      entity]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} has the same meaning as in subsection
      31(3). ([entité constitutive avec charge d'impôt
      négative]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[net asset gain]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[net asset gain]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}, of a constituent entity
      for a fiscal year, in respect of an election under subsection
      18(23), means the net gain from the disposition of local tangible
      assets by the constituent entity located in the jurisdiction for
      which the election was made, excluding the gain or loss on a
      transfer of assets to another group entity. ([profit net sur
      cession d'actifs]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[net asset loss]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[net asset loss]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}, of a constituent entity
      for a fiscal year, in respect of an election under subsection
      18(23), means the net loss from the disposition of local tangible
      assets by the constituent entity in the fiscal year (excluding the
      gain or loss on a transfer of assets to another group entity),
      less the total of all amounts, if any, allocated to the
      constituent entity under a previous election under that
      subsection. ([perte nette sur cession d'actifs]{.DefinedTermLink
      lang="fr"})

  [[net GloBE income]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[net GloBE income]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} has the same meaning as
      in subsection 29(2). ([revenu GloBE net]{.DefinedTermLink
      lang="fr"})

  [[net GloBE loss]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[net GloBE loss]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}, of an MNE group for a
      jurisdiction for a fiscal year, means the absolute value of the
      negative amount, if any, that would be determined, in the absence
      of section 8, by the formula

      ::: {#1474398 .Definition}
      A − B

      where

      [A]{.dfn}
      :   is the total of all amounts each of which is the GloBE income
          for the fiscal year of a standard constituent entity of the
          MNE group that is located in the jurisdiction; and

      [B]{.dfn}
      :   is the total of all amounts each of which is the GloBE loss
          for the fiscal year of a standard constituent entity of the
          MNE group that is located in the jurisdiction. ([perte GloBE
          nette]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})
      :::

  [[net income or loss from international shipping]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[net income or loss from international
      shipping]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} has the same meaning as in
      subsection 19(2). ([résultat net de transport maritime
      international]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[non-marketable transferable tax credit]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[non-marketable transferable tax
      credit]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means all or a portion of a tax
      credit (other than a qualified refundable tax credit) that

      - [(a)]{.lawlabel} in respect of an originator, is not a
        marketable transferable tax credit but that is transferable in
        whole or in part; and

      - [(b)]{.lawlabel} in respect of a purchaser, is not a marketable
        transferable tax credit. ([crédit d'impôt transférable non
        commercialisable]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[non-profit organization]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[non-profit organization]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means an entity,
      if

      - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the entity is established and operated in the
        jurisdiction where it is located

        - [(i)]{.lawlabel} exclusively for religious, charitable,
          scientific, artistic, cultural, athletic, educational or other
          similar purposes, or

        - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} as a professional organization, business
          league, chamber of commerce, labour organization, agricultural
          or horticultural organization, civic league or an organization
          operated exclusively for the promotion of social welfare;

      - [(b)]{.lawlabel} substantially all of the income of the entity
        from the activities referred to in paragraph (a) is exempt from
        income tax in the jurisdiction where the entity is located;

      - [(c)]{.lawlabel} the entity has no shareholders or members that
        have a proprietary or beneficial interest in its income or
        assets;

      - [(d)]{.lawlabel} the income or assets of the entity may not be
        distributed to, or applied for the benefit of, a private person
        or non-charitable entity other than

        - [(i)]{.lawlabel} pursuant to the conduct of the entity's
          charitable activities,

        - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} as payment of reasonable compensation for
          services rendered or for the use of property or capital, or

        - [(iii)]{.lawlabel} as payment representing the fair market
          value of property that the entity has purchased;

      - [(e)]{.lawlabel} on termination, liquidation or dissolution of
        the entity, all of the entity's assets must be distributed or
        revert to a non-profit organization or to the government
        (including any governmental entity) of the jurisdiction where it
        is located or any political subdivision thereof; and

      - [(f)]{.lawlabel} the entity does not carry on a trade or
        business that is not directly related to the purposes for which
        it was established. ([organisation à but non
        lucratif]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[non-qualifying gain or loss]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[non-qualifying gain or loss]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means the
      lesser of

      - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the portion of any gain or loss, arising in
        respect of a transfer of assets and liabilities in connection
        with a GloBE reorganization, that is subject to income or
        profits tax in the jurisdiction where the transferor is located;
        and

      - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the portion of the gain or loss that is
        reflected in the financial accounts. ([profit ou perte non
        admissible]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[OECD]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[OECD]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means the Organisation for Economic
      Co-operation and Development. ([OCDE]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[OECD Model Tax Convention]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[OECD Model Tax Convention]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means the Model
      Tax Convention on Income and on Capital: Condensed Version 2017,
      published by the OECD on December 18, 2017, as amended from time
      to time. ([Modèle de Convention fiscale de
      l'OCDE]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[origination year]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[origination year]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means the fiscal year in
      which the originator of a tax credit satisfies the eligibility
      criteria for the tax credit under the laws of the jurisdiction
      granting the tax credit. ([année de création]{.DefinedTermLink
      lang="fr"})

  [[originator]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[originator]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means the person that engages
      in the activities that generate a tax
      credit. ([initiateur]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[other comprehensive income]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[other comprehensive income]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}, in respect of
      a constituent entity, means items of income and expense that are
      recognized outside of the profit or loss account in the financial
      statements used in determining the entity's GloBE income or
      loss. ([autres éléments du résultat global]{.DefinedTermLink
      lang="fr"})

  [[owner]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[owner]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means an entity that directly or
      indirectly holds an ownership interest in another
      entity. ([détenteur]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[ownership interest]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[ownership interest]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means an interest that
      an entity or person holds in a particular entity

      - [(a)]{.lawlabel} in the case of a direct interest, that

        - [(i)]{.lawlabel} carries rights to the profits, capital or
          reserves of the particular entity or the particular entity's
          permanent establishment, and

        - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} would, in the absence of any requirement to
          consolidate the assets, liabilities, income, expenses and cash
          flows of the particular entity in the consolidated financial
          statements, be accounted for as equity in those statements; or

      - [(b)]{.lawlabel} in the case of an indirect interest, through a
        chain of direct ownership interests. ([titre de
        participation]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[partially-owned parent entity]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[partially-owned parent entity]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a
      constituent entity of an MNE group (other than an ultimate parent
      entity, permanent establishment, investment entity or insurance
      investment entity)

      - [(a)]{.lawlabel} that holds, directly or indirectly, an
        ownership interest in another constituent entity of the MNE
        group; and

      - [(b)]{.lawlabel} in which more than 20% of the ownership
        interests, determined having regard only to the ownership
        interests that carry rights to profits, are held, directly or
        indirectly, by persons or entities that are not constituent
        entities of the MNE group. ([entité mère partiellement
        détenue]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[passive income]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[passive income]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means income that is
      included in computing a constituent entity's GloBE income or loss
      and that is

      - [(a)]{.lawlabel} a dividend or dividend equivalent;

      - [(b)]{.lawlabel} interest or an interest equivalent;

      - [(c)]{.lawlabel} rent;

      - [(d)]{.lawlabel} a royalty;

      - [(e)]{.lawlabel} an annuity; or

      - [(f)]{.lawlabel} a net gain from property of a type that
        produces income described in any of paragraphs (a) to
        (e). ([revenu passif]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[patronage dividend]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[patronage dividend]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a distribution by
      a cooperative to its members. ([ristourne]{.DefinedTermLink
      lang="fr"})

  [[pension fund]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[pension fund]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means

      - [(a)]{.lawlabel} an entity that is established and operated in a
        jurisdiction exclusively or almost exclusively to administer or
        provide retirement benefits and ancillary or incidental benefits
        to individuals, if

        - [(i)]{.lawlabel} the entity is regulated as such by that
          jurisdiction or one of its political subdivisions or local
          authorities, or

        - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} those benefits are secured or otherwise
          protected by national regulations and funded by a pool of
          assets held through a fiduciary arrangement or trust to secure
          the fulfilment of the corresponding pension obligations
          against a case of insolvency of the entity or the MNE group of
          which the entity is a member; or

      - [(b)]{.lawlabel} a pension services entity. ([fonds de
        pension]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[pension services entity]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[pension services entity]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means an entity
      that is established and operated exclusively or almost exclusively

      - [(a)]{.lawlabel} to invest funds for the benefit of entities
        described in paragraph (a) of the definition [[pension
        fund]{.dfn}]{.DefinitionRef} in this subsection; or

      - [(b)]{.lawlabel} to carry out activities that are ancillary to
        the regulated activities carried out by an entity described in
        that paragraph that is a member of the same group. ([entité de
        services de fonds de pension]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[permanent establishment]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[permanent establishment]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a place of
      business, including a deemed place of business,

      - [(a)]{.lawlabel} that is situated in a jurisdiction and treated
        as a permanent establishment in accordance with an applicable
        tax treaty in force, provided that the jurisdiction taxes the
        income attributable to it in accordance with a provision similar
        to Article 7 of the OECD Model Tax Convention;

      - [(b)]{.lawlabel} if there is no applicable tax treaty in force,
        in respect of which a jurisdiction taxes the income attributable
        to that place of business under its domestic law on a net basis
        similar to the manner in which it taxes its own tax residents;

      - [(c)]{.lawlabel} if a jurisdiction has no corporate income tax
        system, that is situated in that jurisdiction and that would be
        treated as a permanent establishment in accordance with the OECD
        Model Tax Convention, provided that the jurisdiction would have
        had the right to tax the income attributable to it in accordance
        with Article 7 of that model convention; or

      - [(d)]{.lawlabel} that is not already described in paragraphs (a)
        to (c) and through which operations are conducted outside the
        jurisdiction where the entity that would be the main entity, if
        the place of business were a permanent establishment, is
        located, provided that that jurisdiction exempts the income
        attributable to the operations conducted through the place of
        business. ([établissement stable]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[person]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[person]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} includes a corporation, partnership
      or trust. ([personne]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[portfolio holding]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[portfolio holding]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}, at any time in a fiscal
      year, means ownership interests in an entity that are held at that
      time by a constituent entity, either alone or together with other
      group entities, and that carry rights to less than 10% of the
      profits, capital, reserves or voting rights of that
      entity. ([titres de portefeuille]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[prescribed]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[prescribed]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means

      - [(a)]{.lawlabel} in the case of a form, the information to be
        given on a form or the manner of filing a form, authorized by
        the Minister;

      - [(b)]{.lawlabel} in the case of the manner of making or filing
        an election, authorized by the Minister; and

      - [(c)]{.lawlabel} in any other case, prescribed by regulation or
        determined in accordance with rules prescribed by
        regulation. ([prescrit]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[QDMTT transition year]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[QDMTT transition year]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}, of an MNE group in
      respect of a jurisdiction, means the first fiscal year in which

      - [(a)]{.lawlabel} a constituent entity of, or joint venture
        entity in respect of, the MNE group that is located in the
        jurisdiction is subject to a qualified domestic minimum top-up
        tax; and

      - [(b)]{.lawlabel} subsection 33(1), 47(2) or 53(3), section 45 or
        an equivalent provision under the laws of the jurisdiction does
        not apply for the jurisdiction. ([année de transition
        ICMNA]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[qualified ancillary international shipping income]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[qualified ancillary international shipping
      income]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} has the same meaning as in subsection
      19(7). ([revenu de transport maritime international accessoire
      admissible]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[qualified debt release amount]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[qualified debt release amount]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}, for a
      fiscal year, of a constituent entity that is a debtor, means an
      amount in respect of a debt release to the extent that

      - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the debt release is pursuant to a statutory
        insolvency or bankruptcy proceeding

        - [(i)]{.lawlabel} that is supervised by a court or other
          judicial body in the jurisdiction in which the debtor is
          located, or

        - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} under which an independent insolvency
          administrator is appointed;

      - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the following conditions are met:

        - [(i)]{.lawlabel} the debt release arises pursuant to an
          arrangement with one or more creditors that are not connected
          with the debtor (each referred to in this definition as a
          "third-party creditor"),

        - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} it is reasonable to consider that, in the
          absence of the release of debts owed to one or more
          third-party creditors under the arrangement, the debtor would
          be insolvent within 12 months of the date of the release, and

        - [(iii)]{.lawlabel} the debtor provides an independent expert
