          opinion attesting that the condition in subparagraph (ii) is
          met; or

      - [(c)]{.lawlabel} if neither paragraph (a) nor (b) applies,

        - [(i)]{.lawlabel} the amount is in respect of a debt owed to a
          third-party creditor,

        - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} the debtor's liabilities exceed the fair
          market value of its assets, determined immediately before the
          debt release, and

        - [(iii)]{.lawlabel} the amount does not exceed the least of

          - [(A)]{.lawlabel} if, as a result of the debt release, the
            debtor's assets are greater than or equal to its
            liabilities, the debtor's liabilities less the fair market
            value of its assets, determined immediately before the debt
            release,

          - [(B)]{.lawlabel} if any amount included, as a result of the
            debt release, in computing net income or loss is offset by a
            corresponding reduction in deferred tax assets, the
            reduction in the debtor's deferred tax assets resulting from
            the debt release, and

          - [(C)]{.lawlabel} the amount included, as a result of the
            debt release, in computing net income or loss. ([montant de
            la libération de la dette admissible]{.DefinedTermLink
            lang="fr"})

  [[qualified domestic minimum top-up tax]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[qualified domestic minimum top-up tax]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}, for
      a fiscal year, means a law of a jurisdiction that has the status
      of a qualified domestic minimum top-up tax (including transitional
      qualified status) for the fiscal year as determined by the
      Inclusive Framework and published on the OECD's website. ([impôt
      complémentaire minimum national admissible]{.DefinedTermLink
      lang="fr"})

  [[qualified flow-through ownership interest]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[qualified flow-through ownership
      interest]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} has the same meaning as in
      subsection 28(1). ([titre de participation intermédiaire
      admissible]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[qualified IIR]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[qualified IIR]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}, for a fiscal year, means an
      IIR that has qualified status (including transitional qualified
      status) for the fiscal year as determined by the Inclusive
      Framework and published on the OECD's website. ([RDIR
      admissible]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[qualified imputation tax]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[qualified imputation tax]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means an amount
      of covered taxes, paid or accrued by a constituent entity, that is

      - [(a)]{.lawlabel} imposed under the laws of a jurisdiction
        (referred to in this definition as the "subject jurisdiction");
        and

      - [(b)]{.lawlabel} refundable to, or creditable against the tax
        liability of, the beneficial owner of a dividend or similar
        distribution by the constituent entity (or by the main entity in
        respect of the constituent entity, if the constituent entity is
        a permanent establishment), if

        - [(i)]{.lawlabel} the amount is refundable or creditable under

          - [(A)]{.lawlabel} subsection 126(1) or (2) of the [Income Tax
            Act](/eng/acts/I-3.3), or

          - [(B)]{.lawlabel} a provision of the laws of another
            jurisdiction that is substantially similar to one of those
            subsections, or

        - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} the beneficial owner is

          - [(A)]{.lawlabel} subject to tax on a current basis, in
            respect of the dividend or similar distribution, under the
            laws of the subject jurisdiction at a nominal rate that is
            greater than or equal to the minimum rate,

          - [(B)]{.lawlabel} an individual who is

            - [(I)]{.lawlabel} resident for income tax purposes in the
              subject jurisdiction, and

            - [(II)]{.lawlabel} subject to tax, in respect of the
              dividend or similar distribution as ordinary income, under
              the laws of the subject jurisdiction,

          - [(C)]{.lawlabel} a governmental entity or international
            organization,

          - [(D)]{.lawlabel} an investment entity (other than a group
            entity in respect of the constituent entity) that is created
            and regulated in the subject jurisdiction,

          - [(E)]{.lawlabel} a non-profit organization or pension fund
            that is created and managed in the subject jurisdiction, or

          - [(F)]{.lawlabel} a life insurance company that is located in
            the subject jurisdiction to the extent that the dividend or
            similar distribution is

            - [(I)]{.lawlabel} received in connection with a business
              that is substantially similar to that of a pension fund,
              and

            - [(II)]{.lawlabel} subject to tax, under the laws of the
              subject jurisdiction, in a manner that is substantially
              similar to the manner in which a pension fund is, under
              the laws of the subject jurisdiction, subject to tax in
              respect of a dividend or similar distribution. ([impôt
              d'imputation admissible]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[qualified refundable tax credit]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[qualified refundable tax credit]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a
      refundable tax credit (other than any amount of tax creditable or
      refundable pursuant to a qualified imputation tax or a
      disqualified refundable imputation tax) to the extent that it is
      designed to be payable or available as cash or cash equivalents
      within four years from when a constituent entity satisfies the
      conditions for receiving the credit under the laws of the
      jurisdiction granting the credit. ([crédit d'impôt remboursable
      admissible]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[qualified UTPR]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[qualified UTPR]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}, for a fiscal year, means a
      UTPR that has qualified status (including transitional qualified
      status) for the fiscal year as determined by the Inclusive
      Framework and published on the OECD's website. ([RPII
      admissible]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[qualifying MNE group]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[qualifying MNE group]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} has the same meaning
      as in subsection 9(1). ([groupe d'EMN admissible]{.DefinedTermLink
      lang="fr"})

  [[qualifying non-profit subsidiary]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[qualifying non-profit subsidiary]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}, for a
      fiscal year, means an entity in respect of an MNE group if

      - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the entity is wholly owned by one or more
        entities that are non-profit organizations; and

      - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the revenue of the MNE group for the fiscal
        year, determined without reference to the revenues of any group
        entities that are non-profit organizations and of any group
        entities that would, in the absence of this definition, be
        described in paragraph 13(1)(b) or (c), is less than

        - [(i)]{.lawlabel} the revenue threshold of the MNE group for
          the fiscal year, and

        - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} 25% of the revenue of the MNE group for the
          fiscal year. ([filiale à but non lucratif
          admissible]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[qualifying tier one capital]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[qualifying tier one capital]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means an
      instrument issued by a constituent entity pursuant to regulatory
      requirements applicable to the banking or insurance sector that is
      convertible to equity or written down if a pre-specified trigger
      event occurs and that has other features that are designed to aid
      loss absorbency in the event of a financial crisis. ([fonds
      propres de catégorie 1 admissibles]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[real estate investment vehicle]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[real estate investment vehicle]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means an
      entity

      - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the taxation of which achieves a single level
        of taxation either in its hands or the hands of its interest
        holders (with at most one year of deferral); and

      - [(b)]{.lawlabel} that

        - [(i)]{.lawlabel} holds predominantly immovable property, and

        - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} is itself widely held. ([véhicule
          d'investissement immobilier]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[recapture account loss carry-forward]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[recapture account loss carry-forward]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} has
      the same meaning as in subsection 37(3). ([report des pertes du
      compte de récupération]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[recapture exception accrual]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[recapture exception accrual]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a tax
      expense accrued in the financial accounts of a constituent entity
      that is attributable to changes in associated deferred tax
      liabilities in respect of

      - [(a)]{.lawlabel} cost recovery allowance on a tangible asset;

      - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the cost of a licence or similar arrangement
        from a government for the use of immovable property or the
        exploitation of natural resources that entails significant
        investment in tangible assets;

      - [(c)]{.lawlabel} research and development expenses;

      - [(d)]{.lawlabel} decommissioning and remediation expenses;

      - [(e)]{.lawlabel} fair value accounting on unrealized net gains;

      - [(f)]{.lawlabel} foreign currency exchange net gains;

      - [(g)]{.lawlabel} insurance reserves and insurance policy
        deferred acquisition costs;

      - [(h)]{.lawlabel} a gain that is

        - [(i)]{.lawlabel} from the sale of tangible property located in
          the jurisdiction in which the constituent entity is located,
          and

        - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} re-invested in tangible property located in
          the jurisdiction; and

      - [(i)]{.lawlabel} an additional amount accrued as a result of
        accounting principle changes with respect to an item described
        in any of paragraphs (a) to (h). ([charge d'impôt non soumise à
        récupération]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[regulation]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[regulation]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a regulation made under
      this Act. ([règlement]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[relevant parent entity]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[relevant parent entity]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} has the same
      meaning as in subsection 14(3). ([entité mère
      pertinente]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[revenue threshold]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[revenue threshold]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} has the same meaning as
      in subsection 9(2). ([seuil de chiffre
      d'affaires]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[reverse hybrid entity]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[reverse hybrid entity]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a flow-through
      entity, if

      - [(a)]{.lawlabel} in relation to an owner that holds a direct
        ownership interest in the flow-through entity, the flow-through
        entity is not fiscally transparent; or

      - [(b)]{.lawlabel} in relation to an owner that holds an indirect
        ownership interest in the flow-through entity,

        - [(i)]{.lawlabel} the flow-through entity is not fiscally
          transparent, and

        - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} each entity through which that owner holds
          its indirect ownership interest is fiscally
          transparent. ([entité hybride inversée]{.DefinedTermLink
          lang="fr"})

  [[revocation year]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[revocation year]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}, in respect of a five-year
      election or an election under section 26, means the first fiscal
      year for which the election is no longer in effect because it has
      been revoked. ([année de révocation]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[short-term portfolio holding]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[short-term portfolio holding]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}, of a
      constituent entity that receives or accrues dividends or other
      distributions, at any time in a fiscal year, means a portfolio
      holding that has been economically held by the constituent entity
      for less than one year at the date of the distribution. ([titre de
      portefeuille à court terme]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[standard constituent entity]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[standard constituent entity]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} has the same
      meaning as in subsection 11(3). ([entité constitutive
      type]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[stapled structure]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[stapled structure]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means an arrangement
      entered into by the ultimate parent entities of two or more groups
      under which

      - [(a)]{.lawlabel} not less than 50% of the ownership interests in
        each ultimate parent entity are (by reason of the form of
        ownership, restrictions on transfer or any other terms or
        conditions) combined with not less than 50% of the ownership
        interests in each other ultimate parent entity and cannot be
        transferred or traded other than in that combined form;

      - [(b)]{.lawlabel} if the combined ownership interests of the
        ultimate parent entities are listed on a securities market, they
        are quoted at a single price; and

      - [(c)]{.lawlabel} financial statements are prepared by one of the
        ultimate parent entities in which the assets, liabilities,
        income, expenses and cash flows of all the entities of all the
        groups are presented together as those of a single economic
        unit, and those financial statements

        - [(i)]{.lawlabel} would be consolidated financial statements if
          the entities of all the groups were included in a single group
          and the ultimate parent entity that prepared the financial
          statements was the ultimate parent entity of that group, and

        - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} are required by an applicable regulatory
          regime of a jurisdiction to be externally audited. ([structure
          indissociable]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[stateless constituent entity]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[stateless constituent entity]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a
      constituent entity of an MNE group that is either

      - [(a)]{.lawlabel} a stateless entity under paragraph 5(2)(b); or

      - [(b)]{.lawlabel} a stateless permanent establishment under
        paragraph 5(3)(d). ([entité constitutive
        apatride]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[substance-based income exclusion amount]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[substance-based income exclusion
      amount]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} has the same meaning as in subsection
      32(1). ([montant de l'exclusion de revenus fondée sur la
      substance]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[substitute loss carry-forward recapture amount]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[substitute loss carry-forward recapture
      amount]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}, of a constituent entity that is
      located in a particular jurisdiction, means an amount if

      - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the amount would be a tax loss or a portion of
        a tax loss (referred to in this definition as the "consumed
        domestic loss") of the constituent entity if the consumed
        domestic loss were not offset against another amount --- in
        respect of the income of another entity (referred to in this
        definition as the "controlled foreign company") that is located
        in a jurisdiction other than the particular jurisdiction and in
        which the constituent entity holds, directly or indirectly, an
        ownership interest --- that is included, under the controlled
        foreign company tax regime of the particular jurisdiction, in
        computing the constituent entity's taxable income in the
        particular jurisdiction; and

      - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the income tax laws of the particular
        jurisdiction allow the consumed domestic loss to be recaptured
        by the constituent entity in subsequent taxation years, by
        recharacterizing amounts of income of the constituent entity
        from sources in the particular jurisdiction as amounts in
        respect of income of the controlled foreign company that are
        included, under the controlled foreign company tax regime of the
        particular jurisdiction, in computing the constituent entity's
        taxable income in the particular jurisdiction or as amounts of
        income from another foreign source. ([montant de récupération du
        report de pertes de remplacement]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[substitute loss carry-forward tax credit]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[substitute loss carry-forward tax credit]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm},
      of a constituent entity that is located in a particular
      jurisdiction, means a tax credit, or any portion of a tax credit,
      of the constituent entity that

      - [(a)]{.lawlabel} arises under the income tax laws of the
        particular jurisdiction in respect of tax paid to the government
        of a jurisdiction other than the particular jurisdiction by
        another entity (referred to in this definition as the
        "controlled foreign company") that is located in a jurisdiction
        other than the particular jurisdiction, in which the constituent
        entity holds, directly or indirectly, an ownership interest, on
        income (referred to in this definition as the "attributed
        controlled foreign company income") of the controlled foreign
        company that is included under the controlled foreign company
        tax regime of the particular jurisdiction for the purposes of
        determining the constituent entity's taxable income in the
        particular jurisdiction;

      - [(b)]{.lawlabel} is not allowed to be used in the particular
        taxation year in which it arises solely because the income tax
        laws of the particular jurisdiction require, in determining the
        constituent entity's taxable income, that an amount that would
        be a tax loss of the constituent entity in the absence of the
        attributed controlled foreign company income in the particular
        taxation year be offset against the attributed controlled
        foreign company income in priority to the tax credit, or portion
        of the tax credit, being used to reduce or eliminate any covered
        taxes for which the constituent entity would otherwise be liable
        in respect of the attributed controlled foreign company income
        under the income tax laws of the particular jurisdiction; and

      - [(c)]{.lawlabel} is, under the income tax laws of the particular
        jurisdiction, allowed to be used by the constituent entity, in a
        taxation year following the particular taxation year, to reduce
        or eliminate any covered taxes for which the constituent entity
        would otherwise be liable under the income tax laws of the
        particular jurisdiction in respect of income that is included in
        computing the constituent entity's GloBE income or
        loss. ([crédit d'impôt pour report de pertes de
        remplacement]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[tax]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[tax]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a compulsory unrequited payment
      to general government. ([impôt]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[tax transparent entity]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[tax transparent entity]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}, in relation to an
      owner, means a flow-through entity to the extent that the
      flow-through entity is fiscally transparent under the laws of the
      jurisdiction in which the owner is located. ([entité fiscalement
      transparente]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[tax transparent structure]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[tax transparent structure]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a chain of
      entities through which a particular entity holds an ownership
      interest, if the entities are

      - [(a)]{.lawlabel} flow-through entities; and

      - [(b)]{.lawlabel} fiscally transparent in relation to the
        particular entity. ([structure fiscalement
        transparente]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[tax treaty]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[tax treaty]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means an agreement for the
      avoidance of double taxation with respect to taxes on income and
      on capital. ([convention fiscale]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[top-up amount]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[top-up amount]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} has the same meaning as in
      subsection 30(1). ([montant complémentaire]{.DefinedTermLink
      lang="fr"})

  [[top-up percentage]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[top-up percentage]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} has the same meaning as
      in subsection 30(3). ([pourcentage
      complémentaire]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[total deferred tax adjustment amount]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[total deferred tax adjustment amount]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} has
      the same meaning as in subsection 25(1). ([montant total de
      l'ajustement pour impôts différés]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[transitional special allocation year]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[transitional special allocation year]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}, of
      an MNE group, means any fiscal year beginning on or before
      December 31, 2025 and ending on or before June 30, 2027. ([année
      de répartition spéciale transitoire]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[ultimate parent entity]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[ultimate parent entity]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} has the same
      meaning as in subsection 12(1). ([entité mère
      ultime]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[unclaimed accrual]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[unclaimed accrual]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}, of a constituent entity
      of an MNE group for a fiscal year, means an increase in a deferred
      tax liability recorded in the financial accounts of the
      constituent entity for the fiscal year if

      - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the increase is not expected to reverse on or
        before the last day of the fifth fiscal year following the
        fiscal year; and

      - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the filing constituent entity of the MNE group
        elects to not include the increase in determining the
        constituent entity's total deferred tax adjustment amount for
        the fiscal year. ([accumulation non réclamée]{.DefinedTermLink
        lang="fr"})

  [[unrelated purchaser]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[unrelated purchaser]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}, in respect of the
      transfer of a tax credit by a person, means another person that
      acquires the tax credit unless

      - [(a)]{.lawlabel} based on all the relevant facts and
        circumstances, one person has control of the other or both
        persons are under the control of the same person or persons;

      - [(b)]{.lawlabel} one of the persons owns directly or indirectly,

        - [(i)]{.lawlabel} if the other person is a corporation, shares
          of the capital stock of the corporation that

          - [(A)]{.lawlabel} give their holder at least 50% of the votes
            of the shareholders of the corporation, and

          - [(B)]{.lawlabel} have a fair market value of at least 50% of
            the fair market value of all the issued and outstanding
            shares of the capital stock of the corporation, and

        - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} in any other case, at least 50% of the
          beneficial interest in the other person; or

      - [(c)]{.lawlabel} a third person owns, directly or indirectly, in
        respect of each of the other persons

        - [(i)]{.lawlabel} if one or both of those other persons are
          corporations, shares of the capital stock of each such
          corporation that

          - [(A)]{.lawlabel} give their holder at least 50% of the votes
            of the shareholders of the corporation, and

          - [(B)]{.lawlabel} have a fair market value of at least 50% of
            the fair market value of all the issued and outstanding
            shares of the capital stock of the corporation, and

        - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} if one or both of those persons are not
          corporations, at least 50% of the beneficial interest in each
          such person. ([acheteur non lié]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[UTPR]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[UTPR]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a law of a jurisdiction that
      may reasonably be considered to have been enacted with the
      intention of implementing, in whole or in part, Articles 2.4 to
      2.6 of the GloBE Model Rules. ([RPII]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Interpretation --- permanent
  establishment

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} In applying the provisions of this Act in respect of
  a constituent entity that is a permanent establishment of a main
  entity or that is the main entity,

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} a reference to a "constituent entity" is to be
    interpreted as a reference to the permanent establishment or the
    main entity, as the context requires; and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the main entity is deemed to hold a controlling
    interest in the permanent establishment.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Interpretation --- flow-through entity

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} In applying the provisions of this Act in respect of
  a constituent entity that is a flow-through entity or that is an owner
  in respect of the flow-through entity, a reference to a "constituent
  entity" is to be interpreted as a reference to the owner or the
  flow-through entity, as the context requires.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Interpretation --- financial accounts

  [(4)]{.lawlabel} A reference to financial accounts or to financial
  statements is to be interpreted as a reference to the accounts or
  statements (which may in some circumstances be hypothetical) that are
  the basis for determining the financial accounting income of a
  constituent entity.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Interpretation --- connected

  [(5)]{.lawlabel} A person or entity is "connected" with another person
  or entity if they are "closely related" within the meaning of that
  term in Article 5(8) of the OECD Model Tax Convention.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Interpretation --- trusts

  [(6)]{.lawlabel} A reference to a trust includes an estate, and a
  reference to a trustee includes any legal representative of a trust
  having ownership or control of the trust property of that trust.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Interpretation --- subject to qualified
  IIR, etc.

  [(7)]{.lawlabel} A constituent entity of, or joint venture entity in
  respect of, an MNE group (referred to in this subsection as the
  "subject entity") is considered to be subject to

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} a qualified IIR or qualified UTPR if a constituent
    entity of the MNE group (or person liable on behalf of a constituent
    entity) would, if the subject entity had a top-up amount (or
    equivalent) greater than nil, be subject to tax under the qualified
    IIR or the qualified UTPR, as the case may be, in respect of that
    top-up amount (or equivalent); and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} a qualified domestic minimum top-up tax (including,
    for greater certainty, the one implemented in Part 3) if a
    constituent entity of, or joint venture entity in respect of, the
    MNE group (or person liable on behalf of a constituent entity or
    joint venture entity) would, if the subject entity had a domestic
    top-up amount (or equivalent) greater than nil, be subject to tax
    under the qualified domestic minimum top-up tax in respect of that
    domestic top-up amount (or equivalent).

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Deemed flow-through entity and tax
  transparent entity

  [(8)]{.lawlabel} A constituent entity that is not subject to covered
  taxes as a result of being tax resident in any jurisdiction and is not
  subject to a qualified domestic minimum top-up tax is deemed to be a
  flow-through entity and a tax transparent entity, to the extent that

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} it is fiscally transparent under the laws of the
    jurisdiction where its owners are located;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} it does not have a place of business in the
    jurisdiction where it was created; and

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} its income, expenditure, profit or loss is not
    attributable to a permanent establishment.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Interpretation --- election

  [(9)]{.lawlabel} A reference to an election or a revocation of an
  election for a fiscal year, or to the filing constituent entity of an
  MNE group electing or revoking an election for a fiscal year, is to be
  interpreted as a reference to an election or a revocation made in the
  GIR of the MNE group for the fiscal year.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Interpretation

- **[[3]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-3 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} This
  Part, Part 2 and relevant provisions of Part 5 implement the GloBE
  Model Rules, the GloBE Commentary and the administrative guidance in
  respect of the GloBE Model Rules approved by the Inclusive Framework
  and published by the OECD from time to time and, unless the context
  otherwise requires, these Parts and provisions are to be interpreted
  consistently with those sources, as amended from time to time.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Designation of additional interpretive
  guidance

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} The Governor in Council may from time to time by
  regulation designate additional sources in respect of which the
  interpretation of this Act should be consistent.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Binding on His Majesty

**[[4]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-4 .sectionLabel}** This Act is binding on His
Majesty in right of Canada or a province.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Location of entities

- **[[5]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-5 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} Subject
  to subsection 6(1), an entity, other than a flow-through entity, is
  located

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} in a jurisdiction if the entity is tax resident in
    that jurisdiction based on its place of management or creation, or
    based on similar criteria; and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} in any other case, in the jurisdiction where it was
    created.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Location of flow-though entities

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} A flow-through entity

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} is located in the jurisdiction where it was
    created, if

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} the flow-through entity is the ultimate parent
      entity of an MNE group, or

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} the flow-through entity

      - [(A)]{.lawlabel} is an entity described in paragraph 14(3)(c),
        and

      - [(B)]{.lawlabel} has a direct or indirect ownership interest in
        at least one constituent entity of the MNE group that

        - [(I)]{.lawlabel} has a top-up amount, and

        - [(II)]{.lawlabel} is not located in the jurisdiction where the
          flow-through entity was created; and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} in any other case, is a stateless entity.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Location of permanent establishments

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} In determining the jurisdiction where a permanent
  establishment is located,

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} if the permanent establishment is described in
    paragraph (a) of the definition [[permanent
    establishment]{.dfn}]{.DefinitionRef} in subsection 2(1), it is
    located in the jurisdiction where it is treated as a permanent
    establishment and is taxed under the applicable tax treaty;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} if the permanent establishment is described in
    paragraph (b) of that definition, it is located in the jurisdiction
    where it is subject to net basis taxation based on its business
    presence;

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} if the permanent establishment is described in
    paragraph (c) of that definition, it is located in the jurisdiction
    where it is situated; and

  - [(d)]{.lawlabel} if the permanent establishment is described in
    paragraph (d) of that definition, it is a stateless permanent
    establishment.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Stateless entity --- notional
  jurisdiction

  [(4)]{.lawlabel} A stateless entity or stateless permanent
  establishment is deemed to be located in a notional jurisdiction in
  which no other entity or permanent establishment is located.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Change of location

  [(5)]{.lawlabel} If an entity's location changes during a fiscal year,
  it is deemed to be located, for the fiscal year, in the jurisdiction
  where it was located at the beginning of that fiscal year.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Dual-located entity --- tie-breaker rule

- **[[6]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-6 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} If a
  constituent entity would, in the absence of this section, be located
  in more than one jurisdiction for a fiscal year under subsection 5(1),
  the following rules apply:

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} if the jurisdictions are party to a tax treaty that
    is in force and the constituent entity is deemed to be resident in
    only one of the jurisdictions for purposes of the treaty, the entity
    is located in that jurisdiction for the fiscal year; and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} in any other case,

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} if the constituent entity has, for the fiscal
      year, a greater amount of covered taxes (determined without
      reference to any taxes under a controlled foreign company tax
      regime) in one of the jurisdictions than in the other
      jurisdictions, the constituent entity is located in that
      jurisdiction for the fiscal year,

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} if subparagraph (i) does not apply and the
      constituent entity has a greater substance-based income exclusion
      amount for one of the jurisdictions than for the other
      jurisdictions, the constituent entity is located in that
      jurisdiction for the fiscal year, and

    - [(iii)]{.lawlabel} if subparagraphs (i) and (ii) do not apply, the
      constituent entity is, for the fiscal year,

      - [(A)]{.lawlabel} if it is an ultimate parent entity of an MNE
        group, located in the jurisdiction where it was created, and

      - [(B)]{.lawlabel} in any other case, a stateless entity.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Substance-based income exclusion amount

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} For the purposes of subparagraph (1)(b)(ii), a
  constituent entity's substance-based income exclusion amount for a
  jurisdiction for a fiscal year is

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} if a substance-based income exclusion amount is
    calculated for the jurisdiction for the fiscal year, the amount that
    would be determined for that constituent entity under subsection
    32(1) if the constituent entity were the only constituent entity
    that is located in that jurisdiction and if the reference to
    "standard constituent entity" in that subsection were read as a
    reference to "constituent entity"; and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} in any other case, nil.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Dual-located entity --- deeming rule

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} If a constituent entity that would, in the absence of
  subsection (1), be located in more than one jurisdiction (each
  referred to in this subsection as a "relevant jurisdiction"), is
  located in only one of those jurisdictions (referred to in this
  subsection as the "location jurisdiction") because of subsection (1)
  and is not subject to tax under a qualified IIR in the location
  jurisdiction,

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} where Canada is a relevant jurisdiction (but is not
    the location jurisdiction) and Canada is not restricted from taxing
    the constituent entity under Part 2 because of an applicable tax
    treaty, the entity is deemed to be located in Canada for the
    purposes of clause 14(1)(b)(i)(B) and subparagraph 14(3)(a)(i); and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} where Canada is not a relevant jurisdiction, and
    the constituent entity is subject to tax in a relevant jurisdiction
    under a qualified IIR because of a provision under the laws of that
    relevant jurisdiction that is equivalent in effect to paragraph (a),
    the entity is deemed to be located in that relevant jurisdiction for
    the purposes of subsection 14(3).

### [Application]{.HTitleText2} {#h-1474656 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Currency conversion --- GloBE
calculations

- **[[7]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-7 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} Subject
  to subsection (2), if an amount that is relevant to the determination
  of the top-up amount, or of any amount or result relevant to the
  determination of the top-up amount, of a constituent entity of an MNE
  group for a fiscal year is denominated in a currency other than the
  reporting currency of the consolidated financial statements of the
  ultimate parent entity of the MNE group and is not converted to that
  reporting currency in the course of preparing the consolidated
  financial statements, that amount is to be converted to that reporting
  currency using the foreign currency translation principles of the
  financial accounting standard that would have been used to convert the
  amount to the reporting currency if that conversion were undertaken in
  the course of preparing the consolidated financial statements.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Euro-denominated thresholds

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} For the purposes of determining if any materiality or
  other threshold in this Act that is denominated in the currency of the
  European Monetary Union is satisfied or exceeded by an amount in
  respect of a group, entity or jurisdiction for a particular fiscal
  year, if the amount is denominated in another currency, the amount is
  to be converted from that currency to the currency of the European
  Monetary Union using the average of the daily rates of exchange, in
  respect of the two currencies for the month of December included in
  the fiscal year immediately preceding the particular fiscal year, as
  quoted by

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the European Central Bank;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the Bank of Canada, if the European Central Bank
    does not quote a daily rate of exchange in respect of the two
    currencies; or

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} another source that is acceptable to the Minister,
    if neither the European Central Bank nor the Bank of Canada quote a
    daily rate of exchange in respect of the two currencies.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}If subsections (1) and (2) do not apply

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} Except as specifically otherwise provided, if an
  amount is relevant to a determination or computation that is required
  for the purposes of this Act in respect of an entity included in an
  MNE group or in respect of the MNE group for a fiscal year

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the currency in which that amount is to be
    denominated for use in the determination or computation is the
    reporting currency of the consolidated financial statements of the
    ultimate parent entity of the MNE group; and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} if that amount is not denominated in the reporting
    currency, it is to be converted, for use in the determination or
    computation, to the reporting currency using the average for the
    fiscal year of the daily rates of exchange quoted by the Bank of
    Canada or, if there is no daily rate quoted by the Bank of Canada
    for a particular day, a daily rate of exchange acceptable to the
    Minister, in respect of the two currencies.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Negative amounts

**[[8]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-8 .sectionLabel}** Except as specifically
otherwise provided, if an amount or a number is required under this Act
to be determined or calculated by or in accordance with an algebraic
formula and the amount or number determined or calculated would, but for
this section, be a negative amount or number, it is deemed to be nil.

### [Scope]{.HTitleText2} {#h-1474671 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Definition of [[qualifying MNE
group]{.dfn}]{.DefinitionRef}

- **[[9]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-9 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} A
  [[qualifying MNE group]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} for a particular fiscal
  year means an MNE group that meets the following conditions:

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} revenue reported in the consolidated financial
    statements of the ultimate parent entity of the MNE group is equal
    to or greater than the revenue threshold of the MNE group in at
    least two of the four fiscal years immediately preceding the
    particular fiscal year; and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the MNE group is not composed exclusively of
    excluded entities for the particular fiscal year.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Definition of [[revenue
  threshold]{.dfn}]{.DefinitionRef}

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} The [[revenue threshold]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} of an
  MNE group for a fiscal year means the amount determined by the formula

  ::: {#1474679 .Subsection}
  A × B ÷ 365

  where

  [A]{.dfn}
  :   is €750 million; and

  [B]{.dfn}
  :   is the number of days in the fiscal year.
  :::

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Revenue --- pre-merger years

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} For the purposes of the definition [[qualifying MNE
  group]{.dfn}]{.DefinitionRef} in subsection (1) and subsection (4), if
  an MNE group is formed as a result of a merger in a particular fiscal
  year between a group, or an entity that is not included in a group
  immediately before the merger (referred to in this subsection as an
  "ungrouped entity"), and one or more other groups or ungrouped
  entities, then for any fiscal year preceding the particular fiscal
  year (referred to in this subsection as the "preceding year")

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} all amounts that are the revenue or a portion of
    the revenue reported in the consolidated financial statements of the
    ultimate parent entity of any of those groups or in the financial
    statements of any of those ungrouped entities, as the case may be,
    and that correspond to any period included in the preceding year
    (with the revenue apportioned between periods, if necessary, on a
    just and reasonable basis) are to be aggregated; and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the aggregate amount determined under paragraph (a)
    is deemed to be the revenue reported in the consolidated financial
    statements of the ultimate parent entity of the MNE group for the
    preceding year.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Qualifying MNE group --- demerger

  [(4)]{.lawlabel} If there is a demerger of an MNE group that was a
  qualifying MNE group in the fiscal year immediately preceding the
  fiscal year in which the demerger occurs and any of the groups
  resulting from the demerger is an MNE group (referred to in this
  subsection as a "demerged MNE group"), the condition in paragraph (a)
  of the definition [[qualifying MNE group]{.dfn}]{.DefinitionRef} in
  subsection (1) is deemed to be satisfied in respect of the demerged
  MNE group for

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the first fiscal year of the demerged MNE group
    ending after the demerger occurs (referred to in this paragraph as
    the "first post-demerger year"), if the revenue reported in the
    consolidated financial statements of the ultimate parent entity of
    the demerged MNE group for the first post-demerger year is greater
    than or equal to the amount determined by the formula

    ::: {#1474690 .Paragraph}
    A × B ÷ 365

    where

    [A]{.dfn}
    :   is €750 million, and

    [B]{.dfn}
    :   is the number of days in the first post-demerger year; and
    :::

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} any fiscal year (referred to in this paragraph as
    the "tested year") among the three fiscal years of the demerged MNE
    group immediately following the first post-demerger year, if the
    revenue reported in the consolidated financial statements of the
    ultimate parent entity of the demerged MNE group for at least two
    out of the tested year and any other fiscal years of the demerged
    MNE group ending before the tested year and after the demerger (the
    tested year and each of those other years referred to in this
    paragraph as a "post-demerger year") is greater than or equal to the
    amount determined by the formula

    ::: {#1474695 .Paragraph}
    A × C ÷ 365

    where

    [C]{.dfn}
    :   is the number of days in the post-demerger year.
    :::

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Merger --- meaning

  [(5)]{.lawlabel} For the purposes of subsection (3), a merger is any
  arrangement under which

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} all or substantially all of the entities of two or
    more groups are brought under common control such that those
    entities form a single group immediately following the conclusion of
    the arrangement; or

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} one or more entities that are not included in any
    group are brought under common control with another entity, or one
    or more groups of entities, such that all those entities form a
    single group immediately following the conclusion of the
    arrangement.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Demerger --- meaning

  [(6)]{.lawlabel} For the purposes of subsection (4), a demerger is any
  arrangement under which the entities of a group are separated into two
  or more groups.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Interpretation --- fiscal year

  [(7)]{.lawlabel} For the purposes of paragraph (1)(a) and subsection
  (3), if an MNE group formed as a result of a merger does not have four
  actual fiscal years preceding the merger, the contiguous periods of
  equal length to the earliest of its actual fiscal years, the latest of
  which immediately precedes that earliest actual fiscal year, are
  deemed to be fiscal years of the MNE group.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Definition of [[MNE
group]{.dfn}]{.DefinitionRef}

- **[[10]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-10 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} An
  [[MNE group]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a group that includes at least
  one entity or permanent establishment that is not located in the
  jurisdiction in which the ultimate parent entity of the group is
  located.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Definition of
  [[group]{.dfn}]{.DefinitionRef}

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} A [[group]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} an ultimate parent entity and one or more other
    entities each of whose assets, liabilities, income, expenses and
    cash flows, by reason of ownership or control, either

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} are included in the consolidated financial
      statements of the ultimate parent entity, or

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} would be included in the consolidated financial
      statements of the ultimate parent entity but for an exclusion on
      size or materiality grounds, or on the grounds that the entity is
      held for sale; or

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} an entity that

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} is not part of a group described in paragraph
      (a), and

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} has one or more permanent establishments that
      are not located in the jurisdiction in which the entity is
      located.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Definition of [[constituent
entity]{.dfn}]{.DefinitionRef}

- **[[11]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-11 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} A
  [[constituent entity]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}, in respect of a group,
  means

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} an entity, other than an excluded entity, that is
    included in the group; or

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} a permanent establishment of a main entity that is
    described in paragraph (a).

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Permanent establishment --- separate
  treatment

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} Except as expressly otherwise provided, a permanent
  establishment that is a constituent entity under paragraph (1)(b) is
  treated as a constituent entity separate from its main entity and any
  other permanent establishment of that main entity.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Definition of [[standard constituent
  entity]{.dfn}]{.DefinitionRef}

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} A [[standard constituent
  entity]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}, of an MNE group, means a constituent
  entity other than

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} an investment entity;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} an insurance investment entity; or

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} a minority-owned constituent entity.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Definition of [[ultimate parent
entity]{.dfn}]{.DefinitionRef}

- **[[12]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-12 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} An
  [[ultimate parent entity]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} an entity

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} that has, directly or indirectly, a controlling
      interest in any other entity, and

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} in which no other entity has, directly or
      indirectly, a controlling interest; or

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the main entity of a group that is described in
    paragraph 10(2)(b).

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Exclusion --- sovereign wealth funds

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} For the purposes of subsection (1), any governmental
  entity that satisfies the condition in subparagraph (b)(ii) of the
  definition [[governmental entity]{.dfn}]{.DefinitionRef} in subsection
  2(1) is deemed not to have, directly or indirectly, a controlling
  interest in any other entity.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Definition of [[excluded
entity]{.dfn}]{.DefinitionRef}

- **[[13]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-13 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} An
  [[excluded entity]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}, for a fiscal year, means

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} an entity (referred to in this subsection as a
    "primary excluded entity") that is throughout the fiscal year

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} a governmental entity,

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} an international organization,

    - [(iii)]{.lawlabel} a non-profit organization,

    - [(iv)]{.lawlabel} a pension fund,

    - [(v)]{.lawlabel} an investment fund that is an ultimate parent
      entity,

    - [(vi)]{.lawlabel} a real estate investment vehicle that is an
      ultimate parent entity, or

    - [(vii)]{.lawlabel} a qualifying non-profit subsidiary;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} an entity if

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} throughout the fiscal year ownership interests in
      the entity having a fair market value equal to at least 95% of the
      fair market value of all ownership interests in the entity are
      held directly, or indirectly through one or more excluded
      entities, by one or more primary excluded entities (other than a
      pension fund that is a pension services entity), and

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} all or substantially all the activities of the
      entity during the fiscal year consist of

      - [(A)]{.lawlabel} holding assets for the benefit of the one or
        more primary excluded entities,

      - [(B)]{.lawlabel} investing funds for the benefit of the one or
        more primary excluded entities,

      - [(C)]{.lawlabel} activities that are ancillary to those carried
        out by the one or more primary excluded entities, or

      - [(D)]{.lawlabel} any combination of clauses (A) to (C); or

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} an entity if

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} throughout the fiscal year ownership interests in
      the entity having a fair market value equal to at least 85% of the
      fair market value of all ownership interests in the entity are
      held directly, or indirectly through one or more excluded
      entities, by one or more primary excluded entities (other than a
      pension fund that is a pension services entity), and

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} all or substantially all of the entity's
      financial accounting income for the fiscal year consists of
      excluded dividends or excluded equity gains or losses.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Excluded entity --- constituent entity
  election

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} For the purposes of subsection (1), if the relevant
  filing constituent entity elects in respect of a particular entity
  that would, in the absence of this subsection, be an excluded entity
  under paragraph (1)(b) or (c) for a fiscal year

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the particular entity is deemed not to be an
    excluded entity for the fiscal year; and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the election is a five-year election.

## [PART 2]{.HLabel1}[Global Minimum Tax]{.HTitleText1} {#h-1474763 .Part}

### [DIVISION 1]{.HLabel2}[Liability for Tax]{.HTitleText2} {#h-1474764 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Top-up tax liability

- **[[14]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-14 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} A
  person must pay a tax in respect of an MNE group for a fiscal year in
  the amount determined under subsection 15(1) if

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the MNE group is a qualifying MNE group for the
    fiscal year;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} one of the following conditions is met:

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} the person is

      - [(A)]{.lawlabel} a relevant parent entity of the MNE group for
        the fiscal year, and

      - [(B)]{.lawlabel} located in Canada,

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} the person would, under the relevant
      assumptions, include in its income for the purposes of Part I of
      the [Income Tax Act](/eng/acts/I-3.3) income for the fiscal year
      of a relevant parent entity that is

      - [(A)]{.lawlabel} located in Canada, and

      - [(B)]{.lawlabel} not a person; and

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} the relevant parent entity referred to in
    subparagraph (b)(i) or (ii) has a direct or indirect ownership
    interest at any time in the fiscal year in one or more constituent
    entities of the MNE group that

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} is not located in Canada, and

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} has a top-up amount for the fiscal year.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Relevant assumptions

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} For the purposes of subparagraph (1)(b)(ii), the
  relevant assumptions are that

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the relevant parent entity referred to in that
    subparagraph has income for the fiscal year that would be included
    in computing its income for the purposes of Part I of the [Income
    Tax Act](/eng/acts/I-3.3) if it were a person resident in Canada;
    and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the person referred to in that subparagraph is
    resident in Canada for the purposes of the [Income Tax
    Act](/eng/acts/I-3.3).

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Definition of [[relevant parent
  entity]{.dfn}]{.DefinitionRef}

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} A [[relevant parent entity]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}, of
  an MNE group for a fiscal year, means an entity that meets the
  following conditions:

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the entity is located in

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} Canada, or

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} another jurisdiction where it is subject to tax
      under a qualified IIR (referred to in this subsection as a "Pillar
      Two jurisdiction");

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the entity is neither

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} an excluded entity for the purposes of this Act,
      nor

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} excluded from the application of the qualified
      IIR of the jurisdiction where it is located because of a provision
      in the law of that jurisdiction equivalent to subsection 13(1);
      and

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} the entity is any of the following:

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} the ultimate parent entity of the MNE group for
      the year,

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} a particular intermediate parent entity of the
      MNE group for the year, if

      - [(A)]{.lawlabel} the ultimate parent entity of the MNE group is
        not located in Canada or a Pillar Two jurisdiction, and

      - [(B)]{.lawlabel} no other intermediate parent entity of the MNE
        group that is located in Canada or a Pillar Two jurisdiction
        has, directly or indirectly, a controlling interest in the
        particular intermediate parent entity,

    - [(iii)]{.lawlabel} a partially-owned parent entity of the MNE
      group for the year that is not wholly owned, directly or
      indirectly, by another partially-owned parent entity of the MNE
      group that is located in Canada or a Pillar Two jurisdiction.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Top-up tax payable

- **[[15]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-15 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} The
  amount of the tax a person must pay in respect of an MNE group for a
  fiscal year is equal to the total of all amounts, each of which is an
  amount determined by the formula

  ::: {#1474800 .Subsection}
  A − B

  where

  [A]{.dfn}
  :   is the allocable share, of the top-up amount of a constituent
      entity of the MNE group that is not located in Canada for the
      fiscal year, of
      - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the person, if subparagraph 14(1)(b)(i)
        applies, or

      - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the relevant parent entity referred to in
        subparagraph 14(1)(b)(ii), if that subparagraph applies; and

  [B]{.dfn}
  :   is the total of all amounts each of which is a portion of the
      top-up amount of the constituent entity for the fiscal year that
      is included in both
      - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the amount determined for A for the fiscal
        year, and

      - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the allocable share, of the top-up amount of
        the constituent entity for the fiscal year, of a relevant parent
        entity of the MNE group through which the person referred to in
        paragraph (a), or the relevant parent entity referred to in
        paragraph (b), of the description of A indirectly holds an
        ownership interest in the constituent entity.
  :::

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Definition of [[allocable
  share]{.dfn}]{.DefinitionRef}

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} The [[allocable share]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}, of the
  top-up amount of a constituent entity of an MNE group for a fiscal
  year, of a relevant parent entity, means the amount determined by the
  formula

  ::: {#1474810 .Subsection}
  A × B

  where

  [A]{.dfn}
  :   is the top-up amount of the constituent entity for the fiscal
      year; and

  [B]{.dfn}
  :   is the relevant parent entity's inclusion ratio for the
      constituent entity for the fiscal year.
  :::

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Definition of [[inclusion
  ratio]{.dfn}]{.DefinitionRef}

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} The [[inclusion ratio]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}, of a
  relevant parent entity for a constituent entity of an MNE group for a
  fiscal year, means the ratio determined by the formula

  ::: {#1474816 .Subsection}
  (A − B) ÷ A

  where

  [A]{.dfn}
  :   is the GloBE income of the constituent entity for the fiscal year;
      and

  [B]{.dfn}
  :   is the GloBE income of the constituent entity for the fiscal year
      that would be attributable to ownership interests other than
      ownership interests held directly or indirectly by the relevant
      parent entity, under the principles of the financial accounting
      standard applicable in preparing the consolidated financial
      statements of the ultimate parent entity of the MNE group for the
      fiscal year, if the net income of the constituent entity were
      equal to its GloBE income and on the assumption that
      - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the relevant parent entity had prepared
        consolidated financial statements (referred to in this
        subsection as "hypothetical consolidated financial statements")
        in accordance with that financial accounting standard,

      - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the relevant parent entity had a controlling
        interest in the constituent entity such that all of the income
        and expenses of the constituent entity were consolidated on a
        line-by-line basis with those of the relevant parent entity in
        the hypothetical consolidated financial statements,

      - [(c)]{.lawlabel} none of the GloBE income of the constituent
        entity were attributable to transactions with group entities of
        the MNE group, and

      - [(d)]{.lawlabel} no ownership interests, other than those held
        directly or indirectly by the relevant parent entity, were held
        by any group entity of the MNE group.
  :::

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Flow-through and investment entities

  [(4)]{.lawlabel} For the purposes of subsection (3), if a constituent
  entity of an MNE group is a flow-through entity, an investment entity
  or an insurance investment entity, none of the GloBE income of the
  constituent entity is to be regarded as attributable to ownership
  interests held by any entity that is not included in the MNE group.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Inclusion ratio --- deemed GloBE income

  [(5)]{.lawlabel} For the purposes of subsection (3), if the net GloBE
  income of the MNE group for a jurisdiction for a fiscal year is nil,
  the GloBE income of a constituent entity of the MNE group that is
  located in the jurisdiction for the fiscal year is deemed to be the
  amount determined by the formula

  ::: {#1474828 .Subsection}
  A + B

  where

  [A]{.dfn}
  :   is the amount determined by the formula
      ::: {#1474831 .NestedFormula}
      C ÷ D

      where

      [C]{.dfn}
      :   is the allocated adjustment top-up amount of the constituent
          entity for the fiscal year, and

      [D]{.dfn}
      :   is the minimum rate; and
      :::

  [B]{.dfn}
  :   is the amount determined by the formula
      ::: {#1474836 .NestedFormula}
      E ÷ D

      where

      [E]{.dfn}
      :   is the excess negative tax expense top-up amount of the
          constituent entity for the fiscal year.
      :::
  :::

### [DIVISION 2]{.HLabel2}[Computation of GloBE Income or Loss]{.HTitleText2} {#h-1474839 .Subheading}

#### [GloBE Income or Loss]{.HTitleText3} {#h-1474840 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Definition of [[GloBE income or
loss]{.dfn}]{.DefinitionRef}

**[[16]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-16 .sectionLabel}** [[GloBE income or
loss]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}, of a constituent entity for a fiscal year,
means the constituent entity's financial accounting income for the year,
adjusted according to the rules in this Division and Divisions 5 and 7.

#### [SUBDIVISION A]{.HLabel3}[Determination of Financial Accounting Income]{.HTitleText3} {#h-1474843 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Definition of [[financial accounting
income]{.dfn}]{.DefinitionRef}

- **[[17]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-17
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} [[Financial accounting
  income]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}, for a constituent entity for a fiscal
  year, means, subject to subsections (2) to (6),

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} for a constituent entity other than a permanent
    establishment, the net income or loss determined for that
    constituent entity

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} in the preparation of the consolidated financial
      statements of the ultimate parent entity of the MNE group that
      includes the constituent entity, or

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} under another acceptable financial accounting
      standard or authorized financial accounting standard, if

      - [(A)]{.lawlabel} it is not reasonably practicable to determine
        the financial accounting income for the constituent entity based
        on the accounting standard used in the preparation of the
        consolidated financial statements of the ultimate parent entity,

      - [(B)]{.lawlabel} the financial accounts of the constituent
        entity are maintained based on the other acceptable financial
        accounting standard or authorized financial accounting standard,

      - [(C)]{.lawlabel} the information in those financial accounts is
        reliable, such that an auditor applying the generally accepted
        auditing standards of the jurisdiction in which the ultimate
        parent entity or constituent entity is located (or, in the case
        of a flow-through entity, the jurisdiction in which the entity
        was created) would reasonably conclude that the constituent
        entity has in place processes and controls that are likely to
        ensure that the information in the financial accounts is fair
        and accurate, and

      - [(D)]{.lawlabel} that amount is adjusted to eliminate any
        permanent difference greater than €1 million that arises for the
        fiscal year because the other financial accounting standard is
        used instead of the financial accounting standard of the
        ultimate parent entity; and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} for a constituent entity that is a permanent
    establishment,

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} if the permanent establishment is described in
      any of paragraphs (a) to (c) of the definition [[permanent
      establishment]{.dfn}]{.DefinitionRef} in subsection 2(1), the
      amount that

      - [(A)]{.lawlabel} is the net income or loss reflected in the
        separate financial accounts of the permanent establishment, if
        those financial accounts are prepared in accordance with an
        acceptable financial accounting standard or in accordance with
        an authorized financial accounting standard and subject to
        adjustments to prevent any material competitive distortions, or

      - [(B)]{.lawlabel} if the permanent establishment does not have
        separate financial accounts described in clause (A), would be
        the net income or loss of that permanent establishment reflected
        in separate financial accounts prepared on a stand-alone basis
        in accordance with the accounting standard used in the
        preparation of the ultimate parent entity's consolidated
        financial statements, and

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} if the constituent entity is a permanent
      establishment described in paragraph (d) of the definition
      [[permanent establishment]{.dfn}]{.DefinitionRef} in subsection
      2(1), the net income or loss determined on the assumption that

      - [(A)]{.lawlabel} the only income of the permanent establishment
        is its income that is exempted from tax in the jurisdiction
        where the main entity in respect of the permanent establishment
        is located and that is attributable to activities carried on
        outside the jurisdiction in which the main entity is located,
        and

      - [(B)]{.lawlabel} the only expenses of the permanent
        establishment are its expenses that are attributable to the
        activities described in clause (A) and are not deducted for tax
        purposes in the jurisdiction in which the main entity is
        located.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Permanent establishments --- adjustment

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} The amount that would, in the absence of this
  subsection, be a permanent establishment's financial accounting income
  is adjusted to reflect only the amounts of income and expense that are
  --- or, if paragraph (c) applies, would be --- attributable to the
  permanent establishment (regardless of whether such amount is subject
  to tax or deductible, as the case may be, in the jurisdiction in which
  the permanent establishment is located) in accordance with

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} if paragraph (a) of the definition [[permanent
    establishment]{.dfn}]{.DefinitionRef} in subsection 2(1) applies,
    the tax treaty applicable to the permanent establishment;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} if paragraph (b) of that definition applies, the
    law of the jurisdiction in which the permanent establishment is
    located; or

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} if paragraph (c) of that definition applies,
    Article 7 of the OECD Model Tax Convention.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Permanent establishments --- general
  rule

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} Except as provided by subsection 18(26), the net
  income or loss of a permanent establishment (other than any portion of
  that amount that is excluded from the financial accounting income of
  the permanent establishment because of subsection (2)) is not to be
  taken into account in determining the GloBE income or loss of the main
  entity in respect of the permanent establishment.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}No consolidation adjustments

  [(4)]{.lawlabel} The financial accounting income of a constituent
  entity is to include income, expenses, gains and losses (other than
  amounts excluded from GloBE income or loss because of subsection (5))
  arising from transactions between the constituent entity and any other
  group entity, other than any transactions to which an election under
  subsection 18(24) applies.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Profit and loss statement --- general
  rule

  [(5)]{.lawlabel} Unless otherwise required under this Act, no amount
  is included in computing the GloBE income or loss of a constituent
  entity if it is recognized outside of the profit and loss statement of
  the constituent entity's financial statements.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Financial accounting income ---
  flow-through entity

  [(6)]{.lawlabel} If a constituent entity is a particular flow-through
  entity, the following rules apply in determining the financial
  accounting income for a fiscal year of the particular flow-through
  entity and any other group entities in respect of the net income or
  loss of the particular flow-through entity:

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} amounts in respect of the particular flow-through
    entity's net income or loss that are attributable to ownership
    interests of persons or entities that are not group entities and
    that hold their ownership interests in the particular flow-through
    entity directly, or through a tax transparent structure, are not to
    be included in computing the financial accounting income of any
    group entity, unless

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} the particular flow-through entity is an ultimate
      parent entity, or

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} the particular flow-through entity is owned,
      directly or through a tax transparent structure, by an ultimate
      parent entity that is also a flow-through entity, in which case
      this paragraph does not apply to amounts in respect of the
      particular flow-through entity's net income or loss to the extent
      those amounts are attributable to persons or entities that hold
      their ownership interests in the particular flow-through entity
      through that ultimate parent entity;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} if a particular group entity has an ownership
    interest in the particular flow-through entity, an amount that, in
    the absence of this paragraph --- and, for greater certainty, after
    excluding amounts to which paragraph (a) applies and amounts
    allocated to a permanent establishment in accordance with paragraph
    (1)(b) --- would be included in the financial accounting income of
    the particular flow-through entity is excluded from its financial
    accounting income and included in the financial accounting income of
    the particular group entity in accordance with the particular group
    entity's ownership interest (determined having regard only to
    ownership interests held by group entities) in the particular
    flow-through entity, to the extent that

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} the particular flow-through entity is not an
      ultimate parent entity,

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} the particular flow-through entity is fiscally
      transparent in relation to the particular group entity,

    - [(iii)]{.lawlabel} the particular group entity is not a
      flow-through entity, other than a reverse hybrid entity or an
      ultimate parent entity, and

    - [(iv)]{.lawlabel} the particular group entity holds its ownership
      interest in the particular flow-through entity

      - [(A)]{.lawlabel} directly, or

      - [(B)]{.lawlabel} indirectly, through one or more entities (each
        referred to in this clause as an "intermediate owner"), if

        - [(I)]{.lawlabel} each intermediate owner is fiscally
          transparent in relation to the particular group entity,

        - [(II)]{.lawlabel} where the particular group entity is not a
          flow-through entity or is a flow-through entity that is an
          ultimate parent entity, there is no intermediate owner that
          both

          [1]{.lawlabel} is not a flow-through entity, and

          [2]{.lawlabel} would meet the conditions in this subparagraph
          and subparagraph (ii) if the references in those subparagraphs
          to the "particular group entity" were read as references to
          that intermediate owner, and

        - [(III)]{.lawlabel} where the particular group entity is a
          reverse hybrid entity, there is no intermediate owner that
          would meet the conditions in this subparagraph and
          subparagraphs (ii) and (iii) if the references in those
          subparagraphs to the "particular group entity" were read as
          references to that intermediate owner;

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} despite paragraph (b), if an amount of the net
    income or loss of the particular flow-through entity would, in the
    absence of this paragraph, be included under paragraph (b) in the
    financial accounting income of a particular group entity (referred
    to in this paragraph as the "lower-tier entity") that is a reverse
    hybrid entity, and would also be included in the financial
    accounting income of another group entity (referred to in this
    paragraph as the "upper-tier entity"), that is not a flow-through
    entity or that is a flow-through entity that is an ultimate parent
    entity, in relation to an ownership interest the upper-tier entity
    holds in the particular flow-through entity through the lower-tier
    entity, the amount is

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} included in the financial accounting income of
      the upper-tier entity, and

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} not included in the financial accounting income
      of the lower-tier entity; and

  - [(d)]{.lawlabel} any amount of the net income or loss of the
    particular flow-through entity that is not excluded in computing its
    financial accounting income because of paragraph (a) or (b), or
    subsection (3), is included in the financial accounting income of
    the particular flow-through entity.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Flow-through entity --- ownership
  interests

  [(7)]{.lawlabel} For the purpose of determining an entity's financial
  accounting income under subsection (6), a reference to an ownership
  interest refers only to an ownership interest that carries rights to
  profit.

#### [SUBDIVISION B]{.HLabel3}[Adjustments in Computing GloBE Income or Loss]{.HTitleText3} {#h-1474895 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Net tax expense

- **[[18]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-18 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} An
  amount is included or excluded, as the case may be, in computing GloBE
  income or loss of a constituent entity for a fiscal year in order to
  reverse any debits or credits in the constituent entity's financial
  accounting income in respect of

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} covered taxes (including, for greater certainty,
    any covered tax in respect of income that is excluded from the
    computation of GloBE income or loss);

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} to the extent it is not included in paragraph (a),
    any deferred tax asset attributable to a loss for the fiscal year;

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} any tax under an IIR or UTPR;

  - [(d)]{.lawlabel} any tax under a qualified domestic minimum top-up
    tax;

  - [(e)]{.lawlabel} any tax paid or accrued by an insurance company in
    respect of returns to policyholders; or

  - [(f)]{.lawlabel} any disqualified refundable imputation tax.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Purchase price accounting adjustments

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} GloBE income or loss, of a constituent entity for a
  fiscal year, excludes amounts in respect of any purchase price
  accounting adjustment reflected in the consolidated financial
  statements of the ultimate parent entity or the constituent entity's
  financial accounts, arising as a result of an entity becoming a group
  entity because of the acquisition of ownership interests in that
  entity by an existing group entity, unless

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the acquisition occurs before December 1, 2021; and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} it is not reasonably practicable to determine the
    constituent entity's financial accounting income in the absence of
    the adjustment.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Excluded dividends

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} In computing the GloBE income or loss, of a
  constituent entity for a fiscal year, the following rules apply in
  respect of excluded dividends:

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the constituent entity's GloBE income or loss for
    the fiscal year excludes any excluded dividends received or accrued
    by the constituent entity in the fiscal year; and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} if the filing constituent entity elects in respect
    of the constituent entity for a fiscal year,

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} for the purposes of this subsection and the
      definition [[excluded dividends]{.dfn}]{.DefinitionRef} in
      subsection 2(1), all portfolio holdings of the constituent entity
      are deemed to be short-term portfolio holdings, and

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} the election is a five-year election.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Excluded equity gains and losses

  [(4)]{.lawlabel} In computing the GloBE income or loss of a
  constituent entity for a fiscal year, the constituent entity's GloBE
  income or loss excludes any excluded equity gain or loss of the
  constituent entity for the fiscal year.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Insurance reserves

  [(5)]{.lawlabel} If a constituent entity is an insurance company, any
  expense in respect of the movement of insurance reserves of the entity
  is excluded in computing that entity's GloBE income or loss for the
  fiscal year to the extent that the movement is economically matched by

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} excluded dividends, net of any investment
    management fees, from a security held on behalf of a policyholder;
    or

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} excluded equity gains or losses from a security
    held on behalf of a policyholder.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Hedging currency risk --- election

  [(6)]{.lawlabel} If the filing constituent entity elects, in respect
  of a particular constituent entity for a fiscal year, the following
  rules apply:

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} any amount, in respect of a foreign exchange gain
    or loss, that is included in the particular constituent entity's
    financial accounting income for a fiscal year is deemed to be an
    excluded equity gain or loss of the particular constituent entity
    for the year, to the extent that

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} the gain or loss is

      - [(A)]{.lawlabel} in respect of a hedging instrument that hedges
        currency risk in respect of ownership interests (other than
        portfolio holdings) held by the particular constituent entity or
        another group entity, and

      - [(B)]{.lawlabel} recognized in other comprehensive income in the
        consolidated financial statements,

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} the hedging instrument is an effective hedge
      under the authorized financial accounting standard used in the
      preparation of the consolidated financial statements, and

    - [(iii)]{.lawlabel} the economic and accounting effect of the
      hedging instrument

      - [(A)]{.lawlabel} has not been transferred to another entity, if
        the particular constituent entity holds the hedging instrument,
        or

      - [(B)]{.lawlabel} has been transferred to the particular
        constituent entity, if the particular constituent entity does
