    paragraph as a "recharacterization year") in which a
    recharacterization of income (referred to in this paragraph as the
    "recharacterized income") described in paragraph (b) of the
    definition [[substitute loss carry-forward recapture
    amount]{.dfn}]{.DefinitionRef} in subsection 2(1) occurs, in an
    amount equal to the tax credits used by the constituent entity
    solely as a result of the recharacterization of income in the
    recharacterization year, to the extent that

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} the tax credits arise under the income tax laws
      of the particular jurisdiction in respect of tax paid to the
      government of a jurisdiction other than the particular
      jurisdiction,

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} the amount of the recharacterized income in the
      recharacterization year, together with the total amounts of
      recharacterized income in the preceding fiscal years, does not
      exceed the substitute loss carry-forward recapture amount, and

    - [(iii)]{.lawlabel} the tax credits are used to reduce or eliminate
      covered taxes that would otherwise be owing by the constituent
      entity under the income tax laws of the particular jurisdiction in
      respect of any amount of income that is included in computing the
      constituent entity's GloBE income or loss.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Deferred tax asset below minimum rate

  [(5)]{.lawlabel} The amount of a deferred tax asset recorded in the
  financial accounts of a constituent entity of an MNE group for a
  fiscal year is deemed to be the amount that it would be if the tax
  rate applicable in determining the amount of the deferred tax asset
  were the minimum rate, if the following conditions are met:

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the tax rate that would, in the absence of this
    subsection, be applicable in determining the amount of the deferred
    tax asset is less than the minimum rate; and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the deferred tax asset is a loss deferred tax asset
    that is attributable to a GloBE loss of the constituent entity for
    the fiscal year.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Recaptured deferred tax liability

  [(6)]{.lawlabel} For the purpose of applying subsection 31(1) in a
  fiscal year (referred to in this subsection as the "current fiscal
  year"), the adjusted covered taxes of a constituent entity for a
  fiscal year (referred to in this subsection as the "adjustment fiscal
  year") that is the fifth fiscal year preceding the current fiscal year
  are to be reduced to the extent of all or the portion of any deferred
  tax liability that is

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} included in determining the total deferred tax
    adjustment amount of the constituent entity for the adjustment
    fiscal year;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} not paid in any of the five fiscal years following
    the adjustment fiscal year; and

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} not a recapture exception accrual.

#### [SUBDIVISION D]{.HLabel3}[GloBE Loss Election]{.HTitleText3} {#h-1475445 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}GloBE loss deferred tax asset

**[[26]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-26 .sectionLabel}** If a filing constituent
entity of a particular MNE group elects, in respect of a jurisdiction
that does not have an eligible distribution tax system, for the GloBE
transition year of the particular MNE group in respect of the
jurisdiction, the following rules apply:

- [(a)]{.lawlabel} paragraphs (b) and (c) apply in respect of each
  fiscal year (referred to in this section as an "election year") of the
  particular MNE group that is the GloBE transition year or that occurs
  after the GloBE transition year but before the revocation year in
  respect of the election;

- [(b)]{.lawlabel} a GloBE loss deferred tax asset of the particular MNE
  group, in respect of the jurisdiction, is deemed to arise in any
  election year in which the particular MNE group has a net GloBE loss
  in respect of the jurisdiction, in an amount determined by the formula

  ::: {#1475450 .Paragraph}
  A × B

  where

  [A]{.dfn}
  :   is the absolute value of the net GloBE loss for the election year,
      and

  [B]{.dfn}
  :   is the minimum rate;
  :::

- [(c)]{.lawlabel} for the purposes of applying paragraph 22(2)(b) in
  respect of an election year (referred to in this paragraph as the
  "application year") in which the particular MNE group has net GloBE
  income in respect of the jurisdiction, a particular constituent entity
  that is located in the jurisdiction is considered to have reversed a
  particular GloBE loss deferred tax asset of the particular MNE group
  in respect of the jurisdiction that arose in a preceding election year
  (referred to in this paragraph as the "loss election year"), in an
  amount determined by the formula

  ::: {#1475455 .Paragraph}
  C x D ÷ E

  where

  [C]{.dfn}
  :   is the lesser of
      - [(i)]{.lawlabel} the amount determined by the formula

        ::: {#1475459 .NestedFormula}
        F × G − H

        where

        [F]{.dfn}
        :   is the particular MNE group's net GloBE income in respect of
            the jurisdiction for the application year,

        [G]{.dfn}
        :   is the minimum rate, and

        [H]{.dfn}
        :   is the total of all amounts each is which is the amount
            determined by the formula
            ::: {#1475464 .NestedFormula}
            K − L

            where

            [K]{.dfn}
            :   is the amount of another GloBE loss deferred tax asset
                of the particular MNE group, in respect of the
                jurisdiction, that arose in another election year
                preceding the loss election year, and

            [L]{.dfn}
            :   is the total of all amounts each of which is the portion
                of the other GloBE loss deferred tax asset that was
                considered to have been applied by a constituent entity
                in an election year preceding the application year, and
            :::
        :::

      - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} the amount determined by the formula

        ::: {#1475469 .NestedFormula}
        I − J

        where

        [I]{.dfn}
        :   is the particular GloBE loss deferred tax asset, and

        [J]{.dfn}
        :   is the total of all amounts each of which is all or the
            portion of the particular GloBE loss deferred tax asset that
            was considered to have been reversed by a constituent entity
            in an election year preceding the application year,
        :::

  [D]{.dfn}
  :   is the particular constituent entity's GloBE income for the
      application year, and

  [E]{.dfn}
  :   is the total of all amounts each of which is the GloBE income of
      any constituent entity of the particular MNE group that is located
      in the jurisdiction for the application year; and
  :::

- [(d)]{.lawlabel} if the ultimate parent entity of the particular MNE
  group is a flow-through entity that is located in the jurisdiction,
  paragraphs (b) and (c) are to be applied in relation to the
  constituent entities of the particular MNE group that are located in
  the jurisdiction as if the ultimate parent entity were

  - [(i)]{.lawlabel} the only constituent entity, of a separate MNE
    group, that is located in the jurisdiction, and

  - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} not a constituent entity, of the particular MNE
    group, that is located in the jurisdiction.

#### [SUBDIVISION E]{.HLabel3}[Post-Filing Adjustments and Tax Rate Changes]{.HTitleText3} {#h-1475478 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Adjustments to covered taxes for a prior
year

- **[[27]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-27 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} If, in
  a fiscal year (referred to in this subsection as the "current year"),
  there is an adjustment to the liability for covered taxes, recorded in
  the financial accounts, of a particular constituent entity of an MNE
  group for a prior fiscal year that are relevant in determining its
  adjusted covered taxes for the prior year, the following rules apply:

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} if the adjustment results in an increase in the
    liability, or an immaterial decrease in the liability,

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} where the adjustment relates to a change, in the
      particular constituent entity's deferred tax expense arising in
      the prior year, to which subsection (2) applies, the particular
      constituent entity's adjusted covered taxes for the fiscal year in
      which the deferred tax expense is reversed are to be increased to
      reflect the increase in the liability to the extent that it would
      not, in the absence of this subsection, already be reflected in
      the adjusted covered taxes of the particular constituent entity
      for any fiscal year, and

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} in any other case, the particular constituent
      entity's adjusted covered taxes for the current year are to be
      increased or reduced, as the case may be, to reflect the increase
      or immaterial decrease in the liability to the extent that it
      would not, in the absence of this subsection, already be reflected
      in the adjusted covered taxes of the particular constituent entity
      for any fiscal year;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} if the adjustment results in a decrease (other than
    an immaterial decrease) in the liability, for the purposes of
    applying subsection 31(1) in the current year

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} the particular constituent entity's adjusted
      covered taxes for the prior year are to be reduced to reflect the
      decrease in the liability,

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} the particular constituent entity's GloBE income
      or loss for the prior year is to be adjusted to the extent

      - [(A)]{.lawlabel} the decrease in the liability results from a
        reduction in the particular constituent entity's GloBE income or
        an increase in its GloBE loss for the prior year, and

      - [(B)]{.lawlabel} it is necessary to ensure there is no reduction
        in the MNE group's effective tax rate (determined without
        reference to this subsection), in respect of the jurisdiction in
        which the particular constituent entity is located, for the
        prior year, and

    - [(iii)]{.lawlabel} the GloBE income or loss, of the particular
      constituent entity and any other constituent entity of the MNE
      group that is located in the jurisdiction, for any fiscal year
      subsequent to the prior year, is to be adjusted, as necessary and
      appropriate, to reflect the decrease in the liability;

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} for the purposes of paragraphs (a) and (b), the
    adjustment results in an immaterial decrease if

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} the filing constituent entity of the MNE group
      elects under this paragraph in respect of the adjustment,

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} the adjustment results in a decrease in the
      liability, and

    - [(iii)]{.lawlabel} the amount determined by the following formula
      is less than €1 million:

      ::: {#1475495 .Subparagraph}
      A − B

      where

      [A]{.dfn}
      :   is the total of all amounts each of which is an adjustment in
          the current year resulting in a decrease to the liability for
          covered taxes, recorded in the financial accounts, of any
          constituent entity that is located in the jurisdiction for the
          prior year that are relevant in determining its adjusted
          covered taxes for the prior year, and

      [B]{.dfn}
      :   is the amount that would be determined for A if the reference
          in the description of A to "decrease" were read as a reference
          to "increase"; and
      :::

  - [(d)]{.lawlabel} to the extent the adjustment arises because a loss
    for tax purposes for any fiscal year following the prior year is
    carried back to reduce income for tax purposes for the prior year,
    the particular constituent entity is deemed to have a deferred tax
    asset that

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} arises in the current year,

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} is equal to the portion of the loss for tax
      purposes that reduces income for tax purposes for the prior year
      multiplied by the minimum rate, and

    - [(iii)]{.lawlabel} is treated as having been reversed in the prior
      year for the purposes of applying subsection 31(1) in respect of
      the prior year.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Adjustments --- deferred tax expense

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} For the purposes of subsection (1), a change, in a
  fiscal year (referred to in this subsection as the "current year"), in
  a constituent entity's deferred tax expense that arose in a prior
  fiscal year is to be treated as an adjustment, in the current year, to
  the constituent entity's liability for covered taxes, recorded in the
  financial accounts, for the prior year to the extent that

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the change results from a reduction, in the current
    year, to the rate of tax --- that was applicable in determining the
    deferred tax expense for the prior year --- below the minimum rate;
    and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the deferred tax expense was reflected in
    determining the constituent entity's total deferred tax adjustment
    amount for the prior year.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Idem

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} For the purposes of subsection (1),

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} a change, in a particular fiscal year, in a
    constituent entity's deferred tax expense that arose in a prior
    fiscal year is to be treated as an adjustment, in the fiscal year in
    which the deferred tax expense is reversed, to the constituent
    entity's liability for covered taxes, recorded in the financial
    accounts, for the prior year to the extent that

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} the change results from an increase, in the
      particular year, to the rate of tax --- that was applicable in
      determining the deferred tax expense for the prior year --- from a
      rate that was less than the minimum rate, and

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} the deferred tax expense was reflected in
      determining the constituent entity's total deferred tax adjustment
      amount for the prior year; and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} any portion of the change that is attributable to
    an increase of the rate of tax in excess of the minimum rate is to
    be disregarded in determining the adjustment under paragraph (a).

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Adjustments --- unpaid covered taxes

  [(4)]{.lawlabel} For the purposes of applying subsection 31(1) in a
  fiscal year (referred to in this subsection as the "current year"), a
  constituent entity's adjusted covered taxes for another fiscal year
  (referred to in this subsection as the "prior year") are to be
  adjusted to exclude any portion of the current tax expense that is
  recorded, in respect of covered taxes, in the constituent entity's
  financial accounts for the prior year and included in its adjusted
  covered taxes for the prior year, if

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the portion is not paid by the day (referred to in
    this subsection as the "specified day") that is three years after
    the last day of the prior year;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the specified day is within the current year; and

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} the portion is greater than €1 million.

#### [SUBDIVISION F]{.HLabel3}[Qualified Flow-Through Tax Benefits]{.HTitleText3} {#h-1475518 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Definitions

- **[[28]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-28 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} The
  following definitions apply in this section.

  [[adjusted investment amount]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[adjusted investment amount]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}, of an owner in
      respect of a qualified flow-through ownership interest for a
      fiscal year (referred to in this definition as the "determination
      year"), means the amount determined by the formula

      ::: {#1475523 .Definition}
      A − B − C

      where

      [A]{.dfn}
      :   is the investment amount of the owner in respect of the
          ownership interest;

      [B]{.dfn}
      :   is the total of all amounts each of which is the qualified
          flow-through tax benefits of the owner in respect of the
          ownership interest for a fiscal year preceding the
          determination year; and

      [C]{.dfn}
      :   is the total of all amounts each of which is the other
          flow-through amounts of the owner in respect of the ownership
          interest for the determination year or any preceding fiscal
          year. ([montant d'investissement ajusté]{.DefinedTermLink
          lang="fr"})
      :::

  [[excess benefits]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[excess benefits]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}, of an owner in respect of
      a proportional amortization method interest for a particular
      fiscal year, means the amount determined by the formula

      ::: {#1475529 .Definition}
      A + B − C

      where

      [A]{.dfn}
      :   is
          - [(a)]{.lawlabel} if the amount determined for C is equal to
            the proportional investment reduction amount of the owner in
            respect of the interest for the particular fiscal year, the
            other flow-through amounts of the owner in respect of the
            interest for the particular fiscal year, and

          - [(b)]{.lawlabel} in any other case, the lesser of

            - [(i)]{.lawlabel} the other flow-through amounts of the
              owner in respect of the interest for the particular fiscal
              year, and

            - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} the amount determined by the formula

              ::: {#1475536 .NestedFormula}
              D − E

              where

              [D]{.dfn}
              :   is the proportional amortization method investment
                  remaining amount of the owner in respect of the
                  interest for the fiscal year immediately preceding the
                  particular fiscal year, and

              [E]{.dfn}
              :   is the qualified flow-through tax benefits of the
                  owner in respect of the interest for the fiscal year
                  immediately preceding the particular fiscal year;
              :::

      [B]{.dfn}
      :   is the flow-through tax benefits of the owner in respect of
          the interest for the particular fiscal year, to the extent
          that the owner's current tax expense in respect of covered
          taxes in its financial accounts can reasonably be considered
          to have been increased (or to not have been reduced) solely
          because of the application of the proportional amortization
          method, or another financial accounting method that provides
          similar results as to current tax expense, in respect of the
          interest for the particular fiscal year; and

      [C]{.dfn}
      :   is the lesser of
          - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the amount determined for subparagraph
            (b)(ii) of the description of A, and

          - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the proportional investment reduction
            amount of the owner in respect of the interest for the
            particular fiscal year. ([bénéfices
            excédentaires]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})
      :::

  [[expected tax benefits ratio]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[expected tax benefits ratio]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}, of an owner
      in respect of a proportional amortization method interest for a
      fiscal year, means the amount determined by the formula

      ::: {#1475545 .Definition}
      A ÷ B

      where

      [A]{.dfn}
      :   is the flow-through tax benefits of the owner in respect of
          the interest for the fiscal year; and

      [B]{.dfn}
      :   is the total amount of flow-through tax benefits that the
          owner reasonably expects, at the time when the interest is
          first acquired, to receive in respect of the interest. ([ratio
          des avantages fiscaux attendus]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})
      :::

  [[flow-through tax benefits]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[flow-through tax benefits]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}, of an owner in
      respect of a qualified flow-through ownership interest for a
      fiscal year, means the total of all amounts each of which is

      - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the tax benefit, in respect of a tax credit
        (other than a qualified refundable tax credit or marketable
        transferable tax credit of the owner), received by the owner in
        respect of the ownership interest for the fiscal year; or

      - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the tax benefit, in respect of a tax loss,
        received by the owner in respect of the ownership interest for
        the fiscal year. ([avantages fiscaux
        intermédiaires]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[investment amount]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[investment amount]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}, of an owner in respect
      of an ownership interest, means the total fair market value of all
      consideration provided by the owner for the ownership
      interest. ([montant d'investissement]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[other flow-through amounts]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[other flow-through amounts]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}, of an owner in
      respect of a qualified flow-through ownership interest for a
      fiscal year, means the total of the following amounts received by
      the owner in respect of the ownership interest in the fiscal year:

      - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the tax benefits of qualified refundable tax
        credits and marketable transferable tax credits of the owner;

      - [(b)]{.lawlabel} distributions (including returns of capital);
        and

      - [(c)]{.lawlabel} proceeds from sales of all or part of the
        ownership interest. ([autres montants
        intermédiaires]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[proportional amortization method interest]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[proportional amortization method
      interest]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a qualified flow-through
      ownership interest in respect of which the owner uses the
      proportional amortization method for financial accounting
      purposes. ([participation selon la méthode d'amortissement
      proportionnelle]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[proportional amortization method investment remaining amount]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[proportional amortization method investment remaining
      amount]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}, of an owner in respect of a
      proportional amortization method interest for a fiscal year
      (referred to in this definition as the "determination year"),
      means the amount determined by the formula

      ::: {#1475559 .Definition}
      A − B

      where

      [A]{.dfn}
      :   is the investment amount of the owner in respect of the
          interest; and

      [B]{.dfn}
      :   is the total of all amounts each of which is the lesser of the
          following amounts for the determination year or a preceding
          fiscal year:
          - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the amount determined by the formula

            ::: {#1475564 .NestedFormula}
            C + D

            where

            [C]{.dfn}
            :   is
                - [(i)]{.lawlabel} in the case of the determination
                  year, nil, and

                - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} in any other case, the qualified
                  flow-through tax benefits of the owner in respect of
                  the interest for the fiscal year, and

            [D]{.dfn}
            :   is the other flow-through amounts of the owner in
                respect of the interest for the fiscal year, and
            :::

          - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the proportional investment reduction
            amount of the owner in respect of the interest for the
            fiscal year. ([montant de l'investissement résiduel selon la
            méthode d'amortissement proportionnelle]{.DefinedTermLink
            lang="fr"})
      :::

  [[proportional investment reduction amount]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[proportional investment reduction amount]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm},
      of an owner in respect of a proportional amortization method
      interest for a fiscal year, means the amount determined by the
      formula

      ::: {#1475572 .Definition}
      A × B

      where

      [A]{.dfn}
      :   is the investment amount of the owner in respect of the
          interest; and

      [B]{.dfn}
      :   is the expected tax benefits ratio of the owner in respect of
          the interest for the fiscal year. ([montant de la réduction de
          l'investissement proportionnel]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})
      :::

  [[qualified flow-through ownership interest]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[qualified flow-through ownership
      interest]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means, subject to subsection (5),
      an ownership interest in a particular tax transparent entity held
      by a constituent entity of an MNE group directly, or indirectly
      through one or more other tax transparent entities that are not
      constituent entities of the MNE group, if

      - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the assets, liabilities, income, expenses and
        cash flows of the particular tax transparent entity are not
        consolidated on a line-by-line basis in the consolidated
        financial statements of the ultimate parent entity of the MNE
        group; and

      - [(b)]{.lawlabel} at the time the ownership interest was acquired
        by the owner, the total return to be received by the owner
        (including distributions, the tax benefits of tax losses and the
        tax benefits of qualified refundable tax credits and marketable
        transferable tax credits of the owner received in respect of the
        ownership interest, but excluding the tax benefits of tax
        credits other than qualified refundable tax credits and
        marketable transferable tax credits of the owner) from its
        investment in the ownership interest could not reasonably have
        been expected to be greater than or equal to the investment
        amount of the owner in respect of the ownership
        interest. ([titre de participation intermédiaire
        admissible]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[qualified flow-through tax benefits]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[qualified flow-through tax benefits]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}, of an
      owner in respect of a qualified flow-through ownership interest
      for a fiscal year in which the ownership interest is subject to an
      election under subsection 18(7), means

      - [(a)]{.lawlabel} if the ownership interest is a proportional
        amortization method interest, the least of

        - [(i)]{.lawlabel} the flow-through tax benefits of the owner in
          respect of the ownership interest for the fiscal year,

        - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} the proportional amortization method
          investment remaining amount of the owner in respect of the
          ownership interest for the fiscal year, and

        - [(iii)]{.lawlabel} the amount, if any, by which the
          proportional investment reduction amount, of the owner in
          respect of the ownership interest for the fiscal year, exceeds
          the other flow-through amounts of the owner in respect of the
          ownership interest for the fiscal year; and

      - [(b)]{.lawlabel} in any other case, the lesser of

        - [(i)]{.lawlabel} the flow-through tax benefits of the owner in
          respect of the ownership interest for the fiscal year, and

        - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} the adjusted investment amount of the owner
          in respect of the ownership interest for the fiscal
          year. ([avantages fiscaux intermédiaires
          admissibles]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[recapture amount]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[recapture amount]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}, of an owner in respect
      of a qualified flow-through ownership interest for a particular
      fiscal year, means the lesser of

      - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the amount determined by the formula

        ::: {#1475589 .Paragraph}
        A − B + C

        where

        [A]{.dfn}
        :   is the other flow-through amounts of the owner in respect of
            the ownership interest for the particular fiscal year,

        [B]{.dfn}
        :   is
            - [(i)]{.lawlabel} if the ownership interest is a
              proportional amortization method interest,

              - [(A)]{.lawlabel} where the amount determined for C in
                the formula in the definition [[excess
                benefits]{.dfn}]{.DefinitionRef} in this subsection is
                equal to the proportional investment reduction amount of
                the owner in respect of the ownership interest for the
                particular fiscal year, the amount determined for A, and

              - [(B)]{.lawlabel} in any other case, the proportional
                amortization method investment remaining amount of the
                owner in respect of the ownership interest for the
                fiscal year immediately preceding the particular fiscal
                year, and

            - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} in any other case, the adjusted
              investment amount of the owner in respect of the ownership
              interest for the fiscal year immediately preceding the
              particular fiscal year, and

        [C]{.dfn}
        :   is
            - [(i)]{.lawlabel} where clause (i)(A) of the description of
              B applies, nil, and

            - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} in any other case, the qualified
              flow-through tax benefits of the owner in respect of the
              ownership interest for the fiscal year immediately
              preceding the particular fiscal year, and
        :::

      - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the amount determined by the formula

        ::: {#1475601 .Paragraph}
        D − E

        where

        [D]{.dfn}
        :   is the total of all amounts each of which is the qualified
            flow-through tax benefits of the owner in respect of the
            ownership interest for a fiscal year preceding the
            particular fiscal year, and

        [E]{.dfn}
        :   is the total of all amounts each of which is the recapture
            amount of the owner in respect of the ownership interest for
            a fiscal year preceding the particular fiscal
            year. ([montant de récupération]{.DefinedTermLink
            lang="fr"})
        :::

  [[tax benefit]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[tax benefit]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}, of an owner in respect of a
      qualified flow-through ownership interest

      - [(a)]{.lawlabel} includes, in respect of a tax credit,

        - [(i)]{.lawlabel} a refund received by the owner in respect of
          the tax credit, and

        - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} any amount received by the owner in exchange
          for the transfer of the tax credit; and

      - [(b)]{.lawlabel} means, in respect of a tax loss attributed to
        the owner in respect of the ownership interest, the product
        obtained by multiplying the amount of the tax loss by the
        statutory income tax rate applicable to the owner in the
        jurisdiction where it is located. ([avantage
        fiscal]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Adjusted covered taxes --- additions
  and reductions

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} If an owner holds a qualified flow-through ownership
  interest in another entity, the adjusted covered taxes of the owner
  for a fiscal year are deemed to be the amount that would, in the
  absence of section 8, be determined by the formula

  ::: {#1475612 .Subsection}
  A + B − C

  where

  [A]{.dfn}
  :   is the adjusted covered taxes of the owner for the fiscal year, as
      determined without regard to any amount determined for B or C in
      respect of the ownership interest for the fiscal year;

  [B]{.dfn}
  :   is
      - [(a)]{.lawlabel} if the ownership interest is a proportional
        amortization method interest, the amount determined by the
        formula

        ::: {#1475617 .NestedFormula}
        D − E

        where

        [D]{.dfn}
        :   is the qualified flow-through tax benefits, if any, of the
            owner in respect of the ownership interest for the fiscal
            year, and

        [E]{.dfn}
        :   is the amount by which the owner's current tax expense in
            respect of covered taxes in its financial accounts can
            reasonably be considered to have been increased (or to not
            have been reduced) in respect of those qualified
            flow-through tax benefits solely because of the application
            of the proportional amortization method, or another
            financial accounting method that provides similar results as
            to current tax expense, in respect of the ownership interest
            for the fiscal year, and
        :::

      - [(b)]{.lawlabel} in any other case, the lesser of

        - [(i)]{.lawlabel} the qualified flow-through tax benefits, if
          any, of the owner in respect of the ownership interest for the
          fiscal year, and

        - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} the amount by which the owner's current tax
          expense in respect of covered taxes in its financial accounts
          can reasonably be considered to have been reduced in the
          fiscal year because of those qualified flow-through tax
          benefits; and

  [C]{.dfn}
  :   is the amount determined by the formula
      ::: {#1475625 .NestedFormula}
      F + G

      where

      [F]{.dfn}
      :   is
          - [(a)]{.lawlabel} if the ownership interest is a proportional
            amortization method interest, the excess benefits of the
            owner in respect of the ownership interest for the fiscal
            year, and

          - [(b)]{.lawlabel} in any other case, the amount by which the
            flow-through tax benefits of the owner in respect of the
            ownership interest for the fiscal year exceeds the adjusted
            investment amount of the owner in respect of the ownership
            interest for the fiscal year, except to the extent that the
            owner's current tax expense in respect of covered taxes in
            its financial accounts can reasonably be considered to have
            been reduced in the fiscal year because of those
            flow-through tax benefits, and

      [G]{.dfn}
      :   is the recapture amount of the owner in respect of the
          ownership interest for the fiscal year.
      :::
  :::

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Proportional amortization method
  interest --- election

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} A qualified flow-through ownership interest held by
  an owner that is a constituent entity of an MNE group that would not,
  in the absence of this subsection, be a proportional amortization
  method interest is irrevocably deemed to be a proportional
  amortization method interest if the filing constituent entity of the
  MNE group so elects in the later of

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the fiscal year in which the ownership interest is
    first acquired by the owner; and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the first fiscal year in which

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} the owner is subject to a qualified IIR,
      qualified UTPR or qualified domestic minimum top-up tax, and

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} the owner is a constituent entity of the MNE
      group.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Deemed ownership interests ---
  debt-accounted investments

  [(4)]{.lawlabel} For the purposes of this section, an investment,
  other than an ownership interest, in a particular tax transparent
  entity held by a particular constituent entity of an MNE group
  directly, or indirectly through one or more other tax transparent
  entities that are not constituent entities of the MNE group, is deemed
  to be an ownership interest held by the particular constituent entity
  in the particular tax transparent entity if the investment

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} is treated as an equity interest under the income
    tax laws of the jurisdiction in which the particular constituent
    entity is located; and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} would be treated as an equity interest under an
    authorized financial accounting standard of the jurisdiction in
    which the particular tax transparent entity operates.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Qualified flow-through ownership
  interest --- anti-avoidance

  [(5)]{.lawlabel} An ownership interest in an entity held by an owner
  that would, in the absence of this subsection, be a qualified
  flow-through ownership interest is not a qualified flow-through
  ownership interest if

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the owner

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} does not have a [bona fide]{.otherLang lang="la"}
      economic interest in the entity, or

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} is protected from the loss of its investment in
      the entity; or

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the tax laws of the jurisdiction in which the
    entity operates make receipt of the tax benefit in respect of any
    tax credits of the entity by the owner conditional on the owner or
    the developer of the project that originates the tax credits being
    subject to a qualified IIR, qualified UTPR or qualified domestic
    minimum top-up tax.

### [DIVISION 4]{.HLabel2}[Computation of Effective Tax Rate and Top-up Amount]{.HTitleText2} {#h-1475647 .Subheading}

#### [SUBDIVISION A]{.HLabel3}[Effective Tax Rate]{.HTitleText3} {#h-1475648 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Definition of [[effective tax
rate]{.dfn}]{.DefinitionRef}

- **[[29]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-29 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} The
  [[effective tax rate]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}, of an MNE group for a
  jurisdiction for a fiscal year, means

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} if the net GloBE income of the MNE group for the
    jurisdiction for the fiscal year is nil, the minimum rate; and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} in any other case, the result (expressed as a
    percentage rounded to four decimal points) of the formula

    ::: {#1475654 .Paragraph}
    (A − B) ÷ C

    where

    [A]{.dfn}
    :   is the jurisdictional adjusted covered taxes of the MNE group
        for the jurisdiction for the fiscal year,

    [B]{.dfn}
    :   is the lesser of
        - [(i)]{.lawlabel} the amount determined for A, and

        - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} the excess negative tax expense of the MNE
          group for the jurisdiction for the fiscal year, and

    [C]{.dfn}
    :   is the net GloBE income of the MNE group for the jurisdiction
        for the fiscal year.
    :::

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Definition of [[net GloBE
  income]{.dfn}]{.DefinitionRef}

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} The [[net GloBE income]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}, of an
  MNE group for a jurisdiction for a fiscal year, means the amount
  determined by the formula

  ::: {#1475663 .Subsection}
  A − B

  where

  [A]{.dfn}
  :   is the total of all amounts each of which is the GloBE income for
      the fiscal year of a standard constituent entity of the MNE group
      that is located in the jurisdiction; and

  [B]{.dfn}
  :   is the total of all amounts each of which is the GloBE loss for
      the fiscal year of a standard constituent entity of the MNE group
      that is located in the jurisdiction.
  :::

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Definition of [[jurisdictional adjusted
  covered taxes]{.dfn}]{.DefinitionRef}

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} The [[jurisdictional adjusted covered
  taxes]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}, of an MNE group for a jurisdiction for a
  fiscal year, means the total of all amounts each of which is the
  adjusted covered taxes for the fiscal year of a standard constituent
  entity of the MNE group that is located in the jurisdiction.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Definition of [[excess negative tax
  expense]{.dfn}]{.DefinitionRef}

  [(4)]{.lawlabel} The [[excess negative tax
  expense]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}, of an MNE group for a jurisdiction for
  a particular fiscal year, means the amount determined by the formula

  ::: {#1475671 .Subsection}
  A + B − C

  where

  [A]{.dfn}
  :   is the absolute value of the total of all amounts each of which is
      the jurisdictional adjusted covered taxes of the MNE group for the
      jurisdiction for a fiscal year
      - [(a)]{.lawlabel} that precedes the particular fiscal year,

      - [(b)]{.lawlabel} in which the amount that would, in the absence
        of section 8, be the net GloBE income of the MNE group for the
        jurisdiction is nil or greater, and

      - [(c)]{.lawlabel} in which the jurisdictional adjusted covered
        taxes of the MNE group for the jurisdiction are less than nil;

  [B]{.dfn}
  :   is the total of all amounts each of which is the amount by which
      the excess negative tax expense top-up amount, of a standard
      constituent entity of the MNE group that is located in the
      jurisdiction, for a fiscal year that precedes the particular
      fiscal year, is reduced because of an election under subsection
      31(5); and

  [C]{.dfn}
  :   is the total of all amounts each of which is the amount determined
      for B in the formula in paragraph (1)(b) in respect of the MNE
      group for the jurisdiction for a fiscal year, that precedes the
      particular fiscal year, in which that paragraph applies.
  :::

#### [SUBDIVISION B]{.HLabel3}[Top-up Amount of a Standard Constituent Entity]{.HTitleText3} {#h-1475679 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Definition of [[top-up
amount]{.dfn}]{.DefinitionRef}

- **[[30]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-30 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} The
  [[top-up amount]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}, of a particular standard
  constituent entity of an MNE group that is located in a jurisdiction
  for a fiscal year, means

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} if the net GloBE income of the MNE group for the
    jurisdiction for the fiscal year is greater than nil, the amount
    determined by the formula

    ::: {#1475684 .Paragraph}
    A × B ÷ C

    where

    [A]{.dfn}
    :   is the jurisdictional top-up amount of the MNE group for the
        jurisdiction for the fiscal year,

    [B]{.dfn}
    :   is the GloBE income of the particular standard constituent
        entity for the fiscal year, and

    [C]{.dfn}
    :   is the total of all amounts each of which is the GloBE income,
        of a standard constituent entity of the MNE group that is
        located in the jurisdiction, for the fiscal year; and
    :::

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} if the net GloBE income of the MNE group for the
    jurisdiction for the fiscal year is nil, the total of

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} the allocated adjustment top-up amount of the
      particular standard constituent entity for the fiscal year, and

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} where the jurisdictional adjusted covered taxes
      of the MNE group for the jurisdiction for the fiscal year is less
      than nil, the excess negative tax expense top-up amount of the
      particular standard constituent entity for the fiscal year.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Definition of [[jurisdictional top-up
  amount]{.dfn}]{.DefinitionRef}

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} The [[jurisdictional top-up
  amount]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}, of an MNE group for a jurisdiction for a
  fiscal year, means the amount determined by the formula

  ::: {#1475694 .Subsection}
  A × B + C − D

  where

  [A]{.dfn}
  :   is the top-up percentage of the MNE group for the jurisdiction for
      the fiscal year;

  [B]{.dfn}
  :   is the excess profit of the MNE group for the jurisdiction for the
      fiscal year;

  [C]{.dfn}
  :   is the total of all amounts each of which is an adjustment top-up
      amount of the MNE group for the jurisdiction for the fiscal year;
      and

  [D]{.dfn}
  :   is the total amount of tax payable for a fiscal year, in respect
      of the standard constituent entities of the MNE group that are
      located in the jurisdiction, under a qualified domestic minimum
      top-up tax of the jurisdiction.
  :::

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Definition of [[top-up
  percentage]{.dfn}]{.DefinitionRef}

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} The [[top-up percentage]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}, of an
  MNE group for a jurisdiction for a fiscal year, means the percentage
  point difference determined by the formula

  ::: {#1475702 .Subsection}
  A − B

  where

  [A]{.dfn}
  :   is the minimum rate; and

  [B]{.dfn}
  :   is the effective tax rate of the MNE group for the jurisdiction
      for the fiscal year.
  :::

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Definition of [[excess
  profit]{.dfn}]{.DefinitionRef}

  [(4)]{.lawlabel} The [[excess profit]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}, of an MNE
  group for a jurisdiction for a fiscal year, means the amount
  determined by the formula

  ::: {#1475708 .Subsection}
  A − B

  where

  [A]{.dfn}
  :   is the net GloBE income of the MNE group for the jurisdiction for
      the fiscal year; and

  [B]{.dfn}
  :   is the substance-based income exclusion amount of the MNE group
      for the jurisdiction for the fiscal year.
  :::

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Definition of [[allocated adjustment
  top-up amount]{.dfn}]{.DefinitionRef}

  [(5)]{.lawlabel} The [[allocated adjustment top-up
  amount]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} of a particular standard constituent
  entity of an MNE group that is located in a jurisdiction for a
  particular fiscal year means the total of all amounts each of which is
  determined by the formula

  ::: {#1475714 .Subsection}
  (A − B) × C ÷ D

  where

  [A]{.dfn}
  :   is a particular adjustment top-up amount of the MNE group for the
      jurisdiction for the particular fiscal year;

  [B]{.dfn}
  :   is the amount determined by the formula
      ::: {#1475718 .NestedFormula}
      E × A ÷ (F + G)

      where

      [E]{.dfn}
      :   is the total amount of tax payable for the particular fiscal
          year, in respect of the standard constituent entities of the
          MNE group that are located in the jurisdiction, under a
          qualified domestic minimum top-up tax of the jurisdiction,

      [F]{.dfn}
      :   is the total of all adjustment top-up amounts of the MNE group
          for the jurisdiction for the particular fiscal year, and

      [G]{.dfn}
      :   is the jurisdictional excess negative tax expense top-up
          amount of the MNE group for the jurisdiction for the
          particular fiscal year, if the formula in that definition in
          subsection 31(4) were read without reference to C;
      :::

  [C]{.dfn}
  :   is the GloBE income of the particular standard constituent entity,
      for the adjustment year to which the particular adjustment top-up
      amount relates, as adjusted because of the application of any ETR
      adjustment provision in the particular fiscal year or any
      preceding fiscal year; and

  [D]{.dfn}
  :   is the total of all amounts each of which is the GloBE income, for
      the adjustment year, of a standard constituent entity of the MNE
      group that is located in the jurisdiction, as adjusted because of
      the application of any ETR adjustment provision in the particular
      fiscal year or any preceding fiscal year.
  :::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Adjustment top-up amount

- **[[31]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-31 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} For
  the purposes of determining an adjustment top-up amount of an MNE
  group for a jurisdiction for a fiscal year, if an ETR adjustment
  provision applies in the fiscal year to adjust, for the purposes of
  this subsection, the GloBE income or loss or adjusted covered taxes of
  any standard constituent entity of the MNE group that is located in
  the jurisdiction, or the jurisdictional adjusted covered taxes of the
  MNE group for the jurisdiction, for an adjustment year, the following
  rules apply:

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the jurisdictional top-up amount of the MNE group
    for the jurisdiction for the adjustment year (referred to in this
    subsection as the "recalculated jurisdictional top-up amount") is
    calculated taking into account the adjustment; and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the amount determined by the following formula is
    an adjustment top-up amount of the MNE group for the jurisdiction
    for the fiscal year:

    ::: {#1475730 .Paragraph}
    (A − B) × C

    where

    [A]{.dfn}
    :   is the recalculated jurisdictional top-up amount of the MNE
        group for the jurisdiction for the adjustment year,

    [B]{.dfn}
    :   is the jurisdictional top-up amount of the MNE group for the
        jurisdiction for the adjustment year, determined without
        reference to paragraph (a), and

    [C]{.dfn}
    :   is
        - [(i)]{.lawlabel} the disposition recapture ratio, if the
          adjustment is an adjustment to adjusted covered taxes under
          subsection 37(5), and

        - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} 1, in any other case.
    :::

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Definition of [[excess negative tax
  expense top-up amount]{.dfn}]{.DefinitionRef}

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} Subject to subsection (5), the [[excess negative tax
  expense top-up amount]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} of a particular negative
  tax expense constituent entity of an MNE group that is located in a
  jurisdiction for a fiscal year means the amount determined by the
  formula

  ::: {#1475739 .Subsection}
  A × B ÷ C

  where

  [A]{.dfn}
  :   is the jurisdictional excess negative tax expense top-up amount of
      the MNE group for the jurisdiction for the fiscal year;

  [B]{.dfn}
  :   is the amount determined by the formula
      ::: {#1475743 .NestedFormula}
      D × E − F

      where

      [D]{.dfn}
      :   is the GloBE income or loss of the particular negative tax
          expense constituent entity for the fiscal year,

      [E]{.dfn}
      :   is the minimum rate, and

      [F]{.dfn}
      :   is the adjusted covered taxes of the particular negative tax
          expense constituent entity for the fiscal year; and
      :::

  [C]{.dfn}
  :   is the total of all amounts each of which is the amount determined
      for B for a negative tax expense constituent entity of the MNE
      group for the jurisdiction for the fiscal year.
  :::

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Definition of [[negative tax expense
  constituent entity]{.dfn}]{.DefinitionRef}

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} A [[negative tax expense constituent
  entity]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}, in respect of an MNE group for a fiscal
  year, means a standard constituent entity of the MNE group whose
  adjusted covered taxes for the fiscal year are less than each of the
  following amounts:

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} nil; and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the amount that would, in the absence of section 8,
    be determined by the formula

    ::: {#1475753 .Paragraph}
    A × B

    where

    [A]{.dfn}
    :   is the GloBE income or loss of the entity for the fiscal year,
        and

    [B]{.dfn}
    :   is the minimum rate.
    :::

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Definition of [[jurisdictional excess
  negative tax expense top-up amount]{.dfn}]{.DefinitionRef}

  [(4)]{.lawlabel} The [[jurisdictional excess negative tax expense
  top-up amount]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}, of an MNE group for a
  jurisdiction for a fiscal year in which its net GloBE income for the
  jurisdiction is nil and its jurisdictional adjusted covered taxes for
  the jurisdiction is less than nil, means the amount determined by the
  formula

  ::: {#1475759 .Subsection}
  A − B − C

  where

  [A]{.dfn}
  :   is the amount that would, in the absence of section 8, be
      determined by the formula
      ::: {#1475762 .NestedFormula}
      D × E

      where

      [D]{.dfn}
      :   is the total of all amounts each of which is the GloBE income
          or loss for the fiscal year of a standard constituent entity
          of the MNE group that is located in the jurisdiction, and

      [E]{.dfn}
      :   is the minimum rate;
      :::

  [B]{.dfn}
  :   is the jurisdictional adjusted covered taxes of the MNE group for
      the jurisdiction for the fiscal year; and

  [C]{.dfn}
  :   is the amount determined by the formula
      ::: {#1475768 .NestedFormula}
      F × (A − B) ÷ (A − B + G)

      where

      [F]{.dfn}
      :   is the total amount of tax payable for the fiscal year, in
          respect of the standard constituent entities of the MNE group
          that are located in the jurisdiction, under a qualified
          domestic minimum top-up tax of the jurisdiction, and

      [G]{.dfn}
      :   is the total of all adjustment top-up amounts of the MNE group
          for the jurisdiction for the fiscal year.
      :::
  :::

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Election --- excess negative tax
  expense carry-forward

  [(5)]{.lawlabel} If the filing constituent entity of an MNE group
  elects for a jurisdiction for a fiscal year, the excess negative tax
  expense top-up amount of a standard constituent entity of the MNE
  group that is located in the jurisdiction for the fiscal year is
  deemed to be the greater of

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} nil; and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the portion of the excess negative tax expense
    top-up amount, determined without reference to this subsection, that
    can reasonably be considered to relate to a tax loss that was
    carried back from the fiscal year to another fiscal year of the MNE
    group ending before the fiscal year and deducted under the income
    tax laws of the jurisdiction in the other fiscal year.

#### [SUBDIVISION C]{.HLabel3}[Substance-based Income Exclusion]{.HTitleText3} {#h-1475776 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Definition of [[substance-based income
exclusion amount]{.dfn}]{.DefinitionRef}

- **[[32]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-32
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} Subject to subsection (16), the
  [[substance-based income exclusion amount]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} of an
  MNE group for a jurisdiction for a fiscal year means the lesser of

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the total of all amounts each of which is
    determined by the formula

    ::: {#1475781 .Paragraph}
    A + B

    where

    [A]{.dfn}
    :   is, subject to subsection 49(1), 5% of the eligible payroll
        costs of a standard constituent entity of the MNE group that is
        located in the jurisdiction for the fiscal year, and

    [B]{.dfn}
    :   is, subject to subsection 49(2), 5% of the eligible tangible
        asset amount of the standard constituent entity for the fiscal
        year, and
    :::

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the portion of the amount described in
    paragraph (a) that the filing constituent entity of the MNE group
    specifies as the substance-based income exclusion amount of the MNE
    group for the jurisdiction in the GIR in respect of the MNE group
    for the fiscal year.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Definition of [[eligible payroll
  costs]{.dfn}]{.DefinitionRef}

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} Subject to subsections (5) to (7) and subparagraph
  38(1)(d)(i), the [[eligible payroll costs]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} of a
  particular standard constituent entity of an MNE group for a fiscal
  year, means all payroll costs (other than excluded costs of the
  particular standard constituent entity) in respect of eligible
  employees of any standard constituent entity of the MNE group,
  incurred

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} if the particular standard constituent entity is a
    permanent establishment, by the main entity in respect of the
    permanent establishment and reflected in the separate financial
    accounts for the fiscal year of the permanent establishment, as
    adjusted in accordance with clause 17(1)(b)(ii)(B) or subsection
    17(2); and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} in any other case, by the particular standard
    constituent entity and reflected in the particular standard
    constituent entity's financial accounts for the fiscal year.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Definition of [[excluded
  costs]{.dfn}]{.DefinitionRef}

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} [[Excluded costs]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}, of a
  particular standard constituent entity of an MNE group, means any
  costs if

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} it is not the case that

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} the costs are payable primarily in respect of
      work done in the course of the ordinary operating activities of
      any standard constituent entity of the MNE group, and

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} that work is done in the jurisdiction in which
      the particular standard constituent entity is located;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the costs are capitalized and included in the
    carrying value of an asset used to calculate the eligible tangible
    asset amount of any constituent entity of the MNE group, as
    determined without reference to subsection (11);

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} where the particular standard constituent entity is
    the main entity in respect of a permanent establishment, the costs
    are reflected in the separate financial accounts of the permanent
    establishment as adjusted in accordance with clause 17(1)(b)(ii)(B)
    or subsection 17(2);

  - [(d)]{.lawlabel} the costs are core international shipping costs, or
    ancillary international shipping costs that can reasonably be
    considered to be attributable to qualified ancillary international
    shipping income, of any constituent entity of the MNE group; or

  - [(e)]{.lawlabel} where the particular standard constituent entity is
    a permanent establishment, the costs can reasonably be considered to
    be attributable to income that is excluded from the GloBE income or
    loss of the particular standard constituent entity under paragraph
    17(6)(a) or subsection 20(4).

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Interjurisdictional employee deeming
  rule

  [(4)]{.lawlabel} For the purposes of subparagraph (3)(a)(ii), if an
  eligible employee of a particular standard constituent entity of an
  MNE group spends more than 50% of the time that the eligible employee
  is doing work (referred to in this subsection as the "ordinary course
  work") in the course of the ordinary operating activities of the
  standard constituent entities of the MNE group in a fiscal year, in
  the jurisdiction in which the particular standard constituent entity
  is located, all the ordinary course work is deemed to be done in the
  jurisdiction for the fiscal year.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Eligible payroll costs --- flow-through
  entity allocation

  [(5)]{.lawlabel} If a particular amount would, in the absence of this
  subsection, be included in the eligible payroll costs of a constituent
  entity that is a flow-through entity for a fiscal year, the following
  rules apply:

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} if the constituent entity is an ultimate parent
    entity, the portion of the particular amount that is determined by
    the following formula is excluded from the eligible payroll costs of
    the constituent entity for the fiscal year:

    ::: {#1475804 .Paragraph}
    A × B ÷ C

    where

    [A]{.dfn}
    :   is the particular amount,

    [B]{.dfn}
    :   is the amount of any reduction to the GloBE income of the
        constituent entity for the fiscal year under subsection 20(1),
        and

    [C]{.dfn}
    :   is the GloBE income of the constituent entity for the fiscal
        year before the reduction referred to in the description of B;
        and
    :::

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} in any other case,

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} the eligible payroll costs of the constituent
      entity for the fiscal year are deemed to be nil, and

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} if all or any portion of the net income or loss
      of the constituent entity is allocated to one or more of its
      constituent entity-owners (each referred to in this subparagraph
      as a "relevant owner") under paragraphs 17(6)(b) and (c) for the
      fiscal year,

      - [(A)]{.lawlabel} the particular amount is allocated to each
        relevant owner in the same proportion as the financial
        accounting income is allocated to that relevant owner, and

      - [(B)]{.lawlabel} if a relevant owner is located in the same
        jurisdiction as the constituent entity, any portion of the
        particular amount that is allocated to the relevant owner under
        clause (A) is included in the eligible payroll costs of the
        relevant owner for the fiscal year.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Eligible payroll costs --- deductible
  dividend regime

  [(6)]{.lawlabel} If a particular amount would, in the absence of this
  subsection, be included in the eligible payroll costs, for a fiscal
  year, of a constituent entity that is subject to a deductible dividend
  regime, the portion of the particular amount determined by the
  following formula is excluded from the eligible payroll costs of the
  constituent entity for the fiscal year:

  ::: {#1475816 .Subsection}
  A × B ÷ C

  where

  [A]{.dfn}
  :   is the particular amount;

  [B]{.dfn}
  :   is the total amount of any reductions to the GloBE income of the
      constituent entity for the fiscal year under subsections 21(1) to
      (3); and

  [C]{.dfn}
  :   is the GloBE income of the constituent entity for the fiscal year
      before the reductions referred to in the description of B.
  :::

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Eligible payroll costs --- taxable
  distribution method

  [(7)]{.lawlabel} If a particular amount would, in the absence of this
  subsection, be included in the eligible payroll costs, for a fiscal
  year, of a constituent entity an ownership interest in which is
  subject to an election under subsection 42(2), the portion of the
  particular amount determined by the following formula is excluded from
  the eligible payroll costs of the constituent entity for the fiscal
  year:

  ::: {#1475823 .Subsection}
  A × B ÷ C

  where

  [A]{.dfn}
  :   is the particular amount;

  [B]{.dfn}
  :   is the portion of the GloBE income of the constituent entity for
      the fiscal year that is attributable to ownership interests of the
      constituent entity in respect of which an election under
      subsection 42(2) applies for the fiscal year; and

  [C]{.dfn}
  :   is the GloBE income of the constituent entity for the fiscal year.
  :::

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Definition of [[eligible
  employee]{.dfn}]{.DefinitionRef}

  [(8)]{.lawlabel} An [[eligible employee]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}, of a
  standard constituent entity of an MNE group, means an individual who
  is

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} regarded as an employee (or, if a distinction is
    made, a part-time employee) of the standard constituent entity under
    the laws of the jurisdiction in which it is located; or

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} an independent contractor who acts under the
    direction and control of one or more standard constituent entities
    of the MNE group.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Definition of [[eligible tangible asset
  amount]{.dfn}]{.DefinitionRef}

  [(9)]{.lawlabel} Subject to subsections (10) to (13) and subparagraph
  38(1)(d)(ii), the [[eligible tangible asset
  amount]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} of a standard constituent entity for a
  fiscal year means the amount determined by the formula

  ::: {#1475834 .Subsection}
  (A + B) ÷ 2

  where

  [A]{.dfn}
  :   is the total of all amounts each of which is the carrying value of
      an eligible tangible asset of the standard constituent entity at
      the start of the fiscal year; and

  [B]{.dfn}
  :   is the total of all amounts each of which is the carrying value of
      an eligible tangible asset of the standard constituent entity at
      the end of the fiscal year.
  :::

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Eligible tangible asset amount --- main
  entity limitation

  [(10)]{.lawlabel} The eligible tangible asset amount for a fiscal year
  of a standard constituent entity that is a main entity in respect of a
  permanent establishment is not to include any amount in respect of the
  carrying value of an eligible tangible asset held by the standard
  constituent entity to the extent that the eligible tangible asset is
  reflected in the separate financial accounts of the permanent
  establishment as adjusted in accordance with clause 17(1)(b)(ii)(B) or
  subsection 17(2).

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Eligible tangible asset amount ---
  flow-through entity allocation

  [(11)]{.lawlabel} If an amount would, in the absence of this
  subsection, be included in the eligible tangible asset amount, for a
  fiscal year, of a constituent entity that is a flow-through entity,
  the rules in subsection (5) apply in respect of the amount, except
  that any reference in that subsection to "eligible payroll costs" is
  to be read as a reference to "eligible tangible asset amount".

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Eligible tangible asset amount ---
  deductible dividend regime

  [(12)]{.lawlabel} If an amount would, in the absence of this
  subsection, be included in the eligible tangible asset amount, for a
  fiscal year, of a constituent entity that is subject to a deductible
  dividend regime, subsection (6) applies in respect of the amount,
  except that any reference in that subsection to "eligible payroll
  costs" is to be read as a reference to "eligible tangible asset
  amount".

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Eligible tangible asset amount ---
  taxable distribution method

  [(13)]{.lawlabel} If an amount would, in the absence of this
  subsection, be included in the eligible tangible asset amount, for a
  fiscal year, of a constituent entity an ownership interest in which is
  subject to an election under subsection 42(2), subsection (7) applies
  in respect of the amount, except that any reference in that subsection
  to "eligible payroll costs" is to be read as a reference to "eligible
  tangible asset amount".

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Definition of [[eligible tangible
  asset]{.dfn}]{.DefinitionRef}

  [(14)]{.lawlabel} An [[eligible tangible asset]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}
  of a constituent entity at a particular time means an asset that is

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} held at the particular time by

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} if the constituent entity is a permanent
      establishment, the main entity in respect of the permanent
      establishment, and

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} in any other case, the constituent entity;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} if the constituent entity is a permanent
    establishment, reflected in the separate financial accounts of the
    permanent establishment as adjusted in accordance with clause
    17(1)(b)(ii)(B) or subsection 17(2) for the fiscal year that
    includes the particular time;

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} any of the following:

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} property, plant or equipment,

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} natural resources,

    - [(iii)]{.lawlabel} if the constituent entity is the lessee, a
      right-of-use asset in respect of the lease of a tangible asset,

    - [(iv)]{.lawlabel} if the constituent entity is the lessor,
      property, all or any portion of which is leased out to a person
      under an operating lease, or

    - [(v)]{.lawlabel} a licence from, or similar arrangement with, a
      government if

      - [(A)]{.lawlabel} the licence or arrangement is for the use of
        immovable property or exploitation of natural resources, and

      - [(B)]{.lawlabel} the use of the property or exploitation of the
        resources entails significant investment in tangible assets; and

  - [(d)]{.lawlabel} none of the following:

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} property that is held for sale, lease or
      investment, or, if the constituent entity is the lessee, that is
      leased out to a person under a finance lease,

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} a tangible asset used in the generation of core
      international shipping income in the fiscal year that includes the
      particular time,

    - [(iii)]{.lawlabel} if the constituent entity is a permanent
      establishment, an asset to the extent it can reasonably be
      considered to be used in the generation of income that is excluded
      from the GloBE income or loss of the constituent entity under
      paragraph 17(6)(a) or subsection 20(4), or

    - [(iv)]{.lawlabel} a right-of-use asset in respect of the lease of
      a tangible asset that is regularly leased several times to
      different lessees during the fiscal year, if the average lease
      period, including any renewals and extensions, with respect to
      lessees of the asset is 30 days or less (referred to in this
      subsection and subsection (15) as a "short-term rental asset").

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Eligible tangible asset --- carrying
  value

  [(15)]{.lawlabel} For the purposes of subsection (9), the carrying
  value of an eligible tangible asset of a constituent entity of an MNE
  group at a particular time is the carrying value of the eligible
  tangible asset recorded, or calculated as it would have been if it
  were recorded, at the particular time for the purposes of preparing
  the consolidated financial statements of the ultimate parent entity of
  the MNE group, as reduced (to the extent it has not already been so
  reduced) by the amount

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} of any positive difference between the carrying
    value so determined and the carrying value of the same eligible
    tangible asset at the time it was most recently acquired by the
    constituent entity, to the extent such difference is solely
    attributable to one or more revaluations of the asset;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} of any accumulated depreciation, amortization,
    depletion or impairment loss in respect of the asset;

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} of the reversal of any impairment loss to the
    extent that the reversal exceeds the impairment loss;

  - [(d)]{.lawlabel} recorded by another constituent entity of the MNE
    group in its financial accounts as a right-of-use asset in respect
    of the eligible tangible asset (other than a short-term rental
    asset);

  - [(e)]{.lawlabel} of undiscounted value of any payments remaining
    due, in respect of the eligible tangible asset (other than a
    short-term rental asset), from a lessee of the eligible tangible
    asset that is not a constituent entity of the MNE group;

  - [(f)]{.lawlabel} if the eligible tangible asset is used in the
    generation of qualified ancillary international shipping income of
    the constituent entity for the fiscal year that includes the
    particular time, determined by the formula

    ::: {#1475873 .Paragraph}
    A × B ÷ C

    where

    [A]{.dfn}
    :   is the carrying value of the eligible tangible asset, determined
        without reference to this paragraph and paragraph (g),

    [B]{.dfn}
    :   is the qualified ancillary international shipping income of the
        constituent entity for the fiscal year, and

    [C]{.dfn}
    :   is the ancillary international shipping income of the
        constituent entity for the fiscal year; and
    :::

  - [(g)]{.lawlabel} determined, for the fiscal year that includes the
    particular time, by the formula

    ::: {#1475879 .Paragraph}
    D × E ÷ F

    where

    [D]{.dfn}
    :   is the carrying value of the eligible tangible asset, determined
        without reference to this paragraph,

    [E]{.dfn}
    :   is
        - [(i)]{.lawlabel} nil, if the eligible tangible asset --- or
          the underlying leased tangible asset, where the condition in
          subparagraph (14)(c)(iii) is satisfied, or the underlying
          immovable property or natural resources, where the condition
          in subparagraph (14)(c)(v) is satisfied --- is located in the
          jurisdiction in which the constituent entity is located for
          more than 50% of the days in the fiscal year (referred to in
          this paragraph as the "qualifying days") that paragraphs
          (14)(a) and, if the constituent entity is a permanent
          establishment, (14)(b) are satisfied in respect of the
          eligible tangible asset, and

        - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} the number of qualifying days in which the
          eligible tangible asset is not located in the jurisdiction in
          which the constituent entity is located, in any other case,
          and

    [F]{.dfn}
    :   is the number of qualifying days.
    :::

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Election not to apply exclusion for a
  jurisdiction

  [(16)]{.lawlabel} The substance-based income exclusion amount of an
  MNE group for a jurisdiction for a fiscal year is deemed to be nil if
  the filing constituent entity of the MNE group elects under this
  subsection not to apply the substance-based income exclusion for the
  jurisdiction for the fiscal year.

#### [SUBDIVISION D]{.HLabel3}[De Minimis Jurisdiction Exclusion]{.HTitleText3} {#h-1475888 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}[De minimis]{.otherLang lang="la"}
jurisdiction exclusion

- **[[33]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-33
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} Despite sections 30 and 34, the
  top-up amount for a particular fiscal year of each constituent entity
  (referred to in this section as an "eligible constituent entity") of
  an MNE group that is located in a jurisdiction (other than a stateless
  constituent entity, investment entity or insurance investment entity)
  is deemed to be the amount that would be the top-up amount of the
  eligible constituent entity if the top-up percentage that is relevant
  in calculating that top-up amount were nil, if

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the filing constituent entity of the MNE group
    elects in respect of the jurisdiction for the particular fiscal
    year;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the amount determined by the following formula is
    less than €10 million:

    ::: {#1475894 .Paragraph}
    (A + B + C) ÷ D

    where

    [A]{.dfn}
    :   is the jurisdictional GloBE revenue of the MNE group for the
        jurisdiction for the particular fiscal year,

    [B]{.dfn}
    :   is the jurisdictional GloBE revenue of the MNE group for the
        jurisdiction for the fiscal year (referred to in this subsection
        as the "first preceding year") immediately preceding the
        particular fiscal year,

    [C]{.dfn}
    :   is the jurisdictional GloBE revenue of the MNE group for the
        jurisdiction for the fiscal year (referred to in this subsection
        as the "second preceding year") immediately preceding the first
        preceding year, and

    [D]{.dfn}
    :   is
        - [(i)]{.lawlabel} 3, if the jurisdictional GloBE revenue of the
          MNE group for the jurisdiction is greater than nil --- or at
          least one eligible constituent entity of the MNE group that is
          located in the jurisdiction has a GloBE loss --- for both the
          first preceding year and the second preceding year,

        - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} 2, if the jurisdictional GloBE revenue of
          the MNE group for the jurisdiction is greater than nil --- or
          at least one eligible constituent entity of the MNE group that
          is located in the jurisdiction has a GloBE loss --- for only
          one of the first preceding year and the second preceding year,
          and

        - [(iii)]{.lawlabel} 1, in any other case; and
    :::

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} the amount determined by the following formula is
    less than €1 million:

    ::: {#1475904 .Paragraph}
    (E + F + G) ÷ D

    where

    [E]{.dfn}
    :   is the jurisdictional GloBE income or loss of the MNE group for
        the jurisdiction for the particular fiscal year,

    [F]{.dfn}
    :   is the jurisdictional GloBE income or loss of the MNE group for
        the jurisdiction for the first preceding year, and

    [G]{.dfn}
    :   is the jurisdictional GloBE income or loss of the MNE group for
        the jurisdiction for the second preceding year.
    :::

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Definition of [[jurisdictional GloBE
  revenue]{.dfn}]{.DefinitionRef}

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} The [[jurisdictional GloBE
  revenue]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}, of an MNE group for a jurisdiction for
  a fiscal year, means the total of all amounts determined by the
  formula

  ::: {#1475911 .Subsection}
  A × B ÷ 365

  where

  [A]{.dfn}
  :   is the financial accounting revenue of an eligible constituent
      entity of the MNE group that is located in the jurisdiction for
      the fiscal year, subject to any adjustments made in determining
      the GloBE income or loss of the eligible constituent entity for
      the fiscal year under Division 2, subsection 25(6) and paragraphs
      27(1)(b) and 37(4)(b) and (5)(b), other than any adjustment
      - [(a)]{.lawlabel} in respect of expenses, or

      - [(b)]{.lawlabel} that is a decrease to the eligible constituent
        entity's GloBE income or loss for the fiscal year resulting from
        the application of an ETR adjustment provision in a subsequent
        fiscal year; and

  [B]{.dfn}
  :   is the number of days in the fiscal year.
  :::

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Definition of [[financial accounting
  revenue]{.dfn}]{.DefinitionRef}

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} The [[financial accounting
  revenue]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}, of an eligible constituent entity of an
  MNE group for a fiscal year, means the revenue amount for the fiscal
  year determined for the entity in the manner set out, and in
  accordance with the principles reflected, in the definition
  [[financial accounting income]{.dfn}]{.DefinitionRef} in subsection
  17(1), with such modifications as the context requires.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Definition of [[jurisdictional GloBE
  income or loss]{.dfn}]{.DefinitionRef}

  [(4)]{.lawlabel} The [[jurisdictional GloBE income or
  loss]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}, of an MNE group for a jurisdiction for a
  fiscal year, means the amount that would, in the absence of section 8,
  be determined by the formula

  ::: {#1475921 .Subsection}
  A × B ÷ 365

  where

  [A]{.dfn}
  :   is the total of all amounts each of which is the GloBE income or
      loss for the fiscal year of an eligible constituent entity of the
      MNE group that is located in the jurisdiction determined without
      taking into account any adjustment that is a decrease to the
      eligible constituent entity's GloBE income or loss for the fiscal
      year resulting from the application of an ETR adjustment provision
      in a subsequent fiscal year; and

  [B]{.dfn}
  :   is the number of days in the fiscal year.
  :::

#### [SUBDIVISION E]{.HLabel3}[Top-up Amount of a Minority-Owned Constituent Entity]{.HTitleText3} {#h-1475925 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Deeming rule --- minority-owned subgroup

- **[[34]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-34 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} For
  the purposes of sections 29 to 31 and subsections 32(1), (2), (4), (8)
  to (10) and (16), each minority-owned constituent entity that would,
  in the absence of this section, be a constituent entity of an MNE
  group is deemed not to be a constituent entity of the MNE group.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Minority-owned constituent entity ---
  top-up amount

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} The top-up amount of a minority-owned constituent
  entity (other than an investment entity or an insurance investment
  entity) of a minority-owned subgroup that is located in a jurisdiction
  for a fiscal year is the amount that would be determined under
  subsection 30(1) if the references in sections 29 to 31 and
  subsections 32(1) to (4), (8) to (10) and (16) to "MNE group" and
  "standard constituent entity" were read as references to
  "minority-owned subgroup" and "minority-owned constituent entity
