    other constituent entity for the fiscal year is included, for the
    purposes of applying the transitional safe harbour, in the total
    revenues and profit (loss) before income tax of the MNE group for
    the jurisdiction where the particular standard constituent entity is
    located for the fiscal year.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}[De minimis]{.otherLang lang="la"}
  threshold test

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} The [de minimis]{.otherLang lang="la"} threshold test
  is met, in respect of an MNE group for a jurisdiction in a fiscal
  year, if

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the total revenues of the MNE group for the
    jurisdiction for the year is less than €10 million; and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the profit (loss) before income tax of the MNE
    group for the jurisdiction for the year is less than €1 million.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Simplified effective tax rate test

  [(4)]{.lawlabel} The simplified effective tax rate test is met, in
  respect of an MNE group for a jurisdiction in a fiscal year, if the
  simplified effective tax rate of the standard constituent entities of
  the MNE group that are located in that jurisdiction is

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} in the case of a fiscal year beginning before
    January 1, 2025, at least 15%;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} in the case of a fiscal year beginning in 2025, at
    least 16%; or

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} in the case of a fiscal year beginning after
    December 31, 2025, at least 17%.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Simplified effective tax rate

  [(5)]{.lawlabel} The simplified effective tax rate of the standard
  constituent entities of an MNE group that are located in a
  jurisdiction in a fiscal year is the result (expressed as a percentage
  rounded to four decimal points) of the formula

  ::: {#1476337 .Subsection}
  A ÷ B

  where

  [A]{.dfn}
  :   is the total of all amounts each of which is the qualifying income
      tax expense of a standard constituent entity of the MNE group that
      is located in the jurisdiction for the fiscal year; and

  [B]{.dfn}
  :   is the profit (loss) before income tax of the MNE group for the
      jurisdiction for the fiscal year.
  :::

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Routine profits test

  [(6)]{.lawlabel} The routine profits test is met, in respect of an MNE
  group for a jurisdiction in a fiscal year, if

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the qualified substance-based income exclusion
    amount for the jurisdiction for the year is equal to or greater than
    the profit (loss) before income tax of the MNE group for the
    jurisdiction for the year; or

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the profit (loss) before income tax of the MNE
    group for the jurisdiction for the year is nil or reflects an
    overall loss.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Application to joint ventures

  [(7)]{.lawlabel} For the purposes of applying this section to a joint
  venture or joint venture group

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} subsection (2) is to be read without reference to
    its paragraph (b);

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} references to "MNE group" are to be read as
    references to "joint venture group", other than in the expression
    "filing constituent entity of an MNE group", paragraphs (2)(c)
    and (d) and subsection (8);

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} references to "standard constituent entity" are to
    be read as references to "joint venture entity", other than in
    subsection (8);

  - [(d)]{.lawlabel} references to "MNE group's qualified
    country-by-country report" are to be read as references to "joint
    venture group's qualified financial statements";

  - [(e)]{.lawlabel} in applying the definition [[qualified financial
    statements]{.dfn}]{.DefinitionRef} in subsection (1) in relation to
    the joint venture group

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} the reference to "ultimate parent entity" in
      subparagraph (a)(i) of that definition is to be read as a
      reference to "joint venture", and

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} the reference to "the financial accounts of the
      standard constituent entity that are used for preparation of the
      MNE group's country-by-country report" in subparagraph (a)(iii) of
      that definition is to be read as a reference to "the financial
      accounts of the joint venture entity that would be used if a
      qualified country-by-country report had been prepared in respect
      of the joint venture group"; and

  - [(f)]{.lawlabel} the definition [[qualified substance-based income
    exclusion amount]{.dfn}]{.DefinitionRef} in subsection (1) is to be
    read as "means the substance-based income exclusion amount of the
    joint venture group for the jurisdiction for the fiscal year".

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Application to minority-owned
  constituent entities

  [(8)]{.lawlabel} For the purposes of this section, a minority-owned
  constituent entity is treated as a standard constituent entity of an
  MNE group.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Tax-neutral ultimate parent entities

  [(9)]{.lawlabel} No election may be made under subsection (2) in
  respect of the jurisdiction where the ultimate parent entity of an MNE
  group is located for a fiscal year if

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the ultimate parent entity is a flow-through
    entity; and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} it is not the case that all the ownership interests
    in the ultimate parent entity are held by qualified persons.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Tax-neutral ultimate parent entities
  --- adjustments

  [(10)]{.lawlabel} If an ultimate parent entity of an MNE group is a
  flow-through entity or subject to a deductible dividend regime,

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the profit (loss) before income tax of the MNE
    group for the jurisdiction where the ultimate parent entity is
    located is to be adjusted to exclude any profit (loss) before income
    tax of the ultimate parent entity to the extent that such amount is

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} attributable to an ownership interest held by a
      qualified person, or

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} distributed in respect of an ownership interest
      held by a qualified person; and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the qualifying income tax expense of any
    constituent entity of the MNE group is adjusted to exclude any taxes
    in respect of the profit (loss) before income tax that is excluded
    under paragraph (a).

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Investment entities as standard
  constituent entities

  [(11)]{.lawlabel} For the purposes of this section, an investment
  entity or insurance investment entity is treated as a standard
  constituent entity of an MNE group for a fiscal year, if

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the investment entity is included in the MNE group;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} all the constituent entities of the MNE group with
    direct ownership interests in the investment entity or insurance
    investment entity are located in the same jurisdiction as the
    investment entity or insurance investment entity; and

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} no election under subsection 41(1) or 42(2) is in
    effect in respect of the investment entity or insurance investment
    entity for the fiscal year.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Investment entities --- amounts
  reflected in owners' jurisdiction

  [(12)]{.lawlabel} If subsection (11) does not apply in respect of an
  investment entity or insurance investment entity that is a constituent
  entity of an MNE group for a fiscal year, for the purposes of applying
  subsections (3) to (6), the profit (loss) before income tax and total
  revenues of the MNE group attributable to, and qualifying income tax
  expense of, the investment entity or insurance investment entity are
  to be reflected in the jurisdictions where its direct constituent
  entity-owners are located, in proportion to their respective ownership
  interest in the investment entity or insurance investment entity.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Location of investment entities

  [(13)]{.lawlabel} For the purposes of this section, an investment
  entity or insurance investment entity is considered to be located in
  the jurisdiction where it is resident for the purposes of a qualified
  country-by-country report.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Hybrid arbitrage arrangements

  [(14)]{.lawlabel} For the purposes of applying subsections (3) to (6)
  in respect of an MNE group for a jurisdiction for a fiscal year,

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the profit (loss) before income tax of the MNE
    group for the jurisdiction for the year is to exclude any expense or
    loss arising as a result of

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} a deduction/non-inclusion arrangement, or

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} a duplicate loss arrangement; and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the qualifying income tax expense of a standard
    constituent entity of the MNE group that is located in the
    jurisdiction for the fiscal year is to exclude any income tax
    expense arising as a result of a duplicate tax recognition
    arrangement.

### [DIVISION 9]{.HLabel2}[Transition Rules]{.HTitleText2} {#h-1476382 .Subheading}

#### [SUBDIVISION A]{.HLabel3}[Tax Attributes on Transition]{.HTitleText3} {#h-1476383 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Transition --- deferred tax assets and
liabilities

- **[[48]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-48
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} Subject to subsections (2), (3) and
  (5), in determining the total deferred tax adjustment amount of a
  constituent entity of an MNE group that is located in a jurisdiction
  for the GloBE transition year of the MNE group in respect of the
  jurisdiction and each subsequent fiscal year in which the constituent
  entity is included in the MNE group,

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} each deferred tax asset and deferred tax liability
    that is reflected in the constituent entity's financial accounts at
    the beginning of the GloBE transition year is to be taken into
    account to the extent of

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} if the constituent entity can demonstrate that a
      deferred tax asset is attributable to a loss of the constituent
      entity that would have been taken into account in determining its
      GloBE income or loss had the GloBE income or loss been determined
      for the prior fiscal year in which the loss arose, the amount that
      the deferred tax asset would be if the rate of tax that applied in
      determining the deferred tax asset were the minimum rate,

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} if a deferred tax asset is in respect of a tax
      credit, the lesser of

      - [(A)]{.lawlabel} the amount of the deferred tax asset, and

      - [(B)]{.lawlabel} the amount determined by the formula

        ::: {#1476392 .Clause}
        A ÷ B × C

        where

        [A]{.dfn}
        :   is the amount of the deferred tax asset,

        [B]{.dfn}
        :   is the applicable tax rate in the fiscal year immediately
            preceding the GloBE transition year, and

        [C]{.dfn}
        :   is the minimum rate, and
        :::

    - [(iii)]{.lawlabel} in any other case, the lesser of

      - [(A)]{.lawlabel} the amount of the deferred tax asset or
        deferred tax liability, as the case may be, and

      - [(B)]{.lawlabel} the amount that the deferred tax asset or
        deferred tax liability would be if the tax rate applicable in
        determining the deferred tax asset or deferred tax liability
        were the minimum rate;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} if a deferred tax asset referred to in
    paragraph (a) is in respect of a credit and the tax rate applicable
    to the constituent entity in a subsequent fiscal year (referred to
    in this paragraph as the "re-application year") is different from
    the applicable tax rate in the fiscal year immediately preceding the
    GloBE transition year,

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} the formula in clause (a)(ii)(B) must be
      re-applied to the outstanding balance of the tax credit in the
      constituent entity's financial accounts at the beginning of the
      re-application year to determine the revised deferred tax asset,

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} the change in the amount of the deferred tax
      asset resulting from the re-application of the formula must not be
      treated as deferred tax expense included in the computation of the
      total deferred tax adjustment amount of the constituent entity in
      the re-application year or any other fiscal year, and

    - [(iii)]{.lawlabel} the deferred tax expense for the re-application
      year and subsequent fiscal years must be determined based on the
      amount of the reversal of the revised deferred tax asset; and

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} paragraph 25(2)(a) and subsection 25(6) do not
    apply in respect of the deferred tax assets and deferred tax
    liabilities referred to in paragraph (a).

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Excluded deferred tax assets

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} In applying subsection (1), a deferred tax asset that
  is reflected in a constituent entity's financial accounts at the
  beginning of the GloBE transition year of the MNE group in respect of
  the jurisdiction in which the constituent entity is located is not to
  be taken into account in determining the constituent entity's total
  deferred tax adjustment amount if the deferred tax asset arises

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} as a result of a transaction that occurs on or
    after December 1, 2021 and before the GloBE transition year; and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} in respect of

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} an amount that

      - [(A)]{.lawlabel} is taken into account in computing the
        constituent entity's income or profits in respect of which an
        entity is subject to an income or profits tax imposed by the
        government of the jurisdiction in which the constituent entity
        is located, and

      - [(B)]{.lawlabel} would not be taken into account in computing
        the constituent entity's GloBE income or loss for the GloBE
        transition year if the amount arose in the GloBE transition
        year, or

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} an amount that

      - [(A)]{.lawlabel} is not included in the constituent entity's
        income or profits in respect of which an entity is subject to an
        income or profits tax imposed by the government of the
        jurisdiction in which the constituent entity is located, and

      - [(B)]{.lawlabel} would be included in computing the constituent
        entity's GloBE income or loss for the GloBE transition year if
        the amount arose in the GloBE transition year.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Valuation adjustments and accounting
  recognition adjustments

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} In applying subsection (1), the impact of any
  valuation adjustment or accounting recognition adjustment with respect
  to the amount of a deferred tax asset is not to be taken into account.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Asset transfers before transferor
  transition year --- conditions

  [(4)]{.lawlabel} Subsection (5) applies in respect of an asset if

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} an entity (referred to in this section as the
    "transferor") transfers the asset --- on or after December 1, 2021,
    and before the earlier of the QDMTT transition year and the GloBE
    transition year (referred to in this section as the "transferor
    transition year") of its MNE group in respect of the jurisdiction in
    which it is located --- to another entity (referred to in this
    section as the "transferee");

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the asset is not manufactured, nor of a class or
    description sold, in the course of carrying on a trade by the
    transferor or the transferee; and

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} had this Act applied immediately before the
    transfer, the transferor and transferee would have been constituent
    entities of the same MNE group.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Asset transfers before transferor
  transition year --- consequences

  [(5)]{.lawlabel} Subject to subsection (9), if this subsection applies
  in respect of an asset

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the carrying value of the asset at the beginning of
    the transferor transition year is deemed to be equal to the carrying
    value of the asset to the transferor immediately before the transfer
    referred to in paragraph (4)(a), adjusted for

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} any subsequent capitalized expenditure incurred
      in respect of the asset before the transferor transition year, and

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} the portion of any amortization and depreciation
      of the asset that would have been recognized before the transferor
      transition year if any increase in the carrying value resulting
      from the transfer had not occurred; and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} in applying subsection (1),

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} any deferred tax assets and deferred tax
      liabilities in respect of the asset that are reflected in any
      entity's financial accounts and that result from the transfer are
      not to be taken into account in determining the total deferred tax
      adjustment amount of any constituent entity, except to the extent
      provided under subparagraph (ii), and

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} a deferred tax asset of the transferee that is
      described in subparagraph (i) may be taken into account but, for
      this purpose, the amount of that deferred tax asset is deemed to
      be equal to the lesser of the amount actually recorded in the
      transferee's financial accounts as a result of the transfer and
      the total of the following amounts, adjusted for any capitalized
      expenditure, amortization and depreciation in respect of the asset
      that are referred to in subparagraphs (a)(i) and (ii):

      - [(A)]{.lawlabel} the portion of the current tax expense for a
        fiscal year of the transferor in respect of covered taxes that
        is demonstrated to have resulted from the transfer, and

      - [(B)]{.lawlabel} the portion of the current tax expense for a
        fiscal year of any other entity in respect of covered taxes that
        is demonstrated to have resulted from the transfer and that
        would have been allocated to the transferor under section 24 if

        - [(I)]{.lawlabel} the transfer had occurred in the transferor
          transition year, and

        - [(II)]{.lawlabel} section 24 were read without reference to
          paragraph 24(4)(c).

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Deemed transfer

  [(6)]{.lawlabel} For the purposes of subsection (4), an asset is
  deemed to be transferred by a transferor to a transferee on or after
  December 1, 2021, and before the transferor transition year, if a
  transaction or arrangement is entered into during that period in
  respect of the asset that

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} would not, in the absence of this subsection, be
    regarded as a transfer of the asset by a transferor to a transferee
    on or after December 1, 2021, and before the transferor transition
    year; and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} is accounted for by one or more parties involved in
    the transaction or arrangement in the same or a similar manner to a
    change in ownership of the asset, or as giving rise to an asset.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Interpretation --- single-entity
  transactions

  [(7)]{.lawlabel} For greater certainty, the transaction or arrangement
  referred to in subsection (6) may involve only one entity ---
  including where it results in an increase to the carrying value of an
  asset, or in the creation or increase of a deferred tax asset --- in
  which case the transferor and transferee referred to in subsection (5)
  are the same entity.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Interpretation --- pre-existing
  deferred tax assets and liabilities

  [(8)]{.lawlabel} For greater certainty, for the purposes of
  subparagraph (5)(b)(i), any deferred tax assets or deferred tax
  liabilities in respect of an asset reflected in the transferee's
  financial accounts immediately after the transfer and that were
  reflected in the transferor's financial accounts immediately before
  the transfer are not deferred tax assets or deferred tax liabilities
  resulting from the transfer.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Election --- non-application of
  paragraph (5)(a) and subparagraph (5)(b)(ii)

  [(9)]{.lawlabel} Paragraph (5)(a) and subparagraph (5)(b)(ii) do not
  apply in respect of an asset transferred by a transferor to a
  transferee that is a constituent entity of an MNE group, if

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the filing constituent entity of the MNE group
    elects; and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the following condition is met:

    ::: {#1476446 .Paragraph}
    A + B ≥ C × D

    where

    [A]{.dfn}
    :   is the sum of the amounts described in clauses (5)(b)(ii)(A)
        and (B) in respect of the transfer of the asset,

    [B]{.dfn}
    :   is the amount of any deferred tax asset in respect of a tax loss
        of the transferor that is demonstrated to have been reversed or
        to not have been created solely because any gain arising from
        the transfer of the asset was included in the taxable income of
        the transferor,

    [C]{.dfn}
    :   is the minimum rate, and

    [D]{.dfn}
    :   is the difference between local tax basis in the asset to the
        transferee immediately after the transfer and the carrying value
        of the asset at the beginning of the transferor transition year
        as would be determined under paragraph (5)(a) in the absence of
        this subsection.
    :::

#### [SUBDIVISION B]{.HLabel3}[Transitional Rates for the Substance-based Income Exclusion]{.HTitleText3} {#h-1476452 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Transitional rates for the
substance-based income exclusion

- **[[49]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-49 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} For
  the purposes of applying subsection 32(1) in respect of a fiscal year
  beginning before 2032, the reference in that provision to "5% of the
  eligible payroll costs" is to be read as

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} "10% of the eligible payroll costs" if the fiscal
    year begins in 2023;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} "9.8% of the eligible payroll costs" if the fiscal
    year begins in 2024;

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} "9.6% of the eligible payroll costs" if the fiscal
    year begins in 2025;

  - [(d)]{.lawlabel} "9.4% of the eligible payroll costs" if the fiscal
    year begins in 2026;

  - [(e)]{.lawlabel} "9.2% of the eligible payroll costs" if the fiscal
    year begins in 2027;

  - [(f)]{.lawlabel} "9% of the eligible payroll costs" if the fiscal
    year begins in 2028;

  - [(g)]{.lawlabel} "8.2% of the eligible payroll costs" if the fiscal
    year begins in 2029;

  - [(h)]{.lawlabel} "7.4% of the eligible payroll costs" if the fiscal
    year begins in 2030;

  - [(i)]{.lawlabel} "6.6% of the eligible payroll costs" if the fiscal
    year begins in 2031; and

  - [(j)]{.lawlabel} "5.8% of the eligible payroll costs" if the fiscal
    year begins in 2032.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Idem

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} For the purposes of applying subsection 32(1) in
  respect of a fiscal year beginning before 2032, the reference in that
  provision to "5% of the eligible tangible asset amount" is to be read
  as

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} "8% of the eligible tangible asset amount" if the
    fiscal year begins in 2023;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} "7.8% of the eligible tangible asset amount" if the
    fiscal year begins in 2024;

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} "7.6% of the eligible tangible asset amount" if the
    fiscal year begins in 2025;

  - [(d)]{.lawlabel} "7.4% of the eligible tangible asset amount" if the
    fiscal year begins in 2026;

  - [(e)]{.lawlabel} "7.2% of the eligible tangible asset amount" if the
    fiscal year begins in 2027;

  - [(f)]{.lawlabel} "7% of the eligible tangible asset amount" if the
    fiscal year begins in 2028;

  - [(g)]{.lawlabel} "6.6% of the eligible tangible asset amount" if the
    fiscal year begins in 2029;

  - [(h)]{.lawlabel} "6.2% of the eligible tangible asset amount" if the
    fiscal year begins in 2030;

  - [(i)]{.lawlabel} "5.8% of the eligible tangible asset amount" if the
    fiscal year begins in 2031; and

  - [(j)]{.lawlabel} "5.4% of the eligible tangible asset amount" if the
    fiscal year begins in 2032.

## [PART 3]{.HLabel1}[Domestic Minimum Top-up Tax]{.HTitleText1} {#h-1476478 .Part}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Interpretation

**[[50]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-50 .sectionLabel}** This Part comprises
provisions that

- [(a)]{.lawlabel} are intended to implement a qualified domestic
  minimum top-up tax that has qualified domestic minimum top-up tax safe
  harbour status (including transitional qualified status); and

- [(b)]{.lawlabel} are to be interpreted consistently with the
  requirements outlined in the GloBE Commentary.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Domestic minimum top-up tax

- **[[51]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-51 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} A
  particular person must pay a tax in respect of a constituent entity of
  an MNE group for a fiscal year in an amount equal to the domestic
  top-up amount of the constituent entity for the fiscal year, if

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the constituent entity is located in Canada for the
    fiscal year;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the MNE group is a qualifying MNE group for the
    fiscal year; and

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} the particular person

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} is the constituent entity, or

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} if the constituent entity is not a person,
      would, under the relevant assumptions, include in its income for
      the purposes of Part I of the [Income Tax Act](/eng/acts/I-3.3)
      income of the constituent entity for the fiscal year.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Relevant assumptions

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} For the purposes of subparagraph (1)(c)(ii), the
  relevant assumptions are that

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the constituent entity referred to in that
    subparagraph has income for the fiscal year that would be included
    in computing its income for the purposes of Part I of the [Income
    Tax Act](/eng/acts/I-3.3) if it were a person resident in Canada;
    and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the person referred to in paragraph (1)(c) is
    resident in Canada for the purposes of the [Income Tax
    Act](/eng/acts/I-3.3).

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Definition of [[domestic top-up
amount]{.dfn}]{.DefinitionRef}

- **[[52]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-52
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} Subject to subsection 53(3), the
  [[domestic top-up amount]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}, of a constituent
  entity of an MNE group that is located in Canada for a fiscal year,
  means the amount that would be the top-up amount of the constituent
  entity for the fiscal year determined under subsection 30(1), 34(2),
  35(1) or 36(2) (as adjusted under any other applicable provision of
  Part 2), as the case may be, if that amount (and any amounts or
  results relevant to the determination of that amount) were required to
  be determined and

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the adjusted covered taxes of the constituent
    entities of the MNE group that are located in Canada were determined
    without reference to any covered taxes that would otherwise be
    allocable to those constituent entities under any of subsections
    24(1) and (4) to (6) (except, in the case of subsection 24(6), any
    amount of those covered taxes imposed under the [Income Tax
    Act](/eng/acts/I-3.3));

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the total amount of tax payable for any fiscal year
    in respect of the constituent entities of the MNE group that are
    located in Canada, under a qualified domestic minimum top-up tax,
    were deemed to be nil;

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} if the fiscal year is, or is subsequent to, the
    QDMTT transition year of the MNE group in respect of Canada but
    precedes the GloBE transition year of the MNE group in respect of
    Canada, all references in section 26 and subsections 48(1) and (2)
    to "GloBE transition year" were read as references to "QDMTT
    transition year";

  - [(d)]{.lawlabel} if the fiscal year is, or is subsequent to, the
    GloBE transition year of the MNE group in respect of Canada and the
    QDMTT transition year of the MNE group in respect of Canada preceded
    the GloBE transition year,

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} the following amounts in respect of the period
      before the start of the GloBE transition year (such period
      referred to in this paragraph as the "refresh period") were deemed
      to be nil:

      - [(A)]{.lawlabel} for the purposes of subsection 25(6), all or
        any portion of a deferred tax liability (other than a recapture
        exception accrual) in respect of which

        - [(I)]{.lawlabel} a positive amount was included in determining
          the total deferred tax adjustment amount of the constituent
          entity for a fiscal year that is included in the refresh
          period, and

        - [(II)]{.lawlabel} subsection 25(6) did not apply in a fiscal
          year that is included in the refresh period, and

      - [(B)]{.lawlabel} any portion, of a GloBE loss deferred tax asset
        that arises under paragraph 26(b) in a fiscal year that is
        included in the refresh period, that does not reverse during the
        refresh period, and

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} all references in subsection 29(4) to "a fiscal
      year that precedes the particular fiscal year" were read as
      references to "a fiscal year that is, or is subsequent to, the
      GloBE transition year and precedes the particular fiscal year";
      and

  - [(e)]{.lawlabel} any election made or revoked under section 44 or an
    equivalent provision of the laws of another jurisdiction were not
    taken into account.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Application --- joint ventures

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} For the purposes of this section and section 51, any
  reference to a constituent entity of an MNE group includes an entity
  that is a joint venture entity in respect of the MNE group.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Definitions --- initial phase of
international activity

- **[[53]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-53 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} The
  following definitions apply in this section.

  [[initial phase of international activity year]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[initial phase of international activity
      year]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}, of an MNE group, means a fiscal year

      - [(a)]{.lawlabel} throughout which there are constituent entities
        of the MNE group that are located in no more than six different
        jurisdictions; and

      - [(b)]{.lawlabel} for which the total of all amounts, each of
        which is the net book value, for the fiscal year, of a tangible
        asset held by a constituent entity of the MNE group that is
        located in a jurisdiction other than the reference jurisdiction,
        does not exceed €50 million. ([année de la phase de démarrage
        des activités internationales]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[net book value]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[net book value]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}, for a fiscal year, of a
      tangible asset, means the average of the beginning and end values
      of the tangible asset for the fiscal year after taking into
      account accumulated depreciation, depletion and impairment, as
      recorded in the financial statements. ([valeur nette
      comptable]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[reference jurisdiction]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[reference jurisdiction]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}, of an MNE Group,
      means the jurisdiction

      - [(a)]{.lawlabel} in which a constituent entity of the MNE group
        is located in the first fiscal year that any constituent entity
        of, or joint venture entity in respect of, the MNE group is
        subject to a qualified UTPR or this Part; and

      - [(b)]{.lawlabel} for which the sum of the net book values, for
        that fiscal year, of tangible assets held by the one or more
        constituent entities that are located in the jurisdiction is
        greater than the sum of the net book values, for that fiscal
        year, of tangible assets held by constituent entities of the MNE
        group that are located in any other single
        jurisdiction. ([juridiction de référence]{.DefinedTermLink
        lang="fr"})

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Tangible assets --- permanent
  establishments

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} For the purposes of the definitions [[initial phase
  of international activity year]{.dfn}]{.DefinitionRef} and [[reference
  jurisdiction]{.dfn}]{.DefinitionRef} in subsection (1),

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the tangible assets held by a constituent entity
    that is a permanent establishment are only those that are reflected
    in the separate financial accounts of the permanent establishment as
    adjusted in accordance with clause 17(1)(b)(ii)(B) or subsection
    17(2) (referred to in this subsection as "permanent establishment
    assets"); and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the tangible assets held by a constituent entity
    that is a main entity in respect of one or more permanent
    establishments are not to include any permanent establishment assets
    of those permanent establishments.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Initial phase of international activity
  --- exclusion

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} The domestic top-up amount of a constituent entity of
  an MNE group that is located in Canada for a fiscal year is deemed to
  be nil if

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} there is no relevant parent entity that is located
    in a jurisdiction (other than Canada) where it is subject to tax
    under a qualified IIR and that holds an ownership interest in any
    constituent entity of the MNE group that is located in Canada;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the fiscal year

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} begins on or before the day that is five years
      after the first day of the first fiscal year that a constituent
      entity of, or joint venture entity in respect of, the MNE group is
      subject to a qualified UTPR, and

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} is an initial phase of international activity
      year; and

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} all fiscal years preceding the fiscal year are
    initial phase of international activity years.

## [PART 4]{.HLabel1}[Anti-Avoidance]{.HTitleText1} {#h-1476532 .Part}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}General anti-avoidance rule

- **[[54]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-54
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} Section 245 of the [Income Tax
  Act](/eng/acts/I-3.3) applies for the purposes of this Act with any
  modifications that the circumstances require.

- [(2)]{.lawlabel} For purposes of subsection (1), the following rules
  also apply:

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} a constituent entity of an MNE group located in
    Canada may file in prescribed manner with the Minister, on behalf of
    the MNE group, an information return in prescribed form and
    containing prescribed information in respect of a transaction
    (which, for the purposes of this section, includes an arrangement or
    event) or series of transactions (within the meaning of subsection
    248(10) of the [Income Tax Act](/eng/acts/I-3.3)) as if subsection
    237.3(12.1) of the [Income Tax Act](/eng/acts/I-3.3) applied for the
    purposes of this Act; and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} if a constituent entity files an information return
    in accordance with paragraph (a), any penalty otherwise applicable
    because of subsection 245(5.1) of the [Income Tax
    Act](/eng/acts/I-3.3) does not apply in respect of the transaction
    or series.

::: HistoricalNote
- 2024, c. 17, s. 81 "54"
- [2024, c. 17, s. 111](#1467151-1477978){.wb-lbx
  aria-controls="centred-popup" ajax-after="1467151-1477978.html"
  role="button"}
:::

## [PART 5]{.HLabel1}[General Provisions, Administration and Enforcement]{.HTitleText1} {#h-1476535 .Part}

### [Definitions]{.HTitleText2} {#h-1476536 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Definitions

- **[[55]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-55 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} The
  following definitions apply in this Part.

  [[Agency]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[Agency]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means the Canada Revenue Agency
      continued by subsection 4(1) of the [Canada Revenue Agency
      Act](/eng/acts/C-10.11). ([Agence]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[assessment]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[assessment]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means an assessment or a
      reassessment under this Act. ([cotisation]{.DefinedTermLink
      lang="fr"})

  [[bank]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[bank]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a
      [[bank]{.dfn}]{.DefinitionRef} or an [[authorized foreign
      bank]{.dfn}]{.DefinitionRef}, as those terms are defined in
      section 2 of the [Bank Act](/eng/acts/B-1.01), that is not subject
      to the restrictions and requirements referred to in subsection
      524(2) of that Act. ([banque]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[bankrupt]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[bankrupt]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} has the same meaning as in
      section 2 of the [Bankruptcy and Insolvency
      Act](/eng/acts/B-3). ([failli]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[business number]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[business number]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means any number (other
      than a Social Insurance Number) used by the Minister to identify a
      person for the purposes of this Act. ([numéro
      d'entreprise]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[Canadian filing entity]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[Canadian filing entity]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means an entity
      appointed under subsection 61(3). ([entité déclarante
      Canadienne]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[Commissioner]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[Commissioner]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means, except in sections 58,
      120 and 137, the Commissioner of Revenue appointed under section
      25 of the [Canada Revenue Agency
      Act](/eng/acts/C-10.11). ([commissaire]{.DefinedTermLink
      lang="fr"})

  [[designated filing entity]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[designated filing entity]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}, in respect of an
      MNE group for a fiscal year, means a constituent entity of the MNE
      group that has been appointed by the MNE group in substitution for
      the ultimate parent entity to file a GIR on behalf of the MNE
      group for the fiscal year, if the entity

      - [(a)]{.lawlabel} has been given everything it may reasonably
        require to comply with the filing obligations under this Part;
        and

      - [(b)]{.lawlabel} is located in a jurisdiction in the fiscal year
        that has a qualified IIR or a qualified UTPR in effect for the
        fiscal year. ([entité déclarante désignée]{.DefinedTermLink
        lang="fr"})

  [[designated local entity]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[designated local entity]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means an entity
      appointed under paragraph 60(3)(a). ([entité locale
      désignée]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[designated notification entity]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[designated notification entity]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means an
      entity appointed under paragraph 60(5)(a). ([entité de
      notification déterminée]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[GIR due date]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[GIR due date]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}, in respect of a qualifying
      MNE group for a fiscal year, means the later of

      - [(a)]{.lawlabel} June 30, 2026; and

      - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the day that is

        - [(i)]{.lawlabel} 18 months after the last day of the fiscal
          year, if the fiscal year is the earlier of

          - [(A)]{.lawlabel} the first fiscal year for which a
            constituent entity of, or joint venture entity in respect
            of, the MNE group that is located in Canada is subject to
            Part 3, and

          - [(B)]{.lawlabel} the first fiscal year that a constituent
            entity of, or joint venture entity in respect of, the MNE
            group is subject to a qualified IIR or qualified UTPR; and

        - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} 15 months after the last day of the fiscal
          year, in any other case. ([date d'échéance
          DRG]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[GIR exchange date]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[GIR exchange date]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}, in respect of a
      qualifying MNE group for a fiscal year, means the date that
      exchanges of GIR information are due to occur under the applicable
      (or relevant) qualifying competent authority agreement. ([date
      d'échange DRG]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[judge]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[judge]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}, in respect of any matter, means a
      judge of a superior court having jurisdiction in the province in
      which the matter arises or a judge of the Federal
      Court. ([juge]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[official]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[official]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a person who is employed in
      the service of, who occupies a position of responsibility in the
      service of, or who is engaged by or on behalf of, His Majesty in
      right of Canada or a province, or a person who was formerly so
      employed, who formerly occupied such a position or who formerly
      was so engaged. ([fonctionnaire]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[person]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[person]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} includes an
      entity. ([personne]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[qualifying competent authority agreement]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[qualifying competent authority
      agreement]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means an agreement that

      - [(a)]{.lawlabel} is between authorized representatives of the
        jurisdictions that are parties to a listed international
        agreement (as defined in subsection 248(1) of the [Income Tax
        Act](/eng/acts/I-3.3)); and

      - [(b)]{.lawlabel} requires the automatic exchange of GIR
        information between the jurisdictions of the parties. ([accord
        admissible entre autorités compétentes]{.DefinedTermLink
        lang="fr"})

  [[qualifying foreign filing entity]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[qualifying foreign filing entity]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}, in
      respect of a qualifying MNE group for a fiscal year, means a
      constituent entity of the MNE group, if

      - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the constituent entity is the ultimate parent
        entity or designated filing entity of the MNE group for the
        fiscal year;

      - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the constituent entity is located in a
        jurisdiction (referred to in this definition as the "filing
        jurisdiction") other than Canada in the fiscal year;

      - [(c)]{.lawlabel} the constituent entity is obligated to file a
        GIR, in respect of the MNE group for the fiscal year, with the
        tax authority of the filing jurisdiction; and

      - [(d)]{.lawlabel} the filing jurisdiction has a qualifying
        competent authority agreement that is in effect, and to which
        Canada is a party, on or before the GIR exchange date for the
        fiscal year. ([entité déclarante étrangère
        admissible]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[record]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[record]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means any material on which
      representations, in any form, of information or concepts are
      recorded or marked and that is capable of being read or understood
      by an individual or a computer system or other
      device. ([registre]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Person resident in Canada

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} For the purposes of this Part, a person is deemed to
  be resident in Canada at any time

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} in the case of a corporation, if the corporation is

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} incorporated in Canada and not continued
      elsewhere, or

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} continued in Canada;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} in the case of a partnership, unincorporated
    society, club, association or organization, or a branch thereof, if
    the member or participant, or a majority of the members or
    participants, having management and control of the person is or are
    resident in Canada at that time;

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} in the case of a labour union, if it is carrying on
    activities as such in Canada and has a local union or branch in
    Canada at that time; or

  - [(d)]{.lawlabel} in the case of an individual, if the individual is
    deemed under any of paragraphs 250(1)(a) to (f) of the [Income Tax
    Act](/eng/acts/I-3.3) to be resident in Canada at that time.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Arm's length

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} For the purposes of this Part,

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} related persons are deemed not to deal with each
    other at arm's length; and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} it is a question of fact whether persons not
    related to each other are, at any time, dealing with each other at
    arm's length.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Related persons

  [(4)]{.lawlabel} For the purposes of this Part, persons are related to
  each other if they are related persons within the meaning of
  subsection 6(2) of the [Excise Act, 2001](/eng/acts/E-14.1).

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Administration or enforcement

  [(5)]{.lawlabel} For greater certainty, a reference in this Part to
  the administration or enforcement of this Act includes the collection
  of any amount payable under this Act.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Partnerships

  [(6)]{.lawlabel} For the purposes of this Part, anything done by a
  person as a member of a partnership is deemed to have been done by the
  partnership in the course of the partnership's activities and not to
  have been done by the person.

### [DIVISION 1]{.HLabel2}[Duties of Minister]{.HTitleText2} {#h-1476590 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Minister's duty

**[[56]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-56 .sectionLabel}** The Minister must
administer and enforce this Act and the Commissioner may exercise the
powers and perform the duties of the Minister under this Act.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Staff

- **[[57]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-57 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} The
  persons that are necessary to administer and enforce this Act are to
  be appointed, employed or engaged in the manner authorized by law.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Delegation of powers

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} The Minister may authorize any person who is employed
  or engaged by the Agency, or occupies a position of responsibility in
  the Agency, to exercise powers or perform duties of the Minister under
  this Act, including any judicial or quasi-judicial power or duty.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Administration of oaths

**[[58]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-58 .sectionLabel}** Any person, if so
designated by the Minister, may administer oaths and take and receive
affidavits, declarations and affirmations for the purposes of, or
incidental to, the administration or enforcement of this Act, and every
person so designated has for those purposes all the powers of a
commissioner for administering oaths or taking affidavits.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Waiving filing of documents

**[[59]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-59 .sectionLabel}** If any provision of this
Act or a regulation requires a person to file a form or other document
(other than a return or a form, or other document, with respect to an
election) or to provide information, prescribed by the Minister, the
Minister may waive the requirement, but at the Minister's request the
person must file the document or provide the information by the date set
out in the request.

### [DIVISION 2]{.HLabel2}[Returns]{.HTitleText2} {#h-1476602 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}GIR filing obligation

- **[[60]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-60 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} A GIR,
  in respect of a qualifying MNE group for a fiscal year, must be filed
  in the prescribed form and manner with the Minister on or before the
  GIR due date for the fiscal year by

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the ultimate parent entity of the MNE group, if

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} the ultimate parent entity is located in Canada
      in the fiscal year, and

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} the MNE group does not have a designated filing
      entity for the fiscal year that is located in Canada;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the designated filing entity in respect of the MNE
    group for the fiscal year, if it is located in Canada in the fiscal
    year; or

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} subject to subparagraph (3)(b)(ii), each
    constituent entity of the MNE group that is located in Canada in the
    fiscal year, if

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} neither paragraph (a) nor (b) applies,

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} the MNE group does not have a qualifying foreign
      filing entity for the fiscal year, and

    - [(iii)]{.lawlabel} neither the ultimate parent entity nor the
      designated filing entity in respect of the MNE group for the
      fiscal year that is located in a jurisdiction other than Canada
      files a complete or substantially complete GIR for the fiscal
      year, in the prescribed form and manner with the Minister, on or
      before the GIR due date.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}GIR filing obligation --- failure to
  exchange

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} A GIR, in respect of a qualifying MNE group for a
  fiscal year, must be filed in prescribed manner with the Minister by
  each constituent entity of the MNE group that is located in Canada in
  the fiscal year, or the designated notification entity, within 30 days
  of the notification described in paragraph (b), if

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the competent authority of the jurisdiction where
    the qualifying foreign filing entity is located has not provided a
    complete or substantially complete GIR for the fiscal year to the
    Minister by the GIR exchange date for the fiscal year; and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the Minister notifies the constituent entities of
    the MNE group that are located in Canada, or the designated
    notification entity, that the Minister has not received the GIR and
    requires it to be filed by one or more of those entities.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}GIR --- appointment of designated local
  entity

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} If more than one constituent entity of a qualifying
  MNE group is required to file a GIR in respect of the MNE group with
  the Minister for a fiscal year under paragraph (1)(c),

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} one of those constituent entities that are located
    in Canada may be appointed as a designated local entity, in the GIR
    filed with the Minister on or before the GIR due date for the fiscal
    year, to file the GIR, in respect of the MNE group for the year,
    with the Minister on behalf of all of those constituent entities;
    and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} where a designated local entity is appointed to
    file the GIR for the fiscal year

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} the designated local entity must file the GIR, in
      respect of the MNE group for the year, with the Minister on or
      before the GIR due date for the fiscal year, and

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} if the designated local entity does so, the GIR
      is deemed to have been filed at that time by each of the other
      constituent entities of the MNE group that are located in Canada.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}GIR notification requirement

  [(4)]{.lawlabel} If there is a qualifying foreign filing entity in
  respect of a qualifying MNE group for a fiscal year that is filing the
  GIR in respect of the MNE group for the fiscal year with the tax
  authority of the jurisdiction where it is located, subject to
  subparagraph (5)(b)(ii), each constituent entity of the MNE group that
  is located in Canada must notify the Minister of the following in the
  prescribed form and manner on or before the GIR due date for the
  fiscal year:

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the identity of the qualifying foreign filing
    entity; and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the jurisdiction in which the qualifying foreign
    filing entity is located.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}GIR --- appointment of designated
  notification entity

  [(5)]{.lawlabel} If more than one constituent entity of a qualifying
  MNE group is required to notify the Minister for a fiscal year under
  subsection (4),

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} one of those constituent entities that are located
    in Canada may be appointed as a designated notification entity, in
    the notification provided to the Minister on or before the GIR due
    date for the fiscal year, to notify the Minister, in respect of the
    MNE group for the year, on behalf of all of those constituent
    entities; and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} where a designated notification entity is appointed
    to provide the notification to the Minister for the fiscal year

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} the designated notification entity must provide
      the notification to the Minister, in respect of the MNE group for
      the year, on or before the GIR due date for the fiscal year, and

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} if the designated notification entity provides
      the notification to the Minister at a time that is on or before
      the GIR due date for the fiscal year, the notification is deemed
      to have been provided at that time by each of the other
      constituent entities of the MNE group that are located in Canada.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Part 2 return

- **[[61]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-61 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} A
  person that is liable to pay tax under Part 2 for a fiscal year must
  file in the prescribed form and manner with the Minister, on or before
  the GIR due date, a return for the fiscal year containing an estimate
  of the tax payable.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Part 3 return

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} A person that is liable to pay tax under Part 3 for a
  fiscal year must file in the prescribed form and manner with the
  Minister, on or before the GIR due date, a return for the fiscal year
  containing an estimate of the tax payable.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Canadian filing entity

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} If more than one person, in respect of an MNE group,
  is required to file a return under subsection (1) or (2) for a fiscal
  year, one of those persons that is resident in Canada may be appointed
  as the Canadian filing entity in the return, with the consent of the
  person, to file the return on behalf of all the persons that would, in
  the absence of subsection (4), be required to file the return for the
  fiscal year.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Consequences --- Canadian filing entity

  [(4)]{.lawlabel} If a Canadian filing entity is appointed, in respect
  of an MNE group, under subsection (3) to file a return with the
  Minister for a fiscal year,

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the Canadian filing entity must file the return ---
    on behalf of all persons that are required to file returns under
    subsection (1) or (2), as the case may be, in respect of the MNE
    group for the fiscal year --- on or before the GIR due date for the
    fiscal year; and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} if the Canadian filing entity does so, the return
    is deemed to have been filed at that time by each of those persons.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Consequences --- appointment

  [(5)]{.lawlabel} If a designated filing entity that is located in
  Canada, a designated local entity or a Canadian filing entity (each
  referred to in this subsection as an "appointed entity") is appointed
  to act on behalf of one or more persons in respect of an MNE group for
  a fiscal year,

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the appointed entity must act on behalf of those
    persons for the purposes of this Part in respect of the fiscal year;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} any action taken by the appointed entity on behalf
    of those persons for the purposes of this Part in respect of the
    fiscal year is deemed to have been performed by those persons; and

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} the Minister must direct to the appointed entity
    and those persons any communication for the purposes of this Part as
    it applies to those persons in respect of the fiscal year.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Demand for return

**[[62]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-62 .sectionLabel}** A person must, on demand
sent by the Minister, file, within any reasonable time that may be
specified in the demand, a return under this Act for any fiscal year
that is designated in the demand.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Trustees, etc.

**[[63]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-63 .sectionLabel}** Every trustee in
bankruptcy, assignee, liquidator, curator, receiver, trustee or
committee and every agent or other person administering, managing,
winding up, controlling or otherwise dealing with the property,
business, estate or income of a person that has not filed a return for a
fiscal year as required by this Division must file the return.

### [DIVISION 3]{.HLabel2}[Payments]{.HTitleText2} {#h-1476654 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Payments

**[[64]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-64 .sectionLabel}** The tax payable under
this Act by a person in respect of a fiscal year must be paid on or
before the GIR due date for the fiscal year.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Manner and form of payments

**[[65]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-65 .sectionLabel}** Every person that is
required under this Act to pay tax or any other amount must make the
payment to the account of the Receiver General for Canada in the manner
and form prescribed by the Minister.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Part 2 --- assessment of another
constituent entity

- **[[66]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-66 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} The
  Minister may assess a particular constituent entity of an MNE group
  that is located in Canada in respect of tax and other amounts payable
  under this Part or Part 2 by another constituent entity of the MNE
  group that is located in Canada. If such an assessment is made, the
  particular constituent entity is jointly and severally, or solidarily,
  liable with the other constituent entity to pay the amount assessed
  and this Part applies to the particular constituent entity in respect
  of the amount assessed, with any modifications that the circumstances
  require.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Limitation

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} Subsection (1) does not limit the liability of the
  other constituent entity under any other provision of this Act or the
  liability of the particular constituent entity for the interest that
  the particular constituent entity is liable to pay under this Act on
  an assessment in respect of the amount that the particular constituent
  entity is liable to pay because of that subsection.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Part 3 --- joint and several, or
  solidary, liability

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} A particular constituent entity that is located in
  Canada is, in respect of tax and other amounts payable under this Part
  or Part 3 by another constituent entity of the same MNE group or a
  joint venture entity in respect of the same MNE group, jointly and
  severally, or solidarily, liable with the other constituent entity or
  joint venture entity to pay those amounts and this Part applies to the
  particular constituent entity in respect of those amounts, with any
  modifications that the circumstances require.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Part 3 --- joint and several, or
  solidary, liability of joint venture entities

  [(4)]{.lawlabel} A particular joint venture entity that is located in
  Canada is, in respect of tax and other amounts payable under this Part
  or Part 3 by another joint venture entity of the same joint venture
  group, jointly and severally, or solidarily, liable with the other
  joint venture entity to pay those amounts and this Part applies to the
  particular joint venture entity in respect of those amounts, with any
  modifications that the circumstances require.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Partnerships --- joint and several, or
  solidary, liability

  [(5)]{.lawlabel} A partnership and each member or former member of the
  partnership (other than a member that is a limited partner and is not
  a general partner) are jointly and severally, or solidarily, liable
  for

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the payment of all amounts that are required to be
    paid by the partnership under this Act before or during the period
    during which the member is a member of the partnership and, if the
    member is a member of the partnership when the partnership is
    dissolved, after the dissolution of the partnership, except that

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} the member is liable for the payment of amounts
      that become payable before the period only to the extent of the
      property that is regarded as property of the partnership under the
      relevant laws of general application to partnerships in force in a
      province or other jurisdiction, and

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} the payment by the partnership or any member of
      the partnership of an amount in respect of the liability
      discharges their liability to the extent of that amount; and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} all other obligations under this Act that arose
    before or during that period for which the partnership is liable or,
    if the member was a member of the partnership at the time the
    partnership was dissolved, the obligations that arose upon or as a
    consequence of the dissolution.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Partnerships --- tiers

  [(6)]{.lawlabel} For the purposes of this section, a partnership or
  other person that is (or is deemed by this subsection to be) a member
  of a particular partnership that is a member of another partnership is
  deemed to be a member of the other partnership.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Trusts --- joint and several, or
  solidary, liability

  [(7)]{.lawlabel} A trust and all persons that are trustees of that
  trust at any time during a fiscal year are jointly and severally, or
  solidarily, liable for the payment of all amounts that are required to
  be paid by the trust under this Act for the fiscal year.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Persons liable in respect of other
  entities

  [(8)]{.lawlabel} All persons that are, in respect of a constituent
  entity, liable to pay an amount under this Act for a fiscal year are
  jointly and severally, or solidarily, liable with the constituent
  entity to pay all amounts that are required to be paid by any of those
  persons or the constituent entity under this Act for the fiscal year.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Discharge of liability

  [(9)]{.lawlabel} If a particular person and another person are jointly
  and severally, or solidarily, liable in respect of part or all of the
  liability of the other person under this Act, the following rules
  apply:

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} a payment by the particular person on account of
    the particular person's liability discharges, to the extent of the
    payment, the joint liability; and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} a payment by the other person on account of the
    other person's liability discharges the particular person's
    liability only to the extent that the payment operates to reduce the
    particular person's liability to an amount less than the amount in
    respect of which the particular person is jointly and severally, or
    solidarily, liable.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Definition of
[[transaction]{.dfn}]{.DefinitionRef}

- **[[67]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-67 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} In
  this section and section 102, a [[transaction]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}
  includes an arrangement or event.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Tax liability --- property transferred
  not at arm's length

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} If at any time a person transfers property, either
  directly or indirectly, by means of a trust or by any other means to
  another person with which the transferor was not, at that time,
  dealing at arm's length, the transferee and transferor are jointly and
  severally, or solidarily, liable to pay under this Act an amount equal
  to the lesser of

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the amount determined by the formula

    ::: {#1476690 .Paragraph}
    A − (B − C)

    where

    [A]{.dfn}
    :   is the amount, if any, by which the fair market value of the
        property at that time exceeds the fair market value at that time
        of the consideration given by the transferee for the transfer of
        the property,

    [B]{.dfn}
    :   is the total of all amounts, if any, the transferee was assessed
        under paragraph 97.44(1)(b) of the [Customs
        Act](/eng/acts/C-52.6), subsection 325(2) of the [Excise Tax
        Act](/eng/acts/E-15), subsection 160(2) of the [Income Tax
        Act](/eng/acts/I-3.3), subsection 297(3) of the [Excise Act,
        2001](/eng/acts/E-14.1), subsection 161(1) of the [Greenhouse
        Gas Pollution Pricing Act](/eng/acts/G-11.55), subsection 80(3)
        of the [Underused Housing Tax Act](/eng/acts/U-0.5), subsection
        150(4) of the [Select Luxury Items Tax Act](/eng/acts/S-8.35) or
        subsection 52(5) of the [Digital Services Tax
        Act](/eng/acts/D-1.65) in respect of the property, and

    [C]{.dfn}
    :   is the amount paid by the transferor in respect of the amount
        determined for B, and
    :::

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the total of all amounts each of which is

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} an amount that the transferor is liable to pay
      under this Act in respect of

      - [(A)]{.lawlabel} the fiscal year that includes that time, or

      - [(B)]{.lawlabel} any preceding fiscal year, or

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} interest or penalties (other than amounts
      referred to in subparagraph (i)) for which the transferor is
      liable at that time.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Limitation

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} Subsection (2) does not limit the liability of the
  transferor under any other provision of this Act or the liability of
  the transferee for the interest that the transferee is liable to pay
  under this Act on an assessment in respect of the amount that the
  transferee is liable to pay because of that subsection.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Fair market value of undivided interest
  or right

  [(4)]{.lawlabel} For the purposes of this section, the fair market
  value at any time of an undivided interest in, or for civil law an
  undivided right in, a property that is expressed as a proportionate
  interest or right in that property is deemed to be equal to the same
  proportion of the fair market value of that property at that time.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Assessment

  [(5)]{.lawlabel} Despite subsection 85(1), the Minister may at any
  time assess a transferee in respect of any amount payable because of
  this section, and this Part applies to the transferee with any
  modifications that the circumstances require.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Rules applicable

  [(6)]{.lawlabel} If a transferor and transferee become, because of
  subsection (2), jointly and severally, or solidarily, liable in
  respect of part or all of the liability of the transferor under this
  Act, the following rules apply:

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} a payment by the transferee on account of the
    transferee's liability discharges, to the extent of the payment, the
    joint liability; and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} a payment by the transferor on account of the
    transferor's liability discharges the transferee's liability only to
    the extent that the payment operates to reduce the transferor's
    liability to an amount less than the amount in respect of which the
    transferee is, because of subsection (2), jointly and severally, or
    solidarily, liable.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Anti-avoidance rules

  [(7)]{.lawlabel} For the purposes of subsections (1) to (6), if a
  person (referred to in this section as the "transferor") transfers
  property, either directly or indirectly, by means of a trust or by any
  other means whatever to another person (referred to in this section as
  the "transferee") in a transaction or as part of a series of
  transactions, the following rules apply:

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the transferor is deemed to not be dealing at arm's
    length with the transferee at all times in the transaction or series
    of transactions if

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} the transferor and the transferee do not deal at
      arm's length at any time during the period beginning immediately
      before the transaction or series of transactions and ending
      immediately after the transaction or series of transactions, and

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} it is reasonable to conclude that one of the
      purposes of undertaking or arranging the transaction or series of
      transactions is to avoid joint and several, or solidary, liability
      of the transferee and the transferor under this section for an
      amount payable under this Act;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} an amount that the transferor is liable to pay
    under this Act (including, for greater certainty, an amount that the
    transferor is liable to pay under this section, regardless of
    whether the Minister has made an assessment under subsection (5) in
    respect of that amount) is deemed to have become payable in the
    fiscal year in which the property is transferred, if it is
    reasonable to conclude that one of the purposes of the transfer of
    the property is to avoid the payment of a future amount payable
    under this Act by the transferor or transferee; and

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} the amount determined for A in paragraph (2)(a) is
    deemed to be the greater of

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} the amount otherwise determined for A in
      paragraph (2)(a) without reference to this paragraph, and

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} the amount determined by the formula

      ::: {#1476719 .Subparagraph}
      A − B

      where

      [A]{.dfn}
      :   is the fair market value of the property at the time of the
          transfer, and

      [B]{.dfn}
      :   is
          - [(A)]{.lawlabel} the lowest fair market value of the
            consideration (that is held by the transferor) given for the
            property at any time during the period beginning immediately
            before the transaction or series of transactions and ending
            immediately after the transaction or series of transactions,
            or

          - [(B)]{.lawlabel} if the consideration is in a form that is
            cancelled or extinguished during the period referred to in
            clause (A),

            - [(I)]{.lawlabel} the amount that is the lower of the
              amount determined under clause (A) and the fair market
              value during that period of any property, other than
              property that is cancelled or extinguished during the
              period, that is substituted for the consideration referred
              to in clause (A), or

            - [(II)]{.lawlabel} if no property is substituted for the
              consideration referred to in clause (A), other than
              property that is cancelled or extinguished during the
              period, nil.
      :::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Payment in Canadian dollars

- **[[68]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-68 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} Every
  person that is required under this Act to pay an amount to the
  Receiver General for Canada must pay the amount in Canadian dollars.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Exchange rate

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} If an amount payable by a person for a fiscal year
  under this Act would, in the absence of this subsection, be
  denominated in a currency other than the Canadian dollar, that amount
  is to be converted to Canadian dollars using the average for the
  fiscal year of the daily rates of exchange quoted by the Bank of
  Canada, or if there is no daily rate quoted by the Bank of Canada for
  a particular day, a daily rate of exchange that is acceptable to the
  Minister, in respect of the two currencies.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Exception

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} The Minister may, at any time, waive the requirement
  under subsection (1) and accept a currency other than Canadian
  dollars. If such a waiver is granted, the amount is to be converted
  from Canadian dollars to the other currency using a rate of exchange
  that is acceptable to the Minister.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Definition of [[electronic
payment]{.dfn}]{.DefinitionRef}

- **[[69]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-69 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} In
  this section, [[electronic payment]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means any
  payment to the Receiver General for Canada that is made through
  electronic services offered by a person described in any of paragraphs
  (2)(a) to (d) or by any electronic means specified by the Minister.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Electronic payment

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} Every person that is required under this Act to pay
  an amount to the Receiver General for Canada must, if the amount is
  \$10,000 or more, make the payment by way of electronic payment,
  unless the person cannot reasonably pay the amount in that manner, to
  the account of the Receiver General for Canada at or through

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} a bank;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} a credit union;

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} a corporation that is authorized under the laws of
    Canada or a province to carry on the business of offering its
    services as a trustee to the public; or

  - [(d)]{.lawlabel} a corporation that is authorized under the laws of
    Canada or a province to accept deposits from the public and that
    carries on the business of lending money on the security of real
    property or immovables or investing in indebtedness on the security
    of mortgages on real property or hypothecs on immovables.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Small amounts owing by a person

- **[[70]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-70 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} If, at
  any time, the total of all unpaid amounts owing by a person to the
  Receiver General for Canada under this Act does not exceed \$2.00, the
  amount owing by the person is deemed to be nil.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Small amounts payable to a person

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} If, at any time, the total of all amounts payable by
  the Minister to a person under this Act does not exceed \$2.00, the
  Minister may apply those amounts against any amount owing, at that
  time, by the person to His Majesty in right of Canada. However, if the
  person, at that time, does not owe any amount to His Majesty in right
  of Canada, those amounts payable are deemed to be nil.

### [DIVISION 4]{.HLabel2}[Interest]{.HTitleText2} {#h-1476748 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Compound interest

- **[[71]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-71 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} If a
  person fails to pay an amount to the Receiver General for Canada as
  and when required under this Act, the person must pay to the Receiver
  General for Canada interest on the amount. The interest must be
  compounded daily at the rate prescribed under section 4301 of the
  [Income Tax Regulations](/eng/regulations/C.R.C.,_c._945), with any
  modifications that the circumstances require, and determined for the
  period beginning on the first day after the day on or before which the
  amount was required to be paid and ending on the day on which the
  amount is paid.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Payment of compounded interest

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} For the purposes of subsection (1), interest that is
  compounded on a particular day on an unpaid amount of a person is
  deemed to be required to be paid by the person to the Receiver General
  for Canada at the end of the particular day, and, if the person has
  not paid the interest so determined by the end of the day after the
  particular day, the interest must be added to the unpaid amount at the
  end of the particular day.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Period when interest not payable

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} If the Minister has served a demand that a person pay
  on or before a specified day all amounts payable by the person under
  this Act on the date of the demand, and the person pays the amount
  demanded on or before the specified day, the Minister must waive any
  interest that would otherwise apply in respect of the amount demanded
  for the period beginning on the first day after the date of the demand
  and ending on the day of payment.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Interest and penalty amounts of \$25 or
  less

  [(4)]{.lawlabel} If, at any time, a person pays an amount that is not
  less than the total of all amounts, other than interest and penalties,
  owing at that time to His Majesty in right of Canada under this Act in
  respect of a fiscal year and the total amount of interest and
  penalties payable by the person under this Act in respect of the
  fiscal year is not more than \$25, the Minister may cancel the
  interest and penalties.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Waiving or cancelling interest

- **[[72]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-72 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} The
  Minister may, on or before the day that is 10 calendar years after the
  end of a particular fiscal year, or on application by a person on or
  before that day, waive, cancel or reduce any interest otherwise
  payable by the person under this Act on an amount that is required to
  be paid by the person in respect of the particular fiscal year, and
  may despite subsection 85(1), make any assessment of the interest
  payable by the person that is necessary to take into account the
  waiver, cancellation or reduction of the interest.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Interest on amounts waived or cancelled

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} If a person has paid an amount of interest and the
  Minister waives, cancels or reduces any portion of that amount under
  subsection (1), the Minister must refund the portion of the amount and
  pay interest on it at the rate prescribed under section 4301 of the
  [Income Tax Regulations](/eng/regulations/C.R.C.,_c._945), with any
  modifications that the circumstances require, beginning on the day
  that is 30 days after the day on which the Minister received an
  application in a manner satisfactory to the Minister to apply that
  subsection (or, if there is no such application, on the day on which
  the Minister waives, cancels or reduces the portion of the amount) and
  ending on the day on which the portion of the amount is paid as a
  refund or applied against another amount owed by the person to His
  Majesty in right of Canada.

### [DIVISION 5]{.HLabel2}[Administrative Charge Under the [Financial Administration Act](/eng/acts/F-11)]{.HTitleText2} {#h-1476763 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Dishonoured instruments

**[[73]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-73 .sectionLabel}** For the purposes of this
Act and section 155.1 of the [Financial Administration
Act](/eng/acts/F-11), any charge that is payable at any time by a person
under the [Financial Administration Act](/eng/acts/F-11) in respect of
an instrument tendered in payment or settlement of an amount that is
payable under this Act is deemed to be an amount that is payable by the
person at that time under this Act. In addition, Part II of the
[Interest and Administrative Charges
Regulations](/eng/regulations/SOR-96-188) does not apply to the charge
and any debt under subsection 155.1(3) of the [Financial Administration
Act](/eng/acts/F-11) in respect of the charge is deemed to be
extinguished at the time the total of the amount and any applicable
interest under this Act is paid.

### [DIVISION 6]{.HLabel2}[Refunds]{.HTitleText2} {#h-1476766 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Statutory recovery rights

**[[74]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-74 .sectionLabel}** Except as specifically
